/**
 * BAGANI — SafeArea-Compliant Reusable Banner Ad Slot (Phase 2)
 * Adheres strictly to Google Play Ads Policy & Apple App Store Guidelines:
 * - Clearly labeled with an "AD" badge
 * - Separated from interactive navigation elements to prevent accidental clicks
 * - Supports Top, Bottom, or Both layout placements
 */

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import {
  AdConfiguration,
  AdService,
  ROTATING_BANNER_ADS,
} from '../../services/ad_service';
import { AppLanguage } from '../l10n/translations';
import { AppThemeColors } from '../theme/app_theme';

interface BannerAdSlotProps {
  position: 'top' | 'bottom';
  adConfig: AdConfiguration;
  theme: AppThemeColors;
  lang: AppLanguage;
  onTogglePosition?: () => void;
}

export const BannerAdSlot: React.FC<BannerAdSlotProps> = ({
  position,
  adConfig,
  theme,
  lang,
  onTogglePosition,
}) => {
  const [adIndex, setAdIndex] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setAdIndex((prev) => (prev + 1) % ROTATING_BANNER_ADS.length);
    }, 8000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);

  if (!adConfig.adsEnabled) return null;

  const shouldRender =
    adConfig.placementPosition === 'both' ||
    adConfig.placementPosition === position;

  if (!shouldRender) return null;

  const currentAd = ROTATING_BANNER_ADS[adIndex];
  const unitId = AdService.getActiveUnitId(adConfig);

  return (
    <View
      style={[
        styles.outerContainer,
        {
          backgroundColor: theme.adBannerBg,
          borderColor: theme.border,
          borderTopWidth: position === 'bottom' ? 1 : 0,
          borderBottomWidth: position === 'top' ? 1 : 0,
        },
      ]}
    >
      <View style={styles.container}>
        <View style={styles.leftRow}>
          <View style={[styles.adBadge, { backgroundColor: theme.primary }]}>
            <Text style={styles.adBadgeText}>{currentAd.badgeText}</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.textColumn}>
            <Text
              numberOfLines={1}
              style={[styles.headline, { color: theme.textPrimary }]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn' ? currentAd.headlineBn : currentAd.headlineEn}
            </Text>
            <Text
              numberOfLines={1}
              style={[styles.subtext, { color: theme.textMuted }]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn' ? currentAd.subtextBn : currentAd.subtextEn} •{' '}
              {unitId.slice(-10)}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        {onTogglePosition && (
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={onTogglePosition}
            style={[
              styles.posSwitchBtn,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[styles.posSwitchText, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}
            >
              {adConfig.placementPosition.toUpperCase()}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  outerContainer: {
    width: '100%',
    alignItems: 'center',
  },
  container: {
    width: '100%',
    maxWidth: 1000,
    minHeight: 54,
    paddingHorizontal: 20,
    paddingVertical: 8,
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
  },
  leftRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    flex: 1,
    gap: 10,
  },
  adBadge: {
    paddingHorizontal: 6,
    paddingVertical: 3,
    borderRadius: 4,
  },
  adBadgeText: {
    color: '#FFFFFF',
    fontSize: 10,
    fontWeight: '800',
    letterSpacing: 0.5,
  },
  textColumn: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  headline: {
    fontSize: 12,
    fontWeight: '700',
  },
  subtext: {
    fontSize: 11,
    marginTop: 1,
  },
  posSwitchBtn: {
    paddingHorizontal: 8,
    paddingVertical: 5,
    borderRadius: 6,
    borderWidth: 1,
    marginLeft: 8,
  },
  posSwitchText: {
    fontSize: 10,
    fontWeight: '700',
  },
});



// ##############################################################################
// FILE END: lib/core/components/banner_ad_slot.tsx
// ##############################################################################


// ##############################################################################
// FILE START: lib/core/components/botanical_search_modal.tsx
// ##############################################################################

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Modal,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { AppLanguage } from '../l10n/translations';
import {
  APP_TYPOGRAPHY,
  AppThemeColors,
  DESIGN_TOKENS,
} from '../theme/app_theme';
import {
  BotanicalEncyclopediaEntry,
  OnlineBotanicalService,
} from '../../services/online_botanical_service';
import { PlantModel } from '../../models/plant_model';

interface BotanicalSearchModalProps {
  visible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  theme: AppThemeColors;
  lang: AppLanguage;
  onSelectPlantToAdd?: (preset: {
    nameBn: string;
    nameEn: string;
    variety: string;
    containerSize: number;
    category: 'fruit' | 'vegetable' | 'herb' | 'flower' | 'indoor';
    isAllSeason: boolean;
  }) => void;
}

export const BotanicalSearchModal: React.FC<BotanicalSearchModalProps> = ({
  visible,
  onClose,
  theme,
  lang,
  onSelectPlantToAdd,
}) => {
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [searchResults, setSearchResults] = useState<BotanicalEncyclopediaEntry[]>([]);
  const [selectedEntry, setSelectedEntry] = useState<BotanicalEncyclopediaEntry | null>(null);
  const [selectedVariety, setSelectedVariety] = useState<string>('');
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);

  const fontFam =
    lang === 'bn'
      ? APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyBengali
      : APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyEnglish;

  // Load initial popular rooftop botanical species
  useEffect(() => {
    if (visible) {
      handleSearch(searchQuery);
    }
  }, [visible]);

  const handleSearch = async (text: string) => {
    setSearchQuery(text);
    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const results = await OnlineBotanicalService.searchEncyclopediaOnline(text);
      setSearchResults(results);
      if (results.length > 0 && !selectedEntry) {
        setSelectedEntry(results[0]);
        setSelectedVariety(
          lang === 'bn' ? results[0].varietiesBn[0] || '' : results[0].varietiesEn[0] || ''
        );
      }
    } catch {
      // Fallback to local
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSelectEntry = (entry: BotanicalEncyclopediaEntry) => {
    setSelectedEntry(entry);
    setSelectedVariety(
      lang === 'bn' ? entry.varietiesBn[0] || '' : entry.varietiesEn[0] || ''
    );
  };

  return (
    <Modal visible={visible} animationType="slide" transparent onRequestClose={onClose}>
      <View style={styles.modalOverlay}>
        <View
          style={[
            styles.modalContainer,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          {/* Modal Header */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.headerRow,
              { borderBottomColor: theme.border, backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated },
            ]}
          >
            <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 200 }}>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.titleText,
                  { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                🌿{' '}
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'অনলাইন উদ্ভিদ ও জাত অনুসন্ধান (Google-Style Botanica)'
                  : 'Botanical Plant & Variety Search Engine'}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.subText,
                  { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'যেকোনো ফল বা সবজির নাম লিখুন—অনলাইন ভেরিফাইড জাত, মাটির মিশ্রণ ও পূর্ণাঙ্গ পরিচর্যা গাইড পেয়ে যান।'
                  : 'Search any plant to explore verified varieties, soil requirements, fertilizer & pruning guides.'}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={onClose}
              style={[
                styles.closeBtn,
                { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={{ color: theme.textPrimary, fontWeight: '700', fontSize: 16 }}>
                ✕
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          {/* Search Input Bar */}
          <View style={styles.searchBarWrap}>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.searchInputBox,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 16 }}>🔍</Text>
              <TextInput
                value={searchQuery}
                onChangeText={handleSearch}
                placeholder={
                  lang === 'bn'
                    ? 'গাছের নাম লিখুন (যেমন: সবেদা, কাটিমন আম, মাল্টা, পেয়ারা, লাউ)...'
                    : 'Search plant (e.g. Sapodilla, Mango, Guava, Malta, Tomato)...'
                }
                placeholderTextColor={theme.textSecondary}
                style={[
                  styles.searchInput,
                  { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              />
              {searchQuery ? (
                <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => handleSearch('')}>
                  <Text style={{ color: theme.textSecondary, fontSize: 14 }}>✕</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              ) : null}
            </View>

            {/* Quick Keyword Chips */}
            <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
              {['সবেদা (Sapodilla)', 'আম (Mango)', 'মাল্টা (Malta)', 'পেয়ারা (Guava)', 'ড্রাগন (Dragon)', 'লাউ ও শসা', 'মরিচ'].map(
                (chip, idx) => (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={idx}
                    onPress={() => handleSearch(chip.split(' ')[0])}
                    style={[
                      styles.chipBtn,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                        borderColor: theme.border,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={{
                        color: theme.primary,
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: '600',
                        fontFamily: fontFam,
                      }}
                    >
                      {chip}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                )
              )}
            </ScrollView>
          </View>

          {/* Results + Details Split/Scroll View */}
          <ScrollView
            style={{ flex: 1 }}
            contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollBody}
            showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
          >
            {/* Matching Plant Species Chips */}
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.sectionLabel,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              🌱{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? `অনলাইন ফলাফল (${searchResults.length}টি প্রজাতি পাওয়া গেছে):`
                : `Verified Results (${searchResults.length} Species):`}
            </Text>

            <View style={styles.speciesRow}>
              {searchResults.map((entry) => {
                const isSelected = selectedEntry?.id === entry.id;
                return (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={entry.id}
                    onPress={() => handleSelectEntry(entry)}
                    style={[
                      styles.speciesPill,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: isSelected
                          ? theme.primary
                          : theme.surfaceElevated,
                        borderColor: isSelected ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={{
                        color: isSelected ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                        fontWeight: '700',
                        fontSize: 13,
                        fontFamily: fontFam,
                      }}
                    >
                      {lang === 'bn' ? entry.canonicalNameBn : entry.canonicalNameEn}
                    </Text>
                    <Text
                      style={{
                        color: isSelected ? '#E7F4EB' : theme.textSecondary,
                        fontSize: 11,
                        fontStyle: 'italic',
                      }}
                    >
                      {entry.scientificName}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                );
              })}
            </View>

            {/* Selected Entry Detailed Structured Guide */}
            {selectedEntry ? (
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.detailsCard,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.surface,
                    borderColor: theme.border,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                {/* Header */}
                <View style={styles.detailsHeader}>
                  <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.speciesName,
                        { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {lang === 'bn'
                        ? selectedEntry.canonicalNameBn
                        : selectedEntry.canonicalNameEn}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={{ color: theme.textSecondary, fontStyle: 'italic', fontSize: 13 }}>
                      Scientific: {selectedEntry.scientificName} •{' '}
                      {selectedEntry.isAllSeasonDefault
                        ? lang === 'bn'
                          ? 'বারোমাসি ফলন'
                          : 'All-Season Crop'
                        : lang === 'bn'
                        ? 'মৌসুমি ফলন'
                        : 'Seasonal Crop'}
                    </Text>
                  </View>

                  {onSelectPlantToAdd && (
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      onPress={() => {
                        onSelectPlantToAdd({
                          nameBn: selectedEntry.canonicalNameBn,
                          nameEn: selectedEntry.canonicalNameEn,
                          variety: selectedVariety || selectedEntry.varietiesBn[0] || 'উন্নত জাত',
                          containerSize: selectedEntry.recommendedContainerInches || 20,
                          category: selectedEntry.category,
                          isAllSeason: selectedEntry.isAllSeasonDefault,
                        });
                        onClose();
                      }}
                      style={[
                        styles.addBtn,
                        { backgroundColor: theme.primary },
                      ]}
                    >
                      <Text style={{ color: '#FFFFFF', fontWeight: '700', fontSize: 13, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                        + {lang === 'bn' ? 'বাগানে যোগ করুন' : 'Add to Garden'}
                      </Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  )}
                </View>

                {/* Varieties Selector */}
                <View style={styles.guideBlock}>
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.guideBlockTitle,
                      { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                    ]}
                  >
                    🏷️{' '}
                    {lang === 'bn'
                      ? 'অনলাইন অনুমোদিত উন্নত জাতসমূহ (Select Variety):'
                      : 'Available Certified Varieties:'}
                  </Text>
                  <View style={styles.varietyChipsWrap}>
                    {(lang === 'bn'
                      ? selectedEntry.varietiesBn
                      : selectedEntry.varietiesEn
                    ).map((vName, vIdx) => {
                      const isVarSelected = selectedVariety === vName;
                      return (
                        <TouchableOpacity
                          key={vIdx}
                          onPress={() => setSelectedVariety(vName)}
                          style={[
                            styles.varietyChip,
                            {
                              backgroundColor: isVarSelected
                                ? theme.selectedSurface
                                : theme.surfaceElevated,
                              borderColor: isVarSelected
                                ? theme.primary
                                : theme.border,
                            },
                          ]}
                        >
                          <Text
                            style={{
                              color: isVarSelected
                                ? theme.primary
                                : theme.textPrimary,
                              fontWeight: isVarSelected ? '700' : '500',
                              fontSize: 12.5,
                              fontFamily: fontFam,
                            }}
                          >
                            {isVarSelected ? '✓ ' : ''}
                            {vName}
                          </Text>
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                      );
                    })}
                  </View>
                </View>

                {/* 1. Recommended Container & Soil Mix */}
                <View style={styles.guideBlock}>
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.guideBlockTitle,
                      { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                    ]}
                  >
                    🪴{' '}
                    {lang === 'bn'
                      ? `টব নির্বাচন ও মাটির মিশ্রণ প্রস্তুত প্রণালী (${selectedEntry.recommendedContainerInches}" জিও ব্যাগ):`
                      : `Recommended Container & Soil Formula (${selectedEntry.recommendedContainerInches}" Geo-Bag):`}
                  </Text>
                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.guideBox,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                        borderColor: theme.border,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.guideText,
                        { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {lang === 'bn'
                        ? selectedEntry.soilSelectionAndMixBn
                        : selectedEntry.soilSelectionAndMixEn}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>

                {/* 2. Planting Method */}
                <View style={styles.guideBlock}>
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.guideBlockTitle,
                      { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                    ]}
                  >
                    🌱{' '}
                    {lang === 'bn'
                      ? 'চারা রোপণ ও প্রাথমিক ড্রেনেজ পদ্ধতি:'
                      : 'Sapling Planting & Drainage Method:'}
                  </Text>
                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.guideBox,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                        borderColor: theme.border,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.guideText,
                        { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {lang === 'bn'
                        ? selectedEntry.plantingMethodBn
                        : selectedEntry.plantingMethodEn}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>

                {/* 3. Ongoing Nutrition & Fertilizer */}
                <View style={styles.guideBlock}>
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.guideBlockTitle,
                      { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                    ]}
                  >
                    🧪{' '}
                    {lang === 'bn'
                      ? 'সার ও নিয়মিত পুষ্টি ব্যবস্থাপনা:'
                      : 'Ongoing Nutrition & Fertilizer Protocol:'}
                  </Text>
                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.guideBox,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                        borderColor: theme.border,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.guideText,
                        { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {lang === 'bn'
                        ? selectedEntry.ongoingCareAndFertilizerBn
                        : selectedEntry.ongoingCareAndFertilizerEn}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>

                {/* 4. Precision Pruning Technique (Requirement #6) */}
                <View style={styles.guideBlock}>
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.guideBlockTitle,
                      { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                    ]}
                  >
                    ✂️{' '}
                    {lang === 'bn'
                      ? 'প্রিসিশন প্রুনিং ও দ্বিগুণ ফলন কৌশল (2x Yield Boost):'
                      : 'Precision Pruning & Yield Multiplier Technique:'}
                  </Text>
                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.guideBox,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                        borderColor: theme.primary,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.guideText,
                        { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {lang === 'bn'
                        ? selectedEntry.pruningTechniqueBn
                        : selectedEntry.pruningTechniqueEn}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>

                {/* 5. Diseases & Organic Remedies (Requirement #5) */}
                <View style={styles.guideBlock}>
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.guideBlockTitle,
                      { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                    ]}
                  >
                    🩺{' '}
                    {lang === 'bn'
                      ? 'সম্ভাব্য রোগবালাই ও জৈব প্রতিকার:'
                      : 'Common Pests, Diseases & Organic Remedies:'}
                  </Text>
                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.guideBox,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                        borderColor: theme.border,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.guideText,
                        { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {lang === 'bn'
                        ? selectedEntry.diseaseAndRemediesBn
                        : selectedEntry.diseaseAndRemediesEn}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>

                {/* 6. Harvest & Post-Harvest Maintenance */}
                <View style={styles.guideBlock}>
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.guideBlockTitle,
                      { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                    ]}
                  >
                    🧺{' '}
                    {lang === 'bn'
                      ? 'ফল সংগ্রহ ও পরবর্তী গাছ সংরক্ষণ:'
                      : 'Harvesting & Post-Harvest Maintenance:'}
                  </Text>
                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.guideBox,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                        borderColor: theme.border,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.guideText,
                        { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {lang === 'bn'
                        ? selectedEntry.harvestAndPostHarvestBn
                        : selectedEntry.harvestAndPostHarvestEn}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
              </View>
            ) : null}
          </ScrollView>
        </View>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({

/**
 * BAGANI — Professional App-Style 5-Tab Bottom Navigation Bar
 * Tabs: Home, My Garden, Scan, Guide, Profile
 * When a tab is selected, the entire menu item box (both icon and label together) is highlighted as a cohesive box.
 */

import React from 'react';
import { StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import Svg, { Path, Rect } from 'react-native-svg';
import { AppScreenRoute } from '../../screens';
import { AppLanguage } from '../l10n/translations';
import {
  APP_TYPOGRAPHY,
  AppThemeColors,
} from '../theme/app_theme';

interface BottomNavBarProps {
  activeRoute: AppScreenRoute;
  onSelectRoute: (route: AppScreenRoute) => void;
  theme: AppThemeColors;
  lang: AppLanguage;
}

interface NavItemConfig {
  route: AppScreenRoute;
  icon: string;
  labelBn: string;
  labelEn: string;
}

const NAV_ITEMS: NavItemConfig[] = [
  { route: 'home', icon: '🏡', labelBn: 'হোম', labelEn: 'Home' },
  {
    route: 'my_garden',
    icon: '🌿',
    labelBn: 'বাগান',
    labelEn: 'Garden',
  },
  {
    route: 'scan',
    icon: 'scan', // Replaced camera with dedicated scan viewfinder (Correction #3)
    labelBn: 'স্ক্যান',
    labelEn: 'Scan',
  },
  {
    route: 'guide',
    icon: '🧭',
    labelBn: 'গাইড',
    labelEn: 'Guide',
  },
  {
    route: 'profile',
    icon: '👤',
    labelBn: 'প্রোফাইল',
    labelEn: 'Profile',
  },
];

export const BottomNavBar: React.FC<BottomNavBarProps> = ({
  activeRoute,
  onSelectRoute,
  theme,
  lang,
}) => {
  const fontFam =
    lang === 'bn'
      ? APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyBengali
      : APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyEnglish;

  const resolveActive = (itemRoute: AppScreenRoute): boolean => {
    if (activeRoute === itemRoute) return true;
    if (activeRoute === 'weather_dashboard' && itemRoute === 'home')
      return true;
    if (activeRoute === 'plant_profile' && itemRoute === 'my_garden')
      return true;
    if (
      (activeRoute === 'scanner' || activeRoute === 'qr_tracker') &&
      itemRoute === 'scan'
    )
      return true;
    if (
      (activeRoute === 'planner' || activeRoute === 'directory') &&
      itemRoute === 'guide'
    )
      return true;
    if (
      (activeRoute === 'settings' || activeRoute === 'notifications') &&
      itemRoute === 'profile'
    )
      return true;
    return false;
  };

  return (
    <View
      style={[
        styles.barOuter,
        {
          backgroundColor: theme.surface,
          borderTopColor: theme.border,
        },
      ]}
    >
      <View
        style={[
          styles.barDock,
          {
            backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
            borderColor: theme.border,
          },
        ]}
      >
        {NAV_ITEMS.map((item) => {
          const isActive = resolveActive(item.route);
          const isScan = item.route === 'scan';

          const boxBg = isActive
            ? theme.primary
            : isScan
            ? theme.mode === 'dark'
              ? 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.14)'
              : '#FFFFFF'
            : 'transparent';

          const boxBorder = isActive
            ? theme.primary
            : isScan
            ? theme.mode === 'dark'
              ? 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.22)'
              : '#DCE5DF'
            : 'transparent';

          const itemColor = isActive
            ? '#FFFFFF'
            : isScan
            ? theme.primary
            : theme.textSecondary;

          return (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={item.route}
              onPress={() => onSelectRoute(item.route)}
              activeOpacity={0.82}
              style={[
                styles.tabBox,
                {
                  backgroundColor: boxBg,
                  borderColor: boxBorder,
                },
                isScan && !isActive && {
                  shadowColor: '#000',
                  shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 1 },
                  shadowOpacity: theme.mode === 'dark' ? 0.25 : 0.08,
                  shadowRadius: 3,
                  elevation: 2,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <View style={styles.iconContainer}>
                {item.icon === 'scan' ? (
                  <Svg width={20} height={20} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none">
                    <Path
                      d="M3 8V5C3 3.89543 3.89543 3 5 3H8"
                      stroke={itemColor}
                      strokeWidth="2.4"
                      strokeLinecap="round"
                    />
                    <Path
                      d="M16 3H19C20.1046 3 21 3.89543 21 5V8"
                      stroke={itemColor}
                      strokeWidth="2.4"
                      strokeLinecap="round"
                    />
                    <Path
                      d="M21 16V19C21 20.1046 20.1046 21 19 21H16"
                      stroke={itemColor}
                      strokeWidth="2.4"
                      strokeLinecap="round"
                    />
                    <Path
                      d="M8 21H5C3.89543 21 3 20.1046 3 19V16"
                      stroke={itemColor}
                      strokeWidth="2.4"
                      strokeLinecap="round"
                    />
                    <Path
                      d="M5 12H19"
                      stroke={itemColor}
                      strokeWidth="2"
                      strokeLinecap="round"
                    />
                    <Rect
                      x="9"
                      y="9"
                      width="6"
                      height="6"
                      rx="1"
                      fill={itemColor}
                    />
                  </Svg>
                ) : (
                  <Text style={styles.iconText}>{item.icon}</Text>
                )}
              </View>
              <Text
                numberOfLines={1}
                style={[
                  styles.labelText,
                  {
                    color: itemColor,
                    fontWeight: isActive || isScan ? '700' : '600',
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? item.labelBn : item.labelEn}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          );
        })}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  barOuter: {
    width: '100%',
    borderTopWidth: 1,
    alignItems: 'center',
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 8,
  },
  barDock: {
    width: '100%',
    maxWidth: 960,
    flexDirection: 'row',
    padding: 6,
    borderRadius: 18,
    borderWidth: 1,
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: 6,
  },
  tabBox: {
    flex: 1,
    minWidth: 0,
    paddingVertical: 7,
    paddingHorizontal: 6,
    borderRadius: 13,
    borderWidth: 1,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    gap: 4,
  },
  iconContainer: {
    width: 28,
    height: 24,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    backgroundColor: 'transparent',
  },
  iconText: {
    fontSize: 15,
  },
  labelText: {
    fontSize: 11.5,
    textAlign: 'center',
  },
});

/**
 * BAGANI — Centralized Semantic Design System, Brand Color Tokens & Typography
 * Strictly implements DESIGN_SYSTEM.md specifications:
 * - Botanical Green Brand Identity (#167A45, #20A464, #E7F4EB, #123B29, #B9DB54)
 * - Light Theme (#F7F9F7 / #FFFFFF / #17231C / #66736A / #E5EBE6 / #167A45 / #E7F4EB)
 * - Dark Theme (#101713 / #19231D / #222E26 / #EDF5EF / #A1B0A5 / #2D3B32 / #55C889 / #83D6A5 / #233D2D)
 * - Typography: Bengali (Hind Siliguri -> Noto Sans Bengali), English (Inter -> Roboto)
 */

import { Platform } from 'react-native';

export type ThemeModePreference = 'light' | 'dark' | 'system';
export type FontScalePreference = 'normal' | 'large';

export const BRAND_COLORS = {
  primaryGreen: '#167A45',
  secondaryGreen: '#20A464',
  softGreen: '#E7F4EB',
  deepForest: '#123B29',
  freshLime: '#B9DB54',
} as const;

  mainCardRadius: 16,
  smallCardRadius: 12,
  buttonRadius: 12,
  inputRadius: 12,
  screenHorizontalPadding: 20,
  cardPadding: 16,
  spacing: {
    xs: 8,
    sm: 12,
    md: 16,
    lg: 24,
    xl: 32,
  },
  buttonHeight: 50,
  iconSize: 22,
} as const;

export interface AppThemeColors {
  mode: 'light' | 'dark';
  background: string;

  surfaceElevated: string;
  selectedSurface: string;
  cardBackground: string;
  primary: string;
  primaryAccent: string;

const BENGALI_FONT_STACK = Platform.select({
  web: '"Hind Siliguri", "Noto Sans Bengali", "Inter", "Roboto", sans-serif',
  default: 'System',
});

const ENGLISH_FONT_STACK = Platform.select({
  web: '"Inter", "Roboto", "Hind Siliguri", "Noto Sans Bengali", sans-serif',
  default: 'System',
});

export const APP_TYPOGRAPHY = {
  fontFamilyBengali: BENGALI_FONT_STACK,
  fontFamilyEnglish: ENGLISH_FONT_STACK,
  headingFont: ENGLISH_FONT_STACK,
  bodyFont: BENGALI_FONT_STACK,
};

/**
 * Injects Google Fonts:
 * - Bengali: 'Hind Siliguri' (Primary) + 'Noto Sans Bengali' (Fallback)
 * - English: 'Inter' (Primary) + 'Roboto' (Fallback)
 * Uses clean inheritance without breaking icon fonts or line-heights.
 */
export function ensureWebFontsLoaded(): void {
  if (Platform.OS !== 'web' || typeof document === 'undefined') return;

  // Remove any older style tags that had !important on span/*
  const oldIds = ['bagani-global-ui-polish', 'bagani-design-system-reset'];
  oldIds.forEach((id) => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) el.remove();
  });

  const fontLinkId = 'bagani-design-system-fonts-v3';
  if (!document.getElementById(fontLinkId)) {
    const link = document.createElement('link');
    link.id = fontLinkId;
    link.rel = 'stylesheet';
    link.href =
      'https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Hind+Siliguri:wght@400;500;600;700&family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700&family=Noto+Sans+Bengali:wght@400;500;600;700&family=Roboto:wght@400;500;700&display=swap';
    document.head.appendChild(link);
  }

  const styleId = 'bagani-clean-layout-v3';
  if (!document.getElementById(styleId)) {
    const style = document.createElement('style');
    style.id = styleId;
    style.textContent = `
      html, body, #root {
        margin: 0;
        padding: 0;
        width: 100%;
        height: 100%;
        overflow: hidden;
        font-family: 'Inter', 'Hind Siliguri', 'Noto Sans Bengali', 'Roboto', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, sans-serif;
        -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
        -moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale;
      }

      input, textarea, button, select {
        font-family: 'Inter', 'Hind Siliguri', 'Noto Sans Bengali', 'Roboto', sans-serif;
      }

      /* Hide scrollbars cleanly while keeping smooth scrolling */
      * {
        scrollbar-width: none;
        -ms-overflow-style: none;
        box-sizing: border-box;
      }
      *::-webkit-scrollbar {
        width: 0px;
        height: 0px;
        display: none;
        background: transparent;
      }

      input:focus, textarea:focus {
        outline: none;
      }
    `;
    document.head.appendChild(style);
  }
}

/**
 * Dynamically adjusts global font scale across the entire web application within safe bounds (88% to 118%)
 * to guarantee that text never overflows containers or disrupts the layout architecture.
 */
export function applyGlobalFontScale(scalePercent: number): void {
  if (Platform.OS !== 'web' || typeof document === 'undefined') return;
  const clamped = Math.max(88, Math.min(118, Math.round(scalePercent)));
  const styleId = 'bagani-dynamic-font-scale';
  let el = document.getElementById(styleId) as HTMLStyleElement | null;
  if (!el) {
    el = document.createElement('style');
    el.id = styleId;
    document.head.appendChild(el);
  }
  el.textContent = `
    html {
      font-size: ${clamped}%;
    }
  `;
}

// FILE END: lib/core/theme/app_theme.ts

// ##############################################################################
// FILE START: lib/core/utils/date_utils.ts
// ##############################################################################

/**
 * BAGANI — Dual Calendar (Bengali বঙ্গাব্দ + English Date) & Greeting Utility
 */

import { AppLanguage, TRANSLATIONS } from '../l10n/translations';

const BENGALI_DIGITS = ['০', '১', '২', '৩', '৪', '৫', '৬', '৭', '৮', '৯'];
const BENGALI_MONTHS = [
  'বৈশাখ',
  'জ্যৈষ্ঠ',
  'আষাঢ়',
  'শ্রাবণ',
  'ভাদ্র',
  'আশ্বিন',
  'কার্তিক',
  'অগ্রহায়ণ',
  'পৌষ',
  'মাঘ',
  'ফাল্গুন',
  'চৈত্র',
];

export function toBengaliNumber(num: number | string): string {
  return String(num).replace(/\d/g, (d) => BENGALI_DIGITS[Number(d)]);
}

export function getDynamicGreeting(lang: AppLanguage, date = new Date()): string {
  const hour = date.getHours();
  const t = TRANSLATIONS[lang];
  if (hour < 12) return t.greetingMorning;
  if (hour < 18) return t.greetingAfternoon;
  return t.greetingEvening;
}

export function getFormattedDualDate(date = new Date()): {
  englishDate: string;
  bengaliDate: string;
  timeString: string;
} {
  const englishDate = date.toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  });

  const timeString = date.toLocaleTimeString([], {
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
  });

  // Approximate Bengali calendar calculation (Bangla Academy revised calendar)
  const gregYear = date.getFullYear();
  const gregMonth = date.getMonth(); // 0-11
  const gregDay = date.getDate();

  let banglaYear = gregYear - 593;
  if (gregMonth < 3 || (gregMonth === 3 && gregDay < 14)) {
    banglaYear = gregYear - 594;
  }

  // Month starts around 14th-16th of each Gregorian month
  const monthStartDays = [14, 15, 15, 16, 16, 16, 16, 15, 15, 14, 13, 14];
  let banglaMonthIndex = (gregMonth + 8) % 12;
  const startDay = monthStartDays[banglaMonthIndex];
  let banglaDay = gregDay - startDay + 1;
  if (banglaDay <= 0) {
    banglaMonthIndex = (banglaMonthIndex + 11) % 12;
    banglaDay = 30 + banglaDay;
  }

  const bengaliDate = `${toBengaliNumber(banglaDay)} ${BENGALI_MONTHS[banglaMonthIndex]}, ${toBengaliNumber(banglaYear)} বঙ্গাব্দ`;

  return { englishDate, bengaliDate, timeString };
}

  | 'sapling'

  | 'flowering'
  | 'fruiting'
  | 'dormant';

  | 'geo_bag'

  | 'loam_compost'

export interface PlantModel {
  id: string;

  containerType?: ContainerType; // Geo-Bag, Gallon Pot, Bucket/Drum, Clay/Cement Pot, Direct Bed
  containerCapacityLabel?: string; // e.g., "20 Inch Geo-Bag (~65L Soil)" or "15 Gallon Pot (~55L)" or "20L Bucket"

  customFertilizerGuideEn?: string;
  customCareGuideBn?: string;
  customCareGuideEn?: string;
  onlineKnowledgeProfile?: PlantOnlineKnowledgeProfile;
  rooftopZone: string; // e.g., South-East Cornice Row
  photoUri?: string;
  healthState: PlantHealthState;
  lastHealthDiagnosisSummary?: string;
  lastWateredIso?: string;
  lastFertilizedIso?: string;
  estimatedMoisturePercent: number; // Software-calculated from logs & local weather
  qrTagConfig: PrintableQrTagConfig;
}

/**
 * BAGANI — User Profile & Preferences Model
 * Supports Google Sign-In, Apple Sign-In (iOS), Editable Profile, and Gardener Taste FAQ
 */

import { AppLanguage } from '../core/l10n/translations';
import { ThemeModePreference } from '../core/theme/app_theme';

export type CultivationInterest = 'fruits' | 'vegetables' | 'both';
export type OriginPreference = 'deshi' | 'exotic' | 'both';
export type TastePreference = 'sweet' | 'sour_tangy' | 'both';

export interface GardenerTasteProfile {
  cultivationInterest: CultivationInterest;
  originPreference: OriginPreference;
  tastePreference: TastePreference;
}

export interface UserModel {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  displayName: string;
  photoUrl?: string;
  district: string;
  address?: string;
  organizationOrProfession?: string;
  authProvider: 'google' | 'apple' | 'guest';
  hasPasswordConfigured: boolean;
  language: AppLanguage;
  themePreference: ThemeModePreference;
  tasteProfile?: GardenerTasteProfile;
  createdAtIso: string;
  lastCloudSyncIso?: string;
}

// ##############################################################################
// FILE START: lib/screens/auth/auth_screen.tsx
// ##############################################################################

/**
 * BAGANI — Clean Minimalist Login / Sign-Up & Profile Editor
 */

import * as ImagePicker from 'expo-image-picker';
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Image,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { APP_CONFIG } from '../../core/constants/app_constants';
import { AppLanguage } from '../../core/l10n/translations';
import {
  APP_TYPOGRAPHY,
  AppThemeColors,
  DESIGN_TOKENS,
} from '../../core/theme/app_theme';
import {
  CultivationInterest,
  OriginPreference,
  TastePreference,
  UserModel,
} from '../../models/user_model';
import { AuthService, BANGLADESH_DISTRICTS } from '../../services/auth_service';

interface AuthScreenProps {
  theme: AppThemeColors;
  lang: AppLanguage;
  initialUser?: UserModel | null;
  onCompleteAuth: (user: UserModel) => void;
  onCancelEdit?: () => void;
}

export const AuthScreen: React.FC<AuthScreenProps> = ({
  theme,
  lang,
  initialUser,
  onCompleteAuth,
  onCancelEdit,
}) => {
  const [step, setStep] = useState<'login' | 'customize_profile'>(
    initialUser ? 'customize_profile' : 'login'
  );
  const [authMode, setAuthMode] = useState<'signin' | 'signup'>('signin');

  // Editable Profile Fields
  const [emailInput, setEmailInput] = useState(
    initialUser?.email || 'bagani.profix@gmail.com'
  );
  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState(
    initialUser?.displayName || 'সবুজ বাগানি'
  );
  const [photoUrl, setPhotoUrl] = useState<string | undefined>(
    initialUser?.photoUrl
  );
  const [district, setDistrict] = useState(
    initialUser?.district || BANGLADESH_DISTRICTS[0].bn
  );
  const [address, setAddress] = useState(
    initialUser?.address || 'পিঙ্গলহাটি, জামালপুর সদর'
  );
  const [organization, setOrganization] = useState(
    initialUser?.organizationOrProfession || 'ProFix - Web & Soft'
  );
  const [appPassword, setAppPassword] = useState('');

  // Gardener Taste Fields
  const [cultivationInterest, setCultivationInterest] =
    useState<CultivationInterest>(
      initialUser?.tasteProfile?.cultivationInterest || 'both'
    );
  const [originPreference, setOriginPreference] = useState<OriginPreference>(
    initialUser?.tasteProfile?.originPreference || 'both'
  );
  const [tastePreference, setTastePreference] = useState<TastePreference>(
    initialUser?.tasteProfile?.tastePreference || 'sweet'
  );

  // Sync state whenever initialUser prop changes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (initialUser) {
      setStep('customize_profile');
      setEmailInput(initialUser.email || 'bagani.profix@gmail.com');
      setDisplayName(initialUser.displayName || 'সবুজ বাগানি');
      setPhotoUrl(initialUser.photoUrl);
      setDistrict(initialUser.district || BANGLADESH_DISTRICTS[0].bn);
      setAddress(initialUser.address || '');
      setOrganization(initialUser.organizationOrProfession || '');
      setCultivationInterest(
        initialUser.tasteProfile?.cultivationInterest || 'both'
      );
      setOriginPreference(initialUser.tasteProfile?.originPreference || 'both');
      setTastePreference(initialUser.tasteProfile?.tastePreference || 'sweet');
    } else {
      setStep('login');
    }
  }, [initialUser]);

  const fontFam =
    lang === 'bn'
      ? APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyBengali
      : APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyEnglish;

  const handleDirectLoginOrSignup = async () => {
    const gUser = await AuthService.signInWithGoogle(emailInput);
    const finalName =
      authMode === 'signup' && displayName.trim()
        ? displayName.trim()
        : gUser.displayName;
    const saved = await AuthService.saveUserProfile(gUser, {
      displayName: finalName,
      email: emailInput.trim() || 'gardener@gmail.com',
      language: lang,
    });
    onCompleteAuth(saved);
  };

  const handleGoogleLogin = async () => {
    const gUser = await AuthService.signInWithGoogle(emailInput);
    const saved = await AuthService.saveUserProfile(gUser, {
      language: lang,
    });
    onCompleteAuth(saved);
  };

  const handleAppleLogin = async () => {
    const aUser = await AuthService.signInWithApple();
    const saved = await AuthService.saveUserProfile(aUser, {
      language: lang,
    });
    onCompleteAuth(saved);
  };

  const handlePickProfilePhoto = async () => {
    const result = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
      mediaTypes: ['images'],
      allowsEditing: true,
      aspect: [1, 1],
      quality: 0.7,
    });
    if (!result.canceled && result.assets && result.assets.length > 0) {
      setPhotoUrl(result.assets[0].uri);
    }
  };

  const handleSaveProfileChanges = async () => {
    const baseUser =
      initialUser || (await AuthService.signInWithGoogle(emailInput));
    const saved = await AuthService.saveUserProfile(baseUser, {
      displayName: displayName.trim() || baseUser.displayName || 'সবুজ বাগানি',
      email: emailInput.trim() || baseUser.email || 'gardener@gmail.com',
      photoUrl,
      district: district || BANGLADESH_DISTRICTS[0].bn,
      address: address.trim(),
      organizationOrProfession: organization.trim(),
      hasPasswordConfigured:
        appPassword.trim().length > 0 || baseUser.hasPasswordConfigured,
      language: lang,
      tasteProfile: {
        cultivationInterest,
        originPreference,
        tastePreference,
      },
    });
    onCompleteAuth(saved);
  };

  // STEP 1: CLEAN, CENTERED LOGIN / SIGN-UP SCREEN (No Top/Bottom Bars, Zero Clutter)
  if (step === 'login') {
    return (
      <ScrollView
        style={{ flex: 1, width: '100%' }}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
        contentContainerStyle={styles.loginCenteredOuter}
      >
        <View
          style={[
            styles.loginCard,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          {/* Brand Logo & Title */}
          <View style={styles.logoWrap}>
            <Image
              source={require('../../../assets/images/logo.png')}
              style={styles.logoImage}
              resizeMode="contain"
            />
          </View>

          <Text
            style={[
              styles.loginTitle,
              { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
            ]}
          >
            {APP_CONFIG.appName}
          </Text>
          <Text
            style={[
              styles.loginSub,
              { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
            ]}
          >
            {authMode === 'signin'
              ? lang === 'bn'
                ? 'আপনার অ্যাকাউন্টে সাইন ইন করুন'
                : 'Sign in to your account'
              : lang === 'bn'
              ? 'নতুন অ্যাকাউন্ট তৈরি করুন'
              : 'Create your account'}
          </Text>

          {/* Sign In / Sign Up Mode Switcher */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.authModeToggle,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => setAuthMode('signin')}
              style={[
                styles.authModeBtn,
                authMode === 'signin' && { backgroundColor: theme.primary },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.authModeText,
                  {
                    color:
                      authMode === 'signin' ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textSecondary,
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'সাইন ইন' : 'Sign In'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => setAuthMode('signup')}
              style={[
                styles.authModeBtn,
                authMode === 'signup' && { backgroundColor: theme.primary },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.authModeText,
                  {
                    color:
                      authMode === 'signup' ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textSecondary,
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'সাইন আপ' : 'Sign Up'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          {/* Form Inputs */}
          {authMode === 'signup' && (
            <View style={styles.inputGroup}>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.label,
                  { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'পূর্ণ নাম' : 'Full Name'}
              </Text>
              <TextInput
                value={displayName}
                onChangeText={setDisplayName}
                placeholder={lang === 'bn' ? 'আপনার নাম' : 'Enter your name'}
                placeholderTextColor={theme.textMuted}
                style={[
                  styles.input,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                    color: theme.textPrimary,
                    borderColor: theme.border,
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  },
                ]}
              />
            </View>
          )}

          <View style={styles.inputGroup}>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.label,
                { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn' ? 'ইমেইল' : 'Email'}
            </Text>
            <TextInput
              value={emailInput}
              onChangeText={setEmailInput}
              keyboardType="email-address"
              autoCapitalize="none"
              placeholder="name@example.com"
              placeholderTextColor={theme.textMuted}
              style={[
                styles.input,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                },
              ]}
            />
          </View>

          <View style={styles.inputGroup}>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.label,
                { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn' ? 'পাসওয়ার্ড' : 'Password'}
            </Text>
            <TextInput
              value={appPassword}
              onChangeText={setAppPassword}
              secureTextEntry
              placeholder="••••••••"
              placeholderTextColor={theme.textMuted}
              style={[
                styles.input,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                },
              ]}
            />
          </View>

          {/* Primary Sign In / Create Account Button */}
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={handleDirectLoginOrSignup}
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            style={[styles.primaryAuthBtn, { backgroundColor: theme.primary }]}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.primaryAuthBtnText, { fontFamily: fontFam }]}>
              {authMode === 'signin'
                ? lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'সাইন ইন'
                  : 'Sign In'
                : lang === 'bn'
                ? 'অ্যাকাউন্ট তৈরি করুন'
                : 'Create Account'}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          {/* Clean Divider */}
          <View style={styles.dividerRow}>
            <View
              style={[styles.dividerLine, { backgroundColor: theme.border }]}
            />
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.dividerText,
                { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn' ? 'অথবা' : 'or'}
            </Text>
            <View
              style={[styles.dividerLine, { backgroundColor: theme.border }]}
            />
          </View>

          {/* Social Sign-In Buttons */}
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={handleGoogleLogin}
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            style={[
              styles.socialBtn,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.socialBtnText,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn' ? 'Google দিয়ে চালিয়ে যান' : 'Continue with Google'}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={handleAppleLogin}
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            style={[
              styles.socialBtn,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.socialBtnText,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn' ? 'Apple দিয়ে চালিয়ে যান' : 'Continue with Apple'}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    );
  }

  // STEP 2: EDIT PROFILE SCREEN (Full Bottom Navigation Bar remains visible)
  return (
    <ScrollView
      style={{ flex: 1, width: '100%' }}
      showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
      contentContainerStyle={styles.editScrollOuter}
    >
      <View style={styles.editContainer}>
        <View
          style={[
            styles.card,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={styles.editHeaderRow}>
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.titleLeft,
                  { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'প্রোফাইল এডিট করুন' : 'Edit Profile'}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textSecondary,
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'আপনার ব্যক্তিগত তথ্য ও বাগানের পছন্দ হালনাগাদ করুন'
                  : 'Update your personal information and gardening preferences'}
              </Text>
            </View>

            {onCancelEdit && (
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={onCancelEdit}
                style={[
                  styles.cancelTopBtn,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                    borderColor: theme.border,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: theme.textPrimary,
                    fontWeight: '600',
                    fontSize: 13,
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  }}
                >
                  ← {lang === 'bn' ? 'ফিরে যান' : 'Back'}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            )}
          </View>

          {/* Profile Photo Picker */}
          <View style={styles.avatarRow}>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.avatarCircle,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                  borderColor: theme.primary,
                },
              ]}
            >
              {photoUrl ? (
                <Image source={{ uri: photoUrl }} style={styles.avatarImg} />
              ) : (
                <Text style={styles.avatarEmoji}>🌿</Text>
              )}
            </View>
            <View style={styles.avatarTextCol}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={handlePickProfilePhoto}
                style={[
                  styles.photoPickBtn,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                    borderColor: theme.primary,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={[
                    styles.photoPickText,
                    { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                  ]}
                >
                  {lang === 'bn'
                    ? '📷 ছবি পরিবর্তন করুন'
                    : '📷 Change Profile Photo'}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Editable Name */}
          <View style={styles.inputGroup}>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.label,
                { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn' ? 'পূর্ণ নাম' : 'Full Name'}
            </Text>
            <TextInput
              value={displayName}
              onChangeText={setDisplayName}
              style={[
                styles.input,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                },
              ]}
            />
          </View>

          {/* Editable Email */}
          <View style={styles.inputGroup}>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.label,
                { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn' ? 'ইমেইল অ্যাড্রেস' : 'Email Address'}
            </Text>
            <TextInput
              value={emailInput}
              onChangeText={setEmailInput}
              keyboardType="email-address"
              autoCapitalize="none"
              style={[
                styles.input,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                },
              ]}
            />
          </View>

          {/* District Selector */}
          <View style={styles.inputGroup}>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.label,
                { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn' ? 'জেলা' : 'District'}
            </Text>
            <ScrollView
              horizontal
              showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
              contentContainerStyle={styles.chipScroll}
            >
              {BANGLADESH_DISTRICTS.map((d) => {
                const selected = district === d.bn || district === d.en;
                return (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={d.en}
                    onPress={() => setDistrict(d.bn)}
                    style={[
                      styles.chip,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: selected
                          ? theme.primary
                          : theme.surfaceElevated,
                        borderColor: selected ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.chipText,
                        {
                          color: selected ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                          fontFamily: fontFam,
                        },
                      ]}
                    >
                      📍 {lang === 'bn' ? d.bn : d.en}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                );
              })}
            </ScrollView>
          </View>

          {/* Address & Organization */}
          <View style={styles.inputGroup}>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.label,
                { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'ঠিকানা / ছাদের অবস্থান'
                : 'Address / Rooftop Location'}
            </Text>
            <TextInput
              value={address}
              onChangeText={setAddress}
              style={[
                styles.input,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                },
              ]}
            />
          </View>

          <View style={styles.inputGroup}>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.label,
                { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'পেশা বা প্রতিষ্ঠান'
                : 'Profession / Organization'}
            </Text>
            <TextInput
              value={organization}
              onChangeText={setOrganization}
              style={[
                styles.input,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                },
              ]}
            />
          </View>

          {/* Gardener Preferences */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.faqBox,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.faqTitle,
                { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              🌱{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'বাগানির পছন্দ ও রুচি'
                : 'Gardening Preferences'}
            </Text>

            <Text
              style={[
                styles.label,
                { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn' ? 'চাষের আগ্রহ:' : 'Cultivation Focus:'}
            </Text>
            <View style={styles.optionRow}>
              {(
                [
                  { val: 'fruits', bn: '🍎 ফল গাছ', en: '🍎 Fruits' },
                  { val: 'vegetables', bn: '🥦 শাকসবজি', en: '🥦 Vegetables' },
                  { val: 'both', bn: '🌿 উভয়ই', en: '🌿 Both' },
                ] as const
              ).map((opt) => (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={opt.val}
                  onPress={() => setCultivationInterest(opt.val)}
                  style={[
                    styles.optChip,
                    {
                      backgroundColor:
                        cultivationInterest === opt.val
                          ? theme.primary
                          : theme.surface,
                      borderColor:
                        cultivationInterest === opt.val
                          ? theme.primary
                          : theme.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color:
                        cultivationInterest === opt.val
                          ? '#FFFFFF'
                          : theme.textPrimary,
                      fontSize: 12,
                      fontWeight: '600',
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    {lang === 'bn' ? opt.bn : opt.en}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              ))}
            </View>

            <Text
              style={[
                styles.label,
                {
                  color: theme.textSecondary,
                  marginTop: 8,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn' ? 'জাতের পছন্দ:' : 'Preferred Varieties:'}
            </Text>
            <View style={styles.optionRow}>
              {(
                [
                  { val: 'deshi', bn: '🇧🇩 দেশি জাত', en: '🇧🇩 Deshi' },
                  { val: 'exotic', bn: '🌏 বিদেশি জাত', en: '🌏 Exotic' },
                  { val: 'both', bn: '✨ উভয়ই', en: '✨ Both' },
                ] as const
              ).map((opt) => (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={opt.val}
                  onPress={() => setOriginPreference(opt.val)}
                  style={[
                    styles.optChip,
                    {
                      backgroundColor:
                        originPreference === opt.val
                          ? theme.primary
                          : theme.surface,
                      borderColor:
                        originPreference === opt.val
                          ? theme.primary
                          : theme.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color:
                        originPreference === opt.val
                          ? '#FFFFFF'
                          : theme.textPrimary,
                      fontSize: 12,
                      fontWeight: '600',
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    {lang === 'bn' ? opt.bn : opt.en}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              ))}
            </View>

            <Text
              style={[
                styles.label,
                {
                  color: theme.textSecondary,
                  marginTop: 8,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn' ? 'স্বাদের পছন্দ:' : 'Taste Preference:'}
            </Text>
            <View style={styles.optionRow}>
              {(
                [
                  { val: 'sweet', bn: '🍯 মিষ্টি', en: '🍯 Sweet' },
                  { val: 'sour_tangy', bn: '🍋 টক-মিষ্টি', en: '🍋 Tangy' },
                  { val: 'both', bn: '🍊 সব ধরনের', en: '🍊 All' },
                ] as const
              ).map((opt) => (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={opt.val}
                  onPress={() => setTastePreference(opt.val)}
                  style={[
                    styles.optChip,
                    {
                      backgroundColor:
                        tastePreference === opt.val
                          ? theme.primary
                          : theme.surface,
                      borderColor:
                        tastePreference === opt.val
                          ? theme.primary
                          : theme.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color:
                        tastePreference === opt.val
                          ? '#FFFFFF'
                          : theme.textPrimary,
                      fontSize: 12,
                      fontWeight: '600',
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    {lang === 'bn' ? opt.bn : opt.en}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              ))}
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Bottom Save & Cancel Action Row */}
          <View style={styles.bottomActionRow}>
            {onCancelEdit && (
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={onCancelEdit}
                activeOpacity={0.8}
                style={[
                  styles.cancelBottomBtn,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                    borderColor: theme.border,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: theme.textSecondary,
                    fontWeight: '700',
                    fontSize: 14,
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  }}
                >
                  {lang === 'bn' ? 'বাতিল' : 'Cancel'}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            )}

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={handleSaveProfileChanges}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
              style={[
                styles.saveProfileBtn,
                { backgroundColor: theme.primary },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.primaryAuthBtnText, { fontFamily: fontFam }]}>
                {lang === 'bn' ? '✓ পরিবর্তন সেভ করুন' : '✓ Save Changes'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>
    </ScrollView>
  );
};
