/**
 * BAGANI — Built-In Legal, Privacy Policy, Terms & Data Safety Modal
 * Ensures 100% Google Play Store & Apple App Store Review Compliance
 */

import React from 'react';
import {
  Modal,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { APP_CONFIG } from '../../core/constants/app_constants';
import { AppLanguage } from '../../core/l10n/translations';
import { AppThemeColors } from '../../core/theme/app_theme';

interface LegalModalProps {
  visible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  theme: AppThemeColors;
  lang: AppLanguage;
}

export const LegalModal: React.FC<LegalModalProps> = ({
  visible,
  onClose,
  theme,
  lang,
}) => {
  return (
    <Modal visible={visible} animationType="slide" transparent>
      <View style={styles.overlay}>
        <View
          style={[
            styles.sheet,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={[styles.header, { borderBottomColor: theme.border }]}>
            <Text style={[styles.title, { color: theme.textPrimary }]}>
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'প্রাইভেসি পলিসি ও শর্তাবলী (Privacy & Terms)'
                : 'Privacy Policy, Terms & Data Safety'}
            </Text>
            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={onClose}
              style={[
                styles.closeBtn,
                { backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.closeText, { color: theme.textPrimary }]}>
                ✕
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          <ScrollView
            showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
            showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
            contentContainerStyle={styles.body}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.sectionHead, { color: theme.primary }]}>
              1. {APP_CONFIG.appName} — {APP_CONFIG.developerName}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.paragraph, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'BAGANI (Smart Gardening & Plant Care) অ্যাপটি গুগল প্লে স্টোর (Android 10+) এবং অ্যাপল অ্যাপ স্টোর (iOS 15.0 - iOS 27+) এর সমস্ত প্রাইভেসি, ডেটা সেফটি এবং ইউজার গাইডলাইন মেনে তৈরি করা হয়েছে।'
                : 'BAGANI (Smart Gardening & Plant Care) is engineered in full compliance with Google Play Store (Android 10+) and Apple App Store (iOS 15.0 to iOS 27+) privacy, data safety, and user protection standards.'}
            </Text>

            <Text style={[styles.sectionHead, { color: theme.primary }]}>
              2. {lang === 'bn' ? 'পারমিশন ও ডেটা সুরক্ষা' : 'Permissions & Data Safety'}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.paragraph, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? '• ক্যামেরা ও ফটো পিকার: শুধুমাত্র টবের QR কোড স্ক্যান এবং গাছের পাতার রোগ নির্ণয়ের ছবি তোলার জন্য ব্যবহৃত হয়।\n• লোকেশন: কেবল স্থানীয় আবহাওয়া ভিত্তিক পানি ও সার দেওয়ার সময়সূচি এবং নিকটস্থ কৃষি অফিস/নার্সারি খুঁজে পেতে ব্যবহৃত হয়।\n• অফলাইন ও ক্লাউড ব্যাকআপ: আপনার সব গাছের তথ্য লোকাল ডাটাবেজে এবং আপনার Google অ্যাকাউন্টে নিরাপদে সিঙ্ক থাকে।'
                : '• Camera & Photo Picker: Used exclusively for scanning plant QR tags and capturing leaf photos for disease diagnosis.\n• Coarse Location: Used only while the app is open to provide local weather care schedules and nearby agriculture directories.\n• Offline & Cloud Sync: Your garden records are stored locally first and backed up securely to your account.'}
            </Text>

            <Text style={[styles.sectionHead, { color: theme.primary }]}>
              3.{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'অ্যাকাউন্ট ও ডেটা ডিলিট করার অধিকার'
                : 'Account & Cloud Data Deletion Right'}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.paragraph, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'ব্যবহারকারী যেকোনো সময় প্রোফাইল বা সেটিংস থেকে "Delete Account & Cloud Data" বাটনে ক্লিক করে তার সম্পূর্ণ অ্যাকাউন্ট ও ক্লাউড ডেটা স্থায়ীভাবে মুছে ফেলতে পারবেন।'
                : 'Users can permanently delete their account and all associated cloud and local data at any time directly from the Profile/Settings screen.'}
            </Text>

            <Text style={[styles.sectionHead, { color: theme.primary }]}>
              4.{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'এআই কৃষি ও উদ্ভিদ পরামর্শ ডিসক্লেইমার'
                : 'AI Botanical Guidance Disclaimer'}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.paragraph, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'অ্যাপের এআই ক্যামেরা স্ক্যানার এবং গ্রোথ পূর্বাভাস ছাদ বাগান পরিচর্যার সহায়ক নির্দেশিকা হিসেবে কাজ করে। জটিল কৃষি সমস্যায় স্থানীয় কৃষি অফিসের পরামর্শ নেওয়ার সুবিধা অ্যাপে যুক্ত রয়েছে।'
                : 'AI plant identification, disease remedies, and growth forecasts are provided as horticultural guidance for rooftop gardeners.'}
            </Text>
          </ScrollView>
        </View>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  overlay: {
    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.65)',
    justifyContent: 'flex-end',
  },
  sheet: {
    maxHeight: '82%',
    borderTopLeftRadius: 24,
    borderTopRightRadius: 24,
    borderWidth: 1,
  },
  header: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
    padding: 18,
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
  },
  title: {
    fontSize: 16,
    fontWeight: '800',
    flex: 1,
  },
  closeBtn: {
    width: 36,
    height: 36,
    borderRadius: 18,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },
  closeText: {
    fontSize: 16,
    fontWeight: '700',
  },
  body: {
    padding: 18,
    gap: 10,
  },
  sectionHead: {
    fontSize: 14,
    fontWeight: '700',
    marginTop: 6,
  },
  paragraph: {
    fontSize: 13,
    lineHeight: 20,
  },
});



// ##############################################################################
// FILE END: lib/screens/auth/legal_modal.tsx
// ##############################################################################


// ##############################################################################
// FILE START: lib/screens/directory/directory_screen.tsx
// ##############################################################################

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Alert,
  Linking,
  Modal,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { AppLanguage } from '../../core/l10n/translations';
import {
  APP_TYPOGRAPHY,
  AppThemeColors,
  DESIGN_TOKENS,
} from '../../core/theme/app_theme';
import {
  GardenerExperienceInsight,
  MasterContactCategory,
  MasterContactEntry,
  RemoteDirectoryService,
} from '../../services/remote_directory_service';

interface DirectoryScreenProps {
  theme: AppThemeColors;
  lang: AppLanguage;
  userDistrict: string;
  userName: string;
}

type DirectoryTab = 'agri_office' | 'experts' | 'nurseries' | 'insights' | 'community';

interface CommunityPost {
  id: string;
  author: string;
  district: string;
  message: string;
  timestamp: string;
  tag: string;
}

export const DirectoryScreen: React.FC<DirectoryScreenProps> = ({
  theme,
  lang,
  userDistrict,
  userName,
}) => {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<DirectoryTab>('agri_office');
  const [selectedDistrict, setSelectedDistrict] = useState<string>('ALL');
  const [contacts, setContacts] = useState<MasterContactEntry[]>([]);
  const [matchedDistrictContacts, setMatchedDistrictContacts] = useState<MasterContactEntry[]>([]);
  const [experiences, setExperiences] = useState<GardenerExperienceInsight[]>([]);
  const [isSyncing, setIsSyncing] = useState(false);

  // Experience Submission Modal State
  const [showAddExpModal, setShowAddExpModal] = useState(false);
  const [expPlantName, setExpPlantName] = useState('');
  const [expVariety, setExpVariety] = useState('');
  const [expContainerSoil, setExpContainerSoil] = useState('');
  const [expPruningTip, setExpPruningTip] = useState('');
  const [expDiseaseSolved, setExpDiseaseSolved] = useState('');
  const [expYieldSummary, setExpYieldSummary] = useState('');

  // Community Chat Posts State
  const [newPostText, setNewPostText] = useState('');
  const [communityPosts, setCommunityPosts] = useState<CommunityPost[]>([
    {
      id: 'post_1',
      author: 'তানভীর আহমেদ',
      district: 'ঢাকা (Dhaka)',
      message:
        'আমার ছাদের বারোমাসি কাটিমন আম গাছে এবার ২য় বার মুকুল এসেছে! কারো চারা বা সায়ন লাগলে জানাবেন।',
      timestamp: '১০ মিনিট আগে',
      tag: '🌱 চারা বিনিময়',
    },
    {
      id: 'post_2',
      author: 'ফারজানা রহমান',
      district: 'চট্টগ্রাম (Chattogram)',
      message:
        'বর্ষার আগে জিও ব্যাগের মাটিতে ট্রাইকোডার্মা ও নিম খৈল ব্যবহার করে দারুণ ফলাফল পেয়েছি, শেকড় পচা রোগ একদম নেই।',
      timestamp: '১ ঘণ্টা আগে',
      tag: '💡 অভিজ্ঞতা',
    },
  ]);

  const fontFam =
    lang === 'bn'
      ? APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyBengali
      : APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyEnglish;

  // Load remotely updatable contacts and experience vault on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    loadDirectoryData();
  }, [userDistrict]);

  const loadDirectoryData = async () => {
    try {
      const locRes = await RemoteDirectoryService.getContactsForLocation(userDistrict);
      setMatchedDistrictContacts(locRes.matchedDistrictContacts);
      const all = await RemoteDirectoryService.getAllContacts();
      setContacts(all);

      const exps = await RemoteDirectoryService.getExperiences();
      setExperiences(exps);
    } catch {
      // Fallback
    }
  };

  const handleRemoteSync = async () => {
    setIsSyncing(true);
    try {
      const res = await RemoteDirectoryService.syncFromRemoteJsonUrl();
      await loadDirectoryData();
      Alert.alert(
        lang === 'bn' ? 'ডিরেক্টরি আপডেট সম্পন্ন' : 'Directory Synced',
        lang === 'bn'
          ? `রিমোট ডাটাবেজ থেকে সর্বমোট ${res.totalCount}টি নার্সারি/বিশেষজ্ঞ এন্ট্রি সফলভাবে সিঙ্ক হয়েছে।`
          : `Successfully synced ${res.totalCount} verified nurseries & agri contacts.`
      );
    } catch {
      Alert.alert('Sync', 'Using cached master database.');
    } finally {
      setIsSyncing(false);
    }
  };

  const handleAddPost = () => {
    if (!newPostText.trim()) return;
    const post: CommunityPost = {
      id: `post_${Date.now()}`,
      author: userName,
      district: userDistrict,
      message: newPostText.trim(),
      timestamp: lang === 'bn' ? 'এইমাত্র' : 'Just now',
      tag: lang === 'bn' ? '🌿 বাগানী পোস্ট' : '🌿 Gardener Post',
    };
    setCommunityPosts([post, ...communityPosts]);
    setNewPostText('');
  };

  const handleUpvoteExperience = async (expId: string) => {
    const updated = await RemoteDirectoryService.markExperienceHelpful(expId);
    setExperiences(updated);
  };

  const handleSubmitNewExperience = async () => {
    if (!expPlantName.trim() || !expPruningTip.trim() || !expYieldSummary.trim()) {
      Alert.alert(
        lang === 'bn' ? 'তথ্য অসম্পূর্ণ' : 'Incomplete Form',
        lang === 'bn'
          ? 'অনুগ্রহ করে গাছের নাম, সফলতার প্রুনিং টিপস ও ফলনের বিবরণ লিখুন।'
          : 'Please enter Plant Name, Pruning Tip, and Yield Result.'
      );
      return;
    }

    const updated = await RemoteDirectoryService.submitExperience({
      authorName: userName,
      district: userDistrict,
      plantName: expPlantName.trim(),
      varietyName: expVariety.trim() || (lang === 'bn' ? 'উন্নত জাত' : 'Selected Variety'),
      containerAndSoilUsed:
        expContainerSoil.trim() ||
        (lang === 'bn' ? '২০" জিও ব্যাগ ও দোআঁশ-কম্পোস্ট মাটি' : '20" Geo-Bag & Loam-Compost'),
      keySuccessOrPruningTip: expPruningTip.trim(),
      diseaseOrChallengeSolved: expDiseaseSolved.trim(),
      yieldResultSummary: expYieldSummary.trim(),
    });

    setExperiences(updated);
    setShowAddExpModal(false);
    setExpPlantName('');
    setExpVariety('');
    setExpContainerSoil('');
    setExpPruningTip('');
    setExpDiseaseSolved('');
    setExpYieldSummary('');

    Alert.alert(
      lang === 'bn' ? 'অভিজ্ঞতা সংরক্ষিত হয়েছে' : 'Experience Saved',
      lang === 'bn'
        ? 'ধন্যবাদ! আপনার মূল্যবান অভিজ্ঞতা সফটওয়্যারের অভিজ্ঞতা ভল্টে সংরক্ষিত হয়েছে যা অন্যান্য বাগানীদের ফলন বাড়াতে সহায়তা করবে।'
        : 'Thank you! Your rooftop gardening breakthrough has been added to the Gardener Vault.'
    );
  };

  const activeCategoryFilter: MasterContactCategory | null =
    activeTab === 'agri_office'
      ? 'agri_office'
      : activeTab === 'experts'
      ? 'gardener'
      : activeTab === 'nurseries'
      ? 'nursery'
      : null;

  const filteredContacts = useMemo(() => {
    if (!activeCategoryFilter) return [];
    return contacts.filter((c) => {
      const matchCat = c.category === activeCategoryFilter;
      const matchDist =
        selectedDistrict === 'ALL' ||
        c.district.toLowerCase().includes(selectedDistrict.toLowerCase()) ||
        c.districtEn.toLowerCase().includes(selectedDistrict.toLowerCase());
      return matchCat && matchDist;
    });
  }, [contacts, activeCategoryFilter, selectedDistrict]);

  return (
    <ScrollView
      showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
      contentContainerStyle={styles.container}
    >
      {/* Emergency National Agri Helpline Banner */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.helplineBanner,
          { backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground, borderColor: theme.primary },
        ]}
      >
        <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 200 }}>
          <Text style={[styles.helplineTitle, { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam }]}>
            📞 {lang === 'bn' ? 'জাতীয় কৃষি কল সেন্টার (১৬১২৩)' : 'National Agri Helpline (16123)'}
          </Text>
          <Text style={{ color: theme.textSecondary, fontSize: 12, lineHeight: 18, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
            {lang === 'bn'
              ? 'সরকারি কৃষিবিদদের সাথে সরাসরি কথা বলুন অথবা আপনার জেলার কৃষি অফিস ও নার্সারি খুঁজুন।'
              : 'Connect directly with certified agriculturists, nurseries & local master gardeners.'}
          </Text>
        </View>

        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => Linking.openURL('tel:16123')}
            style={[styles.callBtn, { backgroundColor: theme.primary }]}
          >
            <Text style={{ color: '#FFFFFF', fontWeight: '800', fontSize: 12 }}>
              📲 16123
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={handleRemoteSync}
            disabled={isSyncing}
            style={[
              styles.syncBtn,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                borderColor: theme.primary,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text style={{ color: theme.primary, fontWeight: '700', fontSize: 12, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
              {isSyncing ? '⏳...' : '🔄 ' + (lang === 'bn' ? 'সিঙ্ক' : 'Sync')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* 5-Sub-Tab Switcher */}
      <View style={styles.tabGrid}>
        {(
          [
            {
              id: 'agri_office',
              bn: '🏛️ কৃষি অফিস',
              en: '🏛️ Agri Offices',
            },
            {
              id: 'experts',
              bn: '👨‍🌾 অভিজ্ঞ বাগানী',
              en: '👨‍🌾 Master Gardeners',
            },
            {
              id: 'nurseries',
              bn: '🪴 নার্সারি ও চারা',
              en: '🪴 Nurseries',
            },
            {
              id: 'insights',
              bn: '💡 অভিজ্ঞতা ভল্ট',
              en: '💡 Experience Vault',
            },
            {
              id: 'community',
              bn: '💬 বাগানী আড্ডা',
              en: '💬 Community',
            },
          ] as { id: DirectoryTab; bn: string; en: string }[]
        ).map((tab) => {
          const active = activeTab === tab.id;
          return (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={tab.id}
              onPress={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)}
              style={[
                styles.tabBtn,
                {
                  backgroundColor: active
                    ? theme.primary
                    : theme.cardBackground,
                  borderColor: active ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: active ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                  fontWeight: '800',
                  fontSize: 12,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? tab.bn : tab.en}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          );
        })}
      </View>

      {/* District Filter Chips for Contact Categories */}
      {activeTab !== 'community' && activeTab !== 'insights' && (
        <ScrollView
          horizontal
          showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
          contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 8, paddingVertical: 2 }}
        >
          {[
            'ALL',
            userDistrict,
            'জামালপুর (Jamalpur)',
            'ঢাকা (Dhaka)',
            'ময়মনসিংহ (Mymensingh)',
            'রাজশাহী (Rajshahi)',
            'চট্টগ্রাম (Chattogram)',
            'সিলেট (Sylhet)',
            'বগুড়া (Bogura)',
          ]
            .filter((val, idx, arr) => arr.indexOf(val) === idx)
            .map((dist) => {
              const active = selectedDistrict === dist;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={dist}
                  onPress={() => setSelectedDistrict(dist)}
                  style={[
                    styles.distChip,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: active
                        ? theme.secondary
                        : theme.cardBackground,
                      borderColor: active ? theme.secondary : theme.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: active ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                      fontSize: 12,
                      fontWeight: '700',
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    📍{' '}
                    {dist === 'ALL'
                      ? lang === 'bn'
                        ? 'সকল জেলা (All)'
                        : 'All Districts'
                      : dist}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
        </ScrollView>
      )}

      {/* TAB 1, 2, 3: Directory Contacts List (Agri Office / Master Gardeners / Nurseries) */}
      {activeTab === 'agri_office' || activeTab === 'experts' || activeTab === 'nurseries' ? (
        <View style={{ gap: 12 }}>
          {/* Matched User District Contacts Alert Banner */}
          {matchedDistrictContacts.filter((c) => c.category === activeCategoryFilter).length > 0 &&
            selectedDistrict === 'ALL' && (
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.matchedBanner,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                    borderColor: theme.primary,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: theme.primary,
                    fontWeight: '800',
                    fontSize: 13,
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  }}
                >
                  📍 আপনার জেলা ({userDistrict})-এর ভেরিফাইড পরিচিতি:
                </Text>
              </View>
            )}

          {filteredContacts.map((item) => (
            <View
              key={item.id}
              style={[
                styles.dirCard,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <View style={styles.dirHeaderRow}>
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: theme.primary,
                    fontSize: 11.5,
                    fontWeight: '800',
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  }}
                >
                  📍 {item.district} {item.isVerified ? '• ✅ Verified' : ''}
                </Text>
              </View>

              <Text style={[styles.dirName, { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam }]}>
                {lang === 'bn' ? item.nameBn : item.nameEn}
              </Text>

              <Text style={{ color: theme.textSecondary, fontSize: 12.5, lineHeight: 18, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                🌿 {lang === 'bn' ? item.specialtyBn : item.specialtyEn}
              </Text>

              <Text style={{ color: theme.textSecondary, fontSize: 12, fontStyle: 'italic' }}>
                🏢 {item.address}
              </Text>

              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => Linking.openURL(`tel:${item.phone}`)}
                style={[
                  styles.dirCallBtn,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                    borderColor: theme.primary,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: theme.primary,
                    fontWeight: '800',
                    fontSize: 12.5,
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  }}
                >
                  📞 {lang === 'bn' ? 'সরাসরি কল করুন:' : 'Call Direct:'} {item.phone}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          ))}
        </View>
      ) : activeTab === 'insights' ? (
        /* TAB 4: GARDENER EXPERIENCE & KEY INSIGHTS COLLECTION VAULT (Requirement #8) */
        <View style={{ gap: 14 }}>
          {/* Header Action to Submit Experience */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.dirCard,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                borderColor: theme.primary,
                borderWidth: 1.5,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>
              <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 200 }}>
                <Text style={{ color: theme.primary, fontWeight: '800', fontSize: 15, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                  💡 বাগানীদের বাস্তব অভিজ্ঞতা ও ফলন ভল্ট
                </Text>
                <Text style={{ color: theme.textSecondary, fontSize: 12, lineHeight: 18, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                  {lang === 'bn'
                    ? 'অন্যান্য সফল ছাদবাগানীদের বাস্তব প্রুনিং কৌশল, কন্টেইনার মিশ্রণ ও অর্জিত ফলনের রেকর্ড।'
                    : 'Real grower breakthroughs, pruning secrets, and verified harvest yield records.'}
                </Text>
              </View>

              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => setShowAddExpModal(true)}
                style={[styles.postBtn, { backgroundColor: theme.primary }]}
              >
                <Text style={{ color: '#FFFFFF', fontWeight: '800', fontSize: 13, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                  + {lang === 'bn' ? 'নিজের অভিজ্ঞতা শেয়ার করুন' : 'Submit My Experience'}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* List of Real Experiences */}
          {experiences.map((exp) => (
            <View
              key={exp.id}
              style={[
                styles.dirCard,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <View style={styles.postHeaderRow}>
                <View>
                  <Text style={{ color: theme.textPrimary, fontWeight: '800', fontSize: 14, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                    👤 {exp.authorName} • <Text style={{ color: theme.primary }}>{exp.district}</Text>
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={{ color: theme.primary, fontWeight: '700', fontSize: 13, marginTop: 2, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                    🌱 {exp.plantName} ({exp.varietyName})
                  </Text>
                </View>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() => handleUpvoteExperience(exp.id)}
                  style={[
                    styles.upvoteBtn,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                      borderColor: theme.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text style={{ color: theme.primary, fontWeight: '700', fontSize: 12 }}>
                    👍 {exp.helpfulCount}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>

              {/* Container & Soil Used */}
              <View style={[styles.insightRow, { backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated }]}>
                <Text style={{ color: theme.textPrimary, fontWeight: '700', fontSize: 12, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                  🪴 টব ও মাটি:
                </Text>
                <Text style={{ color: theme.textSecondary, fontSize: 12.5, flex: 1, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                  {exp.containerAndSoilUsed}
                </Text>
              </View>

              {/* Key Pruning or Success Tip */}
              <View style={[styles.insightRow, { backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface, borderColor: theme.primary, borderWidth: 1 }]}>
                <Text style={{ color: theme.primary, fontWeight: '700', fontSize: 12, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                  ✂️ ফলন বাড়ানোর সিক্রেট টিপস:
                </Text>
                <Text style={{ color: theme.textPrimary, fontSize: 12.5, flex: 1, lineHeight: 18, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                  {exp.keySuccessOrPruningTip}
                </Text>
              </View>

              {/* Disease Solved if any */}
              {exp.diseaseOrChallengeSolved ? (
                <View style={[styles.insightRow, { backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated }]}>
                  <Text style={{ color: theme.warning, fontWeight: '700', fontSize: 12, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                    🩺 রোগ সমাধান:
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={{ color: theme.textSecondary, fontSize: 12.5, flex: 1, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                    {exp.diseaseOrChallengeSolved}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              ) : null}

              {/* Yield Result Summary */}
              <View style={[styles.insightRow, { backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated, borderLeftWidth: 3, borderLeftColor: theme.secondary }]}>
                <Text style={{ color: theme.secondary, fontWeight: '800', fontSize: 12, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                  🧺 অর্জিত ফলন:
                </Text>
                <Text style={{ color: theme.textPrimary, fontWeight: '700', fontSize: 12.5, flex: 1, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                  {exp.yieldResultSummary}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          ))}
        </View>
      ) : (
        /* TAB 5: Community Chat Feed */
        <View style={{ gap: 12 }}>
          {/* Compose Community Post */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.dirCard,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={{
                color: theme.textPrimary,
                fontWeight: '800',
                fontSize: 14,
                fontFamily: fontFam,
              }}
            >
              ✍️{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'স্থানীয় বাগানীদের সাথে প্রশ্ন বা চারার তথ্য শেয়ার করুন'
                : 'Share a Question or Sapling Swap with Local Gardeners'}
            </Text>
            <TextInput
              value={newPostText}
              onChangeText={setNewPostText}
              placeholder={
                lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'আপনার ছাদবাগানের আপডেট বা প্রশ্ন লিখুন...'
                  : 'Write your rooftop garden update or question...'
              }
              placeholderTextColor={theme.textSecondary}
              multiline
              style={[
                styles.postInput,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                },
              ]}
            />
            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={handleAddPost}
              style={[styles.postBtn, { backgroundColor: theme.primary }]}
            >
              <Text style={{ color: '#FFFFFF', fontWeight: '800', fontSize: 13, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                🚀 {lang === 'bn' ? 'কমিউনিটিতে পোস্ট করুন' : 'Post to Community'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          {communityPosts.map((post) => (
            <View
              key={post.id}
              style={[
                styles.dirCard,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <View style={styles.postHeaderRow}>
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: theme.textPrimary,
                    fontWeight: '800',
                    fontSize: 13,
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  }}
                >
                  👤 {post.author} •{' '}
                  <Text style={{ color: theme.primary, fontSize: 12 }}>
                    {post.district}
                  </Text>
                </Text>
                <Text style={{ color: theme.textSecondary, fontSize: 11 }}>
                  {post.timestamp}
                </Text>
              </View>
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  fontSize: 13,
                  lineHeight: 20,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                {post.message}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.secondary,
                  fontSize: 11,
                  fontWeight: '700',
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                {post.tag}
              </Text>
            </View>
          ))}
        </View>
      )}

      {/* Modal: Add My Gardener Experience Insight (Requirement #8) */}
      <Modal visible={showAddExpModal} transparent animationType="slide" onRequestClose={() => setShowAddExpModal(false)}>
        <View style={styles.modalOverlay}>
          <View style={[styles.modalCard, { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border }]}>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
              <Text style={{ color: theme.textPrimary, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '800', fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                ✍️ {lang === 'bn' ? 'বাগানির বাস্তব অভিজ্ঞতা ও টিপস যুক্ত করুন' : 'Submit Gardener Insight'}
              </Text>
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setShowAddExpModal(false)}>
                <Text style={{ color: theme.textSecondary, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '700' }}>✕</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 10, paddingVertical: 6 }}>
              <View style={{ gap: 4 }}>
                <Text style={{ color: theme.textPrimary, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '700', fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                  গাছের নাম (Plant Name) *
                </Text>
                <TextInput
                  value={expPlantName}
                  onChangeText={setExpPlantName}
                  placeholder={lang === 'bn' ? 'যেমন: কাটিমন আম, থাই সবেদা, লাউ' : 'e.g., Katimon Mango'}
                  placeholderTextColor={theme.textSecondary}
                  style={[styles.inputField, { backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated, borderColor: theme.border, color: theme.textPrimary }]}
                />
              </View>

              <View style={{ gap: 4 }}>
                <Text style={{ color: theme.textPrimary, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '700', fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                  জাত / ভ্যারাইটি (Variety)
                </Text>
                <TextInput
                  value={expVariety}
                  onChangeText={setExpVariety}
                  placeholder={lang === 'bn' ? 'যেমন: থাই কাটিমন-১২, বারি লাউ-৪' : 'e.g., Thai Katimon-12'}
                  placeholderTextColor={theme.textSecondary}
                  style={[styles.inputField, { backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated, borderColor: theme.border, color: theme.textPrimary }]}
                />
              </View>

              <View style={{ gap: 4 }}>
                <Text style={{ color: theme.textPrimary, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '700', fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                  ব্যবহৃত টব ও মাটির মিশ্রণ (Container & Soil Mix)
                </Text>
                <TextInput
                  value={expContainerSoil}
                  onChangeText={setExpContainerSoil}
                  placeholder={lang === 'bn' ? 'যেমন: ২৪ ইঞ্চি জিও ব্যাগ, ৫০% দোআঁশ + ভার্মিকম্পোস্ট' : 'e.g., 24" Geo-Bag, Vermicompost'}
                  placeholderTextColor={theme.textSecondary}
                  style={[styles.inputField, { backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated, borderColor: theme.border, color: theme.textPrimary }]}
                />
              </View>

              <View style={{ gap: 4 }}>
                <Text style={{ color: theme.primary, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '800', fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                  ফলন বাড়ানোর প্রুনিং বা বিশেষ সার কৌশল * (Success Secret)
                </Text>
                <TextInput
                  value={expPruningTip}
                  onChangeText={setExpPruningTip}
                  multiline
                  placeholder={lang === 'bn' ? 'কীভাবে ডাল কেটেছিলেন বা সার দিয়েছিলেন যার ফলে ফলন দ্বিগুণ হয়েছে?' : 'How did you prune or feed to achieve high yield?'}
                  placeholderTextColor={theme.textSecondary}
                  style={[styles.inputField, { minHeight: 65, backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated, borderColor: theme.border, color: theme.textPrimary }]}
                />
              </View>

              <View style={{ gap: 4 }}>
                <Text style={{ color: theme.textPrimary, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '700', fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                  কী রোগ বা সমস্যা সমাধান করেছিলেন? (Disease Challenge Solved)
                </Text>
                <TextInput
                  value={expDiseaseSolved}
                  onChangeText={setExpDiseaseSolved}
                  placeholder={lang === 'bn' ? 'যেমন: মিলিবাগ নিম তেল স্প্রে করে সমাধান' : 'e.g., Solved mealybug with neem oil'}
                  placeholderTextColor={theme.textSecondary}
                  style={[styles.inputField, { backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated, borderColor: theme.border, color: theme.textPrimary }]}
                />
              </View>

              <View style={{ gap: 4 }}>
                <Text style={{ color: theme.secondary, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '800', fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                  অর্জিত ফলন / ফলাফল * (Harvest Yield Result)
                </Text>
                <TextInput
                  value={expYieldSummary}
                  onChangeText={setExpYieldSummary}
                  placeholder={lang === 'bn' ? 'যেমন: ১টি গাছ থেকে ১৮ কেজি মিষ্টি আম পেয়েছি' : 'e.g., Harvested 18 kg sweet mangoes'}
                  placeholderTextColor={theme.textSecondary}
                  style={[styles.inputField, { backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated, borderColor: theme.border, color: theme.textPrimary }]}
                />
              </View>

              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={handleSubmitNewExperience}
                style={[styles.postBtn, { backgroundColor: theme.primary, marginTop: 8 }]}
              >
                <Text style={{ color: '#FFFFFF', fontWeight: '800', fontSize: 13, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                  ✓ {lang === 'bn' ? 'ভল্টে সংরক্ষণ করুন' : 'Save to Insights Vault'}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </ScrollView>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </ScrollView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    width: '100%',

/**
 * BAGANI — Guide & Weather Care Hub Screen (Sections 8 & 9 of DESIGN_SYSTEM.md)
 * Combines:
 * 1. Smart Rooftop Garden Planner & 6-Stage Practical Lifecycle Guide
 * 2. Live Weather Dashboard & Automated Plant Care Recommendations (Screens 11 & 12)
 * 3. District Agriculture Directory & Local Gardener Community
 */

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { AppLanguage } from '../../core/l10n/translations';
import {
  APP_TYPOGRAPHY,
  AppThemeColors,
  DESIGN_TOKENS,
} from '../../core/theme/app_theme';
import { GranularCareTask } from '../../models/care_schedule_model';
import { PlantModel } from '../../models/plant_model';
import { DirectoryScreen } from '../directory/directory_screen';
import { PlannerScreen } from '../planner/planner_screen';
import { WeatherCareScreen } from '../weather/weather_care_screen';

export type GuideSubTab = 'planner' | 'weather' | 'directory';

interface GuideScreenProps {
  theme: AppThemeColors;
  lang: AppLanguage;
  userDistrict: string;
  userName: string;
  plants: PlantModel[];
  careTasks: GranularCareTask[];
  onToggleTaskCheckbox: (task: GranularCareTask) => void;
  onSelectPlant?: (plant: PlantModel) => void;
  initialSubTab?: GuideSubTab;
}

export const GuideScreen: React.FC<GuideScreenProps> = ({
  theme,
  lang,
  userDistrict,
  userName,
  plants,
  careTasks,
  onToggleTaskCheckbox,
  onSelectPlant,
  initialSubTab = 'planner',
}) => {
  const [subTab, setSubTab] = useState<GuideSubTab>(initialSubTab);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSubTab(initialSubTab);
  }, [initialSubTab]);

  const fontFam =
    lang === 'bn'
      ? APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyBengali
      : APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyEnglish;

  return (
    <View style={styles.wrapper}>
      {/* Top Segmented Switcher */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.switcherOuter,
          { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderBottomColor: theme.border },
        ]}
      >
        <View
          style={[
            styles.switcherBar,
            {
              backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
              borderColor: theme.border,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => setSubTab('planner')}
            activeOpacity={0.82}
            style={[
              styles.segmentBtn,
              {
                backgroundColor:
                  subTab === 'planner' ? theme.primary : 'transparent',
                borderColor:
                  subTab === 'planner' ? theme.primary : 'transparent',
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              numberOfLines={1}
              style={{
                color: subTab === 'planner' ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textSecondary,
                fontWeight: '700',
                fontSize: 13,
                fontFamily: fontFam,
              }}
            >
              📐 {lang === 'bn' ? 'ছাদবাগান প্ল্যানার' : 'Rooftop Planner'}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => setSubTab('weather')}
            activeOpacity={0.82}
            style={[
              styles.segmentBtn,
              {
                backgroundColor:
                  subTab === 'weather' ? theme.primary : 'transparent',
                borderColor:
                  subTab === 'weather' ? theme.primary : 'transparent',
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              numberOfLines={1}
              style={{
                color: subTab === 'weather' ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textSecondary,
                fontWeight: '700',
                fontSize: 13,
                fontFamily: fontFam,
              }}
            >
              🌦️ {lang === 'bn' ? 'আবহাওয়া ও যত্ন' : 'Weather & Care'}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => setSubTab('directory')}
            activeOpacity={0.82}
            style={[
              styles.segmentBtn,
              {
                backgroundColor:
                  subTab === 'directory' ? theme.primary : 'transparent',
                borderColor:
                  subTab === 'directory' ? theme.primary : 'transparent',
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              numberOfLines={1}
              style={{
                color:
                  subTab === 'directory' ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textSecondary,
                fontWeight: '700',
                fontSize: 13,
                fontFamily: fontFam,
              }}
            >
              🏛️ {lang === 'bn' ? 'কৃষি ডিরেক্টরি' : 'Agri Directory'}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>

      <View style={{ flex: 1, width: '100%' }}>
        {subTab === 'planner' ? (
          <PlannerScreen theme={theme} lang={lang} />
        ) : subTab === 'weather' ? (
          <WeatherCareScreen
            theme={theme}
            lang={lang}
            userDistrict={userDistrict}
            plants={plants}
            careTasks={careTasks}
            onToggleTaskCheckbox={onToggleTaskCheckbox}
            onSelectPlant={onSelectPlant}
          />
        ) : (
          <DirectoryScreen
            theme={theme}
            lang={lang}
            userDistrict={userDistrict}
            userName={userName}
          />
        )}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  wrapper: {
    flex: 1,
    width: '100%',
  },
  switcherOuter: {
    width: '100%',
    alignItems: 'center',
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
    paddingHorizontal: DESIGN_TOKENS.screenHorizontalPadding,
    paddingVertical: 8,
  },
  switcherBar: {
    width: '100%',
    maxWidth: 960,
    flexDirection: 'row',
    flexWrap: 'wrap',
    padding: 5,
    borderRadius: 14,
    borderWidth: 1,
    gap: 6,
  },
  segmentBtn: {
    flex: 1,
    minWidth: 110,
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    gap: 6,
    minHeight: 40,
    borderRadius: 10,
    borderWidth: 1,
    paddingHorizontal: 10,
  },
});
