/**
 * BAGANI — Home Dashboard & Weather-Based Care Screen
 * Preserves 100% of all core BAGANI features while using the Brand Color System (#167A45, #20A464, #E7F4EB):
 * - Personalized Welcome Hero, Avatar, Quote, Dual Date (Bengali বঙ্গাব্দ + English) & Time
 * - Live Weather Widget (Tap to expand Past 7 Days + Today + Next 7 Days Forecast + Plant Watering Prescriptions)
 * - Spacious 2x2 Garden Statistics Grid (single-line labels, zero wrapping/overflow)
 * - Urgent Care Notification Cards with 1-Tap Checkbox (✓) that logs directly to Plant Timeline
 * - Quick Action Shortcuts (+ Register New Plant, Scan QR Tag, AI Disease Finder)
 * - Filterable Grid View & List View of Registered Plants with "View Current & Future Status →"
 * - Recent Plant Activities
 */

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Image,
  Modal,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { APP_CONFIG } from '../../core/constants/app_constants';
import { AppLanguage, TRANSLATIONS } from '../../core/l10n/translations';
import { AddPlantModal } from '../../core/components/add_plant_modal';
import { BotanicalSearchModal } from '../../core/components/botanical_search_modal';
import {
  APP_TYPOGRAPHY,
  AppThemeColors,
  DESIGN_TOKENS,
} from '../../core/theme/app_theme';
import {
  getDynamicGreeting,
  getFormattedDualDate,
  toBengaliNumber,
} from '../../core/utils/date_utils';
import {
  CareTaskStatus,
  GranularCareTask,
} from '../../models/care_schedule_model';
import { PlantTimelineLog } from '../../models/plant_log_model';
import { PlantModel } from '../../models/plant_model';
import { UserModel } from '../../models/user_model';
import { LocalDbService } from '../../services/local_db_service';
import { NotificationService } from '../../services/notification_service';
import { WeatherService } from '../../services/weather_service';

interface HomeScreenProps {
  user: UserModel;
  plants: PlantModel[];
  careTasks: GranularCareTask[];
  theme: AppThemeColors;
  lang: AppLanguage;
  onSelectPlant: (plant: PlantModel) => void;
  onToggleTaskCheckbox: (task: GranularCareTask) => void;
  onSaveTaskStatus?: (
    task: GranularCareTask,
    nextStatus: CareTaskStatus
  ) => void;
  onAddCareTask?: (task: GranularCareTask) => void;
  onAddNewPlant: (plant: PlantModel) => void;
  onOpenQrScanner: () => void;
  onOpenAiDiagnosis?: () => void;
  onOpenMyGarden?: () => void;
  onOpenWeatherDashboard?: () => void;
}

export const HomeScreen: React.FC<HomeScreenProps> = ({
  user,
  plants,
  careTasks,
  theme,
  lang,
  onSelectPlant,
  onToggleTaskCheckbox,
  onSaveTaskStatus,
  onAddCareTask,
  onAddNewPlant,
  onOpenQrScanner,
  onOpenAiDiagnosis,
  onOpenWeatherDashboard,
}) => {
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'grid' | 'list'>('grid');
  const [weatherExpanded, setWeatherExpanded] = useState(false);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [addModalOpen, setAddModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [botanicalSearchOpen, setBotanicalSearchOpen] = useState(false);
  const [recentLogs, setRecentLogs] = useState<PlantTimelineLog[]>([]);

  // Urgent Care Selection & Status Draft Map (taskId -> 'completed' | 'do_later')
  const [selectedTaskDrafts, setSelectedTaskDrafts] = useState<
    Record<string, CareTaskStatus>
  >({});
  const [taskFilter, setTaskFilter] = useState<
    'all' | 'pending' | 'do_later' | 'completed'
  >('all');
  const [showAddTaskRow, setShowAddTaskRow] = useState(false);
  const [newTaskNote, setNewTaskNote] = useState('');
  const [newTaskLiters, setNewTaskLiters] = useState('2.0');

  // New Plant Form state
  const [newNameBn, setNewNameBn] = useState('');
  const [newVariety, setNewVariety] = useState('');
  const [newBagSize, setNewBagSize] = useState('20');
  const [newIsAllSeason, setNewIsAllSeason] = useState(true);

  const t = TRANSLATIONS[lang];
  const { englishDate, bengaliDate, timeString } = getFormattedDualDate();
  const greeting = getDynamicGreeting(lang);
  const [weatherWindow, setWeatherWindow] = useState(() =>
    LocalDbService.get15DayWeatherWindow()
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    WeatherService.fetchLive15DayWeather(user.district).then((res) => {
      if (active) {
        setWeatherWindow({
          past7Days: res.past7Days,
          today: res.today,
          next7Days: res.next7Days,
        });
      }
    });
    LocalDbService.getTimelineLogs().then((logs) => {
      if (active) setRecentLogs(logs.slice(0, 4));
    });
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [user.district, plants, careTasks]);

  const fontFam =
    lang === 'bn'
      ? APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyBengali
      : APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyEnglish;

  const filteredPlants = plants.filter(
    (p) =>
      p.nameBengali.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      p.nameEnglish.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      p.variety.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      p.qrCodeId.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())
  );

  const totalPlants = plants.length;
  const healthyPlants = plants.filter((p) => p.healthState === 'healthy').length;
  const attentionCount = plants.filter((p) => p.healthState !== 'healthy').length;
  const avgMoisture =
    totalPlants > 0
      ? Math.round(
          plants.reduce((acc, p) => acc + p.estimatedMoisturePercent, 0) /
            totalPlants
        )
      : 0;

  const handleCreatePlant = () => {
    const cleanName = newNameBn.trim() || 'ব্যানানা ম্যাঙ্গো (আম)';
    const cleanVar = newVariety.trim() || 'থাই বারোমাসি কলম';
    const qrId = 'BAGANI-QR-' + (1000 + plants.length + 1);

    const created: PlantModel = {
      id: 'plt_' + Date.now().toString(36),
      qrCodeId: qrId,
      nameBengali: cleanName,
      nameEnglish: cleanName,
      variety: cleanVar,
      category: 'fruit',
      isAllSeason: newIsAllSeason,
      plantedDateIso: new Date().toISOString(),
      containerSizeInches: Number(newBagSize) || 20,
      rooftopZone: `ছাদ বাগান সারি - টব #${plants.length + 1}`,
      healthState: 'healthy',
      lastHealthDiagnosisSummary: 'নতুন রেজিস্টার্ড চারা — সুস্থ অবস্থা',
      lastWateredIso: new Date().toISOString(),
      lastFertilizedIso: new Date().toISOString(),
      estimatedMoisturePercent: 75,
      qrTagConfig: {
        qrCodeValue: qrId,
        tagSize: 'medium',
        plantName: cleanName,
        varietyName: cleanVar,
        isAllSeason: newIsAllSeason,
        customQuoteBengali: 'গাছ লাগান, পরিবেশ বাঁচান',
        ownerName: user.displayName,
        ownerAddressOrCode: `${user.district} • ${qrId}`,
      },
    };
    onAddNewPlant(created);
    setNewNameBn('');
    setNewVariety('');
    setAddModalOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <ScrollView
      showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
      contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollOuter}
    >
      <View style={styles.container}>
        {/* 1. User Greeting, Quote & Dual Bengali/English Date Hero Card */}
        <View
          style={[
            styles.card,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={{ gap: 10 }}>
            {/* 1. Greeting positioned at the very top with 24px font size (Correction #1) */}
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.greetingTopLabel,
                { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              👋 {greeting}
            </Text>

            {/* 2. User info displayed right below greetings, exactly matching the user's profile page (Correction #2) */}
            <View style={styles.heroProfileRow}>
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.avatarBox,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                    borderColor: theme.primary,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                {user.photoUrl ? (
                  <Image
                    source={{ uri: user.photoUrl }}
                    style={styles.avatarImg}
                  />
                ) : (
                  <Image
                    source={require('../../../assets/images/logo.png')}
                    style={{ width: 54, height: 44 }}
                    resizeMode="contain"
                  />
                )}
              </View>

              <View style={styles.heroTextCol}>
                <Text
                  style={[
                    styles.greetingText,
                    { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                  ]}
                >
                  {user.displayName}
                </Text>
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: theme.textSecondary,
                    fontSize: 13,
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  }}
                >
                  📧 {user.email}
                </Text>
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: theme.primary,
                    fontSize: 13,
                    fontWeight: '600',
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  }}
                >
                  📍 {user.district}
                  {user.address ? ` • ${user.address}` : ''}
                  {user.organizationOrProfession
                    ? ` • ${user.organizationOrProfession}`
                    : ` • ${APP_CONFIG.developerName}`}
                </Text>
                <Text
                  style={[
                    styles.quoteText,
                    { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                  ]}
                >
                  {t.inspQuote}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Dual Date (Bengali + English) & Live District Weather Bar */}
          <TouchableOpacity
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            onPress={() => setWeatherExpanded(!weatherExpanded)}
            style={[
              styles.weatherSummaryBar,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.dateText,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              📅 {bengaliDate}  •  {englishDate}  •  ⏰ {timeString}
            </Text>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.weatherLiveText,
                { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              ☀️ {user.district}: {weatherWindow.today.tempCelsius}°C (আর্দ্রতা{' '}
              {weatherWindow.today.humidityPercent}% • বৃষ্টি{' '}
              {weatherWindow.today.rainProbabilityPercent}%) —{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'গত ৭ দিন ও আগামী ৭ দিনের পূর্বাভাস ▾'
                : 'Tap for 15-Day Weather & Care ▾'}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          {/* Expandable 15-Day Weather Window + Plant Watering Prescriptions */}
          {weatherExpanded && (
            <View
              style={[
                styles.weatherExpandBox,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.weatherGroupTitle,
                  { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                ⏪{' '}
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'গত ৭ দিনের আবহাওয়া রেকর্ড:'
                  : 'Past 7 Days Weather:'}
              </Text>
              <ScrollView
                horizontal
                showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
                contentContainerStyle={styles.weatherStrip}
              >
                {weatherWindow.past7Days.map((w, i) => (
                  <View
                    key={i}
                    style={[
                      styles.weatherPill,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: theme.surface,
                        borderColor: theme.border,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[styles.wDay, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}
                    >
                      {lang === 'bn' ? w.dayLabelBengali : w.dayLabelEnglish}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.wTemp, { color: theme.textPrimary }]}>
                      {w.condition === 'rainy' ? '🌧️' : '☀️'} {w.tempCelsius}°C
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                ))}
              </ScrollView>

              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.weatherGroupTitle,
                  { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam, marginTop: 6 },
                ]}
              >
                ⏩{' '}
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'আগামী ৭ দিনের আবহাওয়া পূর্বাভাস:'
                  : 'Upcoming 7 Days Forecast:'}
              </Text>
              <ScrollView
                horizontal
                showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
                contentContainerStyle={styles.weatherStrip}
              >
                {weatherWindow.next7Days.map((w, i) => (
                  <View
                    key={i}
                    style={[
                      styles.weatherPill,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: theme.surface,
                        borderColor: theme.primary,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text style={[styles.wDay, { color: theme.textPrimary }]}>
                      {lang === 'bn' ? w.dayLabelBengali : w.dayLabelEnglish}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.wTemp, { color: theme.primary }]}>
                      {w.condition === 'rainy' ? '🌧️' : '☀️'} {w.tempCelsius}°C
                      (💧{w.rainProbabilityPercent}%)
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                ))}
              </ScrollView>

              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.weatherGroupTitle,
                  {
                    color: theme.textPrimary,
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                    marginTop: 6,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                💧{' '}
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'গাছ অনুযায়ী আজকের সেচ পরামর্শ:'
                  : 'Plant-Specific Watering Recommendations:'}
              </Text>
              {plants.slice(0, 3).map((p) => {
                const presc = NotificationService.computeWeatherPrescription(
                  p,
                  weatherWindow.today
                );
                return (
                  <View
                    key={p.id}
                    style={[
                      styles.prescRow,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: theme.surface,
                        borderColor: theme.border,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={{
                        color: theme.textPrimary,
                        fontWeight: '600',
                        fontSize: 13,
                        fontFamily: fontFam,
                      }}
                    >
                      🌱 {lang === 'bn' ? p.nameBengali : p.nameEnglish} (
                      {p.containerSizeInches}" Bag)
                    </Text>
                    <Text
                      style={{
                        color: theme.textSecondary,
                        fontSize: 12.5,
                        fontFamily: fontFam,
                      }}
                    >
                      {lang === 'bn' ? presc.advisoryBn : presc.advisoryEn}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                );
              })}

              {onOpenWeatherDashboard && (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={onOpenWeatherDashboard}
                  style={{
                    marginTop: 6,
                    paddingVertical: 10,
                    paddingHorizontal: 14,
                    borderRadius: 10,
                    backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                    borderWidth: 1,
                    borderColor: theme.primary,
                    alignItems: 'center',
                  }}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: theme.primary,
                      fontWeight: '700',
                      fontSize: 13,
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    🌦️{' '}
                    {lang === 'bn'
                      ? 'পূর্ণাঙ্গ আবহাওয়া ও পরিচর্যা ড্যাশবোর্ড খুলুন →'
                      : 'Open Full Weather & Care Dashboard →'}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              )}
            </View>
          )}
        </View>

        {/* 2. Spacious 2x2 Garden Statistics Grid (Single-Line Labels, Zero Overflow) */}
        <View style={styles.statsGrid}>
          <View
            style={[
              styles.statCard,
              { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
            ]}
          >
            <View
              style={[
                styles.statIconWrap,
                { backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={styles.statIconText}>🌳</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.statTextCol}>
              <Text style={[styles.statNumber, { color: theme.textPrimary }]}>
                {lang === 'bn' ? toBengaliNumber(totalPlants) : totalPlants}
              </Text>
              <Text
                numberOfLines={1}
                style={[
                  styles.statLabel,
                  { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'মোট গাছ' : 'Total Plants'}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          <View
            style={[
              styles.statCard,
              { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
            ]}
          >
            <View
              style={[
                styles.statIconWrap,
                { backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={styles.statIconText}>✅</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.statTextCol}>
              <Text style={[styles.statNumber, { color: theme.primary }]}>
                {lang === 'bn' ? toBengaliNumber(healthyPlants) : healthyPlants}
              </Text>
              <Text
                numberOfLines={1}
                style={[
                  styles.statLabel,
                  { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'সুস্থ গাছ' : 'Healthy Plants'}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          <View
            style={[
              styles.statCard,
              { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
            ]}
          >
            <View
              style={[
                styles.statIconWrap,
                { backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={styles.statIconText}>⚠️</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.statTextCol}>
              <Text style={[styles.statNumber, { color: theme.warning }]}>
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? toBengaliNumber(attentionCount)
                  : attentionCount}
              </Text>
              <Text
                numberOfLines={1}
                style={[
                  styles.statLabel,
                  { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'যত্ন প্রয়োজন' : 'Needs Care'}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          <View
            style={[
              styles.statCard,
              { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
            ]}
          >
            <View
              style={[
                styles.statIconWrap,
                { backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={styles.statIconText}>💧</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.statTextCol}>
              <Text style={[styles.statNumber, { color: theme.secondary }]}>
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? `${toBengaliNumber(avgMoisture)}%`
                  : `${avgMoisture}%`}
              </Text>
              <Text
                numberOfLines={1}
                style={[
                  styles.statLabel,
                  { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'গড় আর্দ্রতা' : 'Avg Moisture'}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* 3. Urgent Care Notification & Interactive Selection with Right-Side Save Button */}
        <View
          style={[
            styles.card,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <View
            style={{
              flexDirection: 'row',
              flexWrap: 'wrap',
              justifyContent: 'space-between',
              alignItems: 'center',
              gap: 8,
            }}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.sectionHeaderTitle,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              🔔 {t.urgentCareAlertTitle}
            </Text>

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => setShowAddTaskRow(!showAddTaskRow)}
              style={{
                paddingHorizontal: 12,
                paddingVertical: 6,
                borderRadius: 10,
                backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderColor: theme.primary,
              }}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.primary,
                  fontSize: 12,
                  fontWeight: '700',
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                {showAddTaskRow
                  ? lang === 'bn'
                    ? '✕ বন্ধ করুন'
                    : '✕ Close'
                  : lang === 'bn'
                  ? '+ নতুন কাজ যুক্ত করুন'
                  : '+ Add Care Task'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          {/* Quick Add Care Task Form */}
          {showAddTaskRow && (
            <View
              style={{
                padding: 12,
                borderRadius: 12,
                backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderColor: theme.border,
                gap: 8,
              }}
            >
              <TextInput
                value={newTaskNote}
                onChangeText={setNewTaskNote}
                placeholder={
                  lang === 'bn'
                    ? 'পরিচর্যার বিবরণ লিখুন (যেমন: বিকেলে জৈব সার ও ২ লিটার পানি)...'
                    : 'Enter care instruction (e.g., Apply organic compost & 2L water)...'
                }
                placeholderTextColor={theme.textSecondary}
                style={[
                  styles.searchInput,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.surface,
                    color: theme.textPrimary,
                    borderColor: theme.border,
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  },
                ]}
              />
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
                <TextInput
                  value={newTaskLiters}
                  onChangeText={setNewTaskLiters}
                  keyboardType="numeric"
                  placeholder="2.0"
                  placeholderTextColor={theme.textSecondary}
                  style={[
                    styles.searchInput,
                    {
                      width: 90,
                      backgroundColor: theme.surface,
                      color: theme.textPrimary,
                      borderColor: theme.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                />
                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() => {
                    const targetPlant = plants[0];
                    if (!targetPlant) return;
                    const createdTask: GranularCareTask = {
                      id: 'tsk_usr_' + Date.now().toString(36),
                      plantId: targetPlant.id,
                      plantNameBengali: targetPlant.nameBengali,
                      plantNameEnglish: targetPlant.nameEnglish,
                      dueDateIso: new Date().toISOString(),
                      isUrgentNext3Days: true,
                      recommendedTimeSlot: 'morning',
                      waterAmountLiters: Number(newTaskLiters) || 2.0,
                      fertilizerOrSprayInstructionBengali:
                        newTaskNote.trim() ||
                        'সকালে পরিমিত পানি ও জৈব পরিচর্যা প্রদান করুন।',
                      fertilizerOrSprayInstructionEnglish:
                        newTaskNote.trim() ||
                        'Apply scheduled morning water and organic care.',
                      isCompleted: false,
                      status: 'pending',
                    };
                    if (onAddCareTask) {
                      onAddCareTask(createdTask);
                    }
                    setNewTaskNote('');
                    setShowAddTaskRow(false);
                  }}
                  style={{
                    flex: 1,
                    backgroundColor: theme.primary,
                    borderRadius: 10,
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                    minHeight: 42,
                  }}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: '#FFFFFF',
                      fontWeight: '700',
                      fontSize: 13,
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    {lang === 'bn'
                      ? '✅ তালিকায় যুক্ত করুন'
                      : '✅ Save New Care Task'}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            </View>
          )}

          {/* Filter Tabs: All | Pending | Do Later | Completed */}
          <View
            style={{
              flexDirection: 'row',
              flexWrap: 'wrap',
              gap: 6,
            }}
          >
            {(
              [
                {
                  id: 'all',
                  bn: `সব (${careTasks.length})`,
                  en: `All (${careTasks.length})`,
                },
                {
                  id: 'pending',
                  bn: `বাকি (${
                    careTasks.filter(
                      (x) => !x.isCompleted && x.status !== 'do_later'
                    ).length
                  })`,
                  en: `Pending (${
                    careTasks.filter(
                      (x) => !x.isCompleted && x.status !== 'do_later'
                    ).length
                  })`,
                },
                {
                  id: 'do_later',
                  bn: `পরে করব (${
                    careTasks.filter((x) => x.status === 'do_later').length
                  })`,
                  en: `Do Later (${
                    careTasks.filter((x) => x.status === 'do_later').length
                  })`,
                },
                {
                  id: 'completed',
                  bn: `সম্পন্ন (${
                    careTasks.filter(
                      (x) => x.isCompleted || x.status === 'completed'
                    ).length
                  })`,
                  en: `Completed (${
                    careTasks.filter(
                      (x) => x.isCompleted || x.status === 'completed'
                    ).length
                  })`,
                },
              ] as const
            ).map((tab) => {
              const active = taskFilter === tab.id;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={tab.id}
                  onPress={() => setTaskFilter(tab.id)}
                  style={{
                    paddingHorizontal: 10,
                    paddingVertical: 6,
                    borderRadius: 8,
                    borderWidth: 1,
                    backgroundColor: active
                      ? theme.primary
                      : theme.surfaceElevated,
                    borderColor: active ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                  }}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: active ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textSecondary,
                      fontSize: 12,
                      fontWeight: '700',
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    {lang === 'bn' ? tab.bn : tab.en}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
          </View>

          {careTasks
            .filter((task) => {
              const effectiveStatus: CareTaskStatus = task.isCompleted
                ? 'completed'
                : task.status || 'pending';
              if (taskFilter === 'pending')
                return effectiveStatus === 'pending';
              if (taskFilter === 'do_later')
                return effectiveStatus === 'do_later';
              if (taskFilter === 'completed')
                return effectiveStatus === 'completed';
              return true;
            })
            .map((task) => {
              const isSelected = task.id in selectedTaskDrafts;
              const draftChoice =
                selectedTaskDrafts[task.id] ||
                (task.isCompleted ? 'completed' : 'completed');
              const effectiveStatus: CareTaskStatus = task.isCompleted
                ? 'completed'
                : task.status || 'pending';

              return (
                <View
                  key={task.id}
                  style={[
                    styles.taskItem,
                    {
                      flexDirection: 'column',
                      alignItems: 'stretch',
                      backgroundColor:
                        effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                          ? theme.selectedSurface
                          : theme.surfaceElevated,
                      borderColor: isSelected
                        ? theme.primary
                        : effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                        ? theme.primary
                        : theme.border,
                      borderWidth: isSelected ? 1.5 : 1,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <View
                    style={{
                      flexDirection: 'row',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      gap: 10,
                    }}
                  >
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      activeOpacity={0.8}
                      onPress={() => {
                        setSelectedTaskDrafts((prev) => {
                          const next = { ...prev };
                          if (task.id in next) {
                            delete next[task.id];
                          } else {
                            next[task.id] =
                              effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                                ? 'do_later'
                                : 'completed';
                          }
                          return next;
                        });
                      }}
                      style={{
                        flex: 1,
                        flexDirection: 'row',
                        alignItems: 'flex-start',
                        gap: 10,
                      }}
                    >
                      <View
                        style={[
                          styles.checkboxBox,
                          {
                            backgroundColor:
                              isSelected || effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                                ? theme.primary
                                : theme.surface,
                            borderColor: theme.primary,
                          },
                        ]}
                      >
                        <Text style={styles.checkboxMark}>
                          {isSelected || effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                            ? '✓'
                            : ''}
                        </Text>
                      </View>

                      <View style={styles.taskTextCol}>
                        <Text
                          style={[
                            styles.taskTitle,
                            {
                              color: theme.textPrimary,
                              textDecorationLine:
                                effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                                  ? 'line-through'
                                  : 'none',
                              fontFamily: fontFam,
                            },
                          ]}
                        >
                          🌱{' '}
                          {lang === 'bn'
                            ? task.plantNameBengali
                            : task.plantNameEnglish}{' '}
                          • 💧 {task.waterAmountLiters}L (
                          {task.recommendedTimeSlot})
                        </Text>
                        <Text
                          style={[
                            styles.taskSub,
                            { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                          ]}
                        >
                          {lang === 'bn'
                            ? task.fertilizerOrSprayInstructionBengali
                            : task.fertilizerOrSprayInstructionEnglish}
                        </Text>
                      </View>
                    </TouchableOpacity>

                    {/* Right-Hand Side: Status Badge OR "Save" Button when selected */}
                    {isSelected ? (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        activeOpacity={0.85}
                        onPress={() => {
                          if (onSaveTaskStatus) {
                            onSaveTaskStatus(task, draftChoice);
                          } else {
                            onToggleTaskCheckbox(task);
                          }
                          setSelectedTaskDrafts((prev) => {
                            const next = { ...prev };
                            delete next[task.id];
                            return next;
                          });
                        }}
                        style={{
                          backgroundColor: theme.primary,
                          paddingHorizontal: 14,
                          paddingVertical: 9,
                          borderRadius: 10,
                          alignItems: 'center',
                          justifyContent: 'center',
                          flexShrink: 0,
                        }}
                      >
                        <Text
                          style={{
                            color: '#FFFFFF',
                            fontWeight: '700',
                            fontSize: 13,
                            fontFamily: fontFam,
                          }}
                        >
                          💾 {lang === 'bn' ? 'সেভ করুন' : 'Save'}
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    ) : (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        onPress={() => {
                          setSelectedTaskDrafts((prev) => ({
                            ...prev,
                            [task.id]:
                              effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                                ? 'do_later'
                                : 'completed',
                          }));
                        }}
                        style={{
                          paddingHorizontal: 10,
                          paddingVertical: 6,
                          borderRadius: 8,
                          backgroundColor:
                            effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                              ? theme.primary
                              : effectiveStatus === 'do_later'
                              ? 'rgba(217, 119, 6, 0.14)'
                              : theme.surface,
                          borderWidth: 1,
                          borderColor:
                            effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                              ? theme.primary
                              : effectiveStatus === 'do_later'
                              ? theme.warning
                              : theme.border,
                          flexShrink: 0,
                        }}
                      >
                        <Text
                          style={{
                            color:
                              effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                                ? '#FFFFFF'
                                : effectiveStatus === 'do_later'
                                ? theme.warning
                                : theme.primary,
                            fontSize: 11.5,
                            fontWeight: '700',
                            fontFamily: fontFam,
                          }}
                        >
                          {effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                            ? lang === 'bn'
                              ? '✓ সম্পন্ন'
                              : '✓ Completed'
                            : effectiveStatus === 'do_later'
                            ? lang === 'bn'
                              ? '⏰ পরে করব'
                              : '⏰ Do Later'
                            : lang === 'bn'
                            ? 'নির্বাচন করুন'
                            : 'Select'}
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    )}
                  </View>

                  {/* Action Choice Bar when Selected: "Already Completed" vs "Do It Later" */}
                  {isSelected && (
                    <View
                      style={{
                        flexDirection: 'row',
                        flexWrap: 'wrap',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        gap: 8,
                        marginTop: 8,
                        paddingTop: 8,
                        borderTopWidth: 1,
                        borderTopColor: theme.border,
                      }}
                    >
                      <Text
                        style={{
                          color: theme.textSecondary,
                          fontSize: 12,
                          fontWeight: '600',
                          fontFamily: fontFam,
                        }}
                      >
                        {lang === 'bn' ? 'অবস্থা নির্বাচন:' : 'Choose Action:'}
                      </Text>

                      <TouchableOpacity
                        onPress={() =>
                          setSelectedTaskDrafts((prev) => ({
                            ...prev,
                            [task.id]: 'completed',
                          }))
                        }
                        style={{
                          paddingHorizontal: 12,
                          paddingVertical: 6,
                          borderRadius: 8,
                          borderWidth: 1,
                          backgroundColor:
                            draftChoice === 'completed'
                              ? theme.primary
                              : theme.surface,
                          borderColor:
                            draftChoice === 'completed'
                              ? theme.primary
                              : theme.border,
                        }}
                      >
                        <Text
                          style={{
                            color:
                              draftChoice === 'completed'
                                ? '#FFFFFF'
                                : theme.textPrimary,
                            fontSize: 12,
                            fontWeight: '700',
                            fontFamily: fontFam,
                          }}
                        >
                          ✓{' '}
                          {lang === 'bn'
                            ? 'সম্পন্ন করেছি (Completed)'
                            : 'Already Completed'}
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>

                      <TouchableOpacity
                        onPress={() =>
                          setSelectedTaskDrafts((prev) => ({
                            ...prev,
                            [task.id]: 'do_later',
                          }))
                        }
                        style={{
                          paddingHorizontal: 12,
                          paddingVertical: 6,
                          borderRadius: 8,
                          borderWidth: 1,
                          backgroundColor:
                            draftChoice === 'do_later'
                              ? theme.warning
                              : theme.surface,
                          borderColor:
                            draftChoice === 'do_later'
                              ? theme.warning
                              : theme.border,
                        }}
                      >
                        <Text
                          style={{
                            color:
                              draftChoice === 'do_later'
                                ? '#FFFFFF'
                                : theme.textPrimary,
                            fontSize: 12,
                            fontWeight: '700',
                            fontFamily: fontFam,
                          }}
                        >
                          ⏰{' '}
                          {lang === 'bn'
                            ? 'পরে করব (Do Later)'
                            : 'Do It Later'}
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    </View>
                  )}
                </View>
              );
            })}
        </View>

        {/* 4. Quick Action Shortcuts: + Add Plant, Plant QR, AI Diagnosis */}
        <View style={styles.shortcutRow}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            onPress={() => setAddModalOpen(true)}
            style={[
              styles.shortcutBtnPrimary,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.primary,
                flexDirection: 'row',
                gap: 8,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <View
              style={{
                width: 24,
                height: 24,
                borderRadius: 12,
                backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.22)',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
              }}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: '#FFFFFF',
                  fontSize: 16,
                  fontWeight: '800',
                  lineHeight: 18,
                }}
              >
                +
              </Text>
            </View>
            <Text style={[styles.shortcutBtnText, { fontFamily: fontFam }]}>
              {lang === 'bn' ? 'গাছ যুক্ত করুন' : 'Add Plant'}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            onPress={onOpenQrScanner}
            style={[
              styles.shortcutBtnSecondary,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.surface,
                borderColor: theme.primary,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.shortcutBtnTextAlt,
                { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              📷 {t.scanQrBtn}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          {onOpenAiDiagnosis && (
            <TouchableOpacity
              activeOpacity={0.85}
              onPress={onOpenAiDiagnosis}
              style={[
                styles.shortcutBtnSecondary,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                  borderColor: theme.primary,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.shortcutBtnTextAlt,
                  { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                🔬 {lang === 'bn' ? 'এআই রোগ নির্ণয়' : 'AI Doctor'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}

          <TouchableOpacity
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            onPress={() => setBotanicalSearchOpen(true)}
            style={[
              styles.shortcutBtnSecondary,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.shortcutBtnTextAlt,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              🌿 {lang === 'bn' ? 'উদ্ভিদ ও জাত সার্চ' : 'Botanical Search'}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        {/* 5. Registered Plants Header + Search Filter + Grid / List View Switcher */}
        <View
          style={[
            styles.card,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={styles.plantsHeaderRow}>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.sectionHeaderTitle,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              🌳 {t.myPlantsTitle} (
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? toBengaliNumber(filteredPlants.length)
                : filteredPlants.length}
              )
            </Text>

            <View style={styles.viewToggleGroup}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => setViewMode('grid')}
                style={[
                  styles.viewToggleBtn,
                  {
                    backgroundColor:
                      viewMode === 'grid'
                        ? theme.primary
                        : theme.surfaceElevated,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: viewMode === 'grid' ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                    fontSize: 12,
                    fontWeight: '600',
                  }}
                >
                  ⊞ Grid
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => setViewMode('list')}
                style={[
                  styles.viewToggleBtn,
                  {
                    backgroundColor:
                      viewMode === 'list'
                        ? theme.primary
                        : theme.surfaceElevated,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: viewMode === 'list' ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                    fontSize: 12,
                    fontWeight: '600',
                  }}
                >
                  ☰ List
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>

          <TextInput
            value={searchQuery}
            onChangeText={setSearchQuery}
            placeholder={
              lang === 'bn'
                ? '🔍 গাছের নাম, জাত বা QR কোড দিয়ে খুঁজুন...'
                : '🔍 Search by plant name, variety or QR ID...'
            }
            placeholderTextColor={theme.textSecondary}
            style={[
              styles.searchInput,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                color: theme.textPrimary,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          />

          {/* Plant Cards in Grid or List View */}
          <View
            style={
              viewMode === 'grid' ? styles.plantsGridWrap : styles.plantsListWrap
            }
          >
            {filteredPlants.map((plant) => {
              const forecast = LocalDbService.getPlantForecast(plant);
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={plant.id}
                  activeOpacity={0.82}
                  onPress={() => onSelectPlant(plant)}
                  style={[
                    viewMode === 'grid'
                      ? styles.plantCardGrid
                      : styles.plantCardList,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                      borderColor: theme.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <View style={styles.cardTopRow}>
                    <View
                      style={[
                        styles.cardEmojiCircle,
                        { backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface },
                      ]}
                    >
                      <Text style={styles.cardEmoji}>🌱</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View
                      style={[
                        styles.healthDotPill,
                        {
                          backgroundColor:
                            plant.healthState === 'healthy'
                              ? theme.selectedSurface
                              : theme.warning + '18',
                        },
                      ]}
                    >
                      <Text
                        style={{
                          color:
                            plant.healthState === 'healthy'
                              ? theme.primary
                              : theme.warning,
                          fontSize: 11,
                          fontWeight: '600',
                        }}
                      >
                        {plant.healthState === 'healthy'
                          ? lang === 'bn'
                            ? '● সুস্থ'
                            : '● Healthy'
                          : lang === 'bn'
                          ? '● যত্ন প্রয়োজন'
                          : '● Care Needed'}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                  </View>

                  <Text
                    numberOfLines={1}
                    style={[
                      styles.cardPlantName,
                      { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                    ]}
                  >
                    {lang === 'bn' ? plant.nameBengali : plant.nameEnglish}
                  </Text>
                  <Text
                    numberOfLines={1}
                    style={[
                      styles.cardVariety,
                      { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                    ]}
                  >
                    {plant.variety}
                  </Text>

                  <View style={styles.cardMetaRow}>
                    <Text
                      style={[styles.cardMetaText, { color: theme.primary }]}
                    >
                      ⏳{' '}
                      {lang === 'bn'
                        ? `বয়স: ${toBengaliNumber(forecast.ageInDays)} দিন`
                        : `Age: ${forecast.ageInDays}d`}
                    </Text>
                    <Text
                      style={[styles.cardMetaText, { color: theme.secondary }]}
                    >
                      💧 {plant.estimatedMoisturePercent}%
                    </Text>
                  </View>

                  <Text
                    numberOfLines={1}
                    style={{
                      color: theme.textSecondary,
                      fontSize: 11.5,
                      fontWeight: '600',
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    🪴{' '}
                    {lang === 'bn'
                      ? forecast.containerSummaryBn ||
                        `${plant.containerSizeInches}" জিও ব্যাগ`
                      : forecast.containerSummaryEn ||
                        `${plant.containerSizeInches}" Geo-Bag`}
                  </Text>

                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.cardFooterTag,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: theme.surface,
                        borderColor: theme.border,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      numberOfLines={1}
                      style={[
                        styles.cardFooterText,
                        { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                      ]}
                    >
                      🔮{' '}
                      {lang === 'bn'
                        ? 'সার ও পরিচর্যা গাইড দেখুন →'
                        : 'Care & Fertilizer Guide →'}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* 6. Recent Plant Activities */}
        {recentLogs.length > 0 && (
          <View
            style={[
              styles.card,
              { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.sectionHeaderTitle,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              📜{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'সাম্প্রতিক বাগান কার্যক্রম'
                : 'Recent Plant Activities'}
            </Text>

            {recentLogs.map((log) => (
              <View
                key={log.id}
                style={[
                  styles.activityRow,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                    borderColor: theme.border,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 15 }}>✅</Text>
                <View style={styles.taskTextCol}>
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: theme.textPrimary,
                      fontWeight: '600',
                      fontSize: 13.5,
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    {lang === 'bn' ? log.titleBengali : log.titleEnglish}
                  </Text>
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: theme.textSecondary,
                      fontSize: 12.5,
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    {log.detailsNotes}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </View>
            ))}
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Comprehensive Add & Register Plant Modal */}
        <AddPlantModal
          visible={addModalOpen}
          lang={lang}
          theme={theme}
          existingPlantCount={plants.length}
          ownerName={user.displayName}
          ownerDistrict={user.district}
          onClose={() => setAddModalOpen(false)}
          onSavePlant={(created) => {
            onAddNewPlant(created);
            setAddModalOpen(false);
          }}
        />

        {/* Structured Botanical Search & Variety Encyclopedia Modal (Requirement #3) */}
        <BotanicalSearchModal
          visible={botanicalSearchOpen}
          theme={theme}
          lang={lang}
          onClose={() => setBotanicalSearchOpen(false)}
          onSelectPlantToAdd={(preset) => {
            const qrId = 'BAGANI-QR-' + (1000 + plants.length + 1);
            const createdPlant: PlantModel = {
              id: 'plt_' + Date.now().toString(36),
              qrCodeId: qrId,
              nameBengali: preset.nameBn,
              nameEnglish: preset.nameEn,
              variety: preset.variety,
              category: preset.category,
              isAllSeason: preset.isAllSeason,
              plantedDateIso: new Date().toISOString(),
              containerSizeInches: preset.containerSize || 20,
              containerType: 'geo_bag',
              containerCapacityLabel: `${preset.containerSize || 20} Inch Geo-Bag`,
              growthStage: 'vegetative',
              canopySize: 'medium',
              soilCondition: 'loam_compost',
              sunlightExposure: 'full_sun',
              rooftopZone: `ছাদবাগান সারি - টব #${plants.length + 1}`,
              healthState: 'healthy',
              lastHealthDiagnosisSummary: 'অনলাইন ভেরিফাইড চারা — সতেজ ও সুস্থ অবস্থা',
              lastWateredIso: new Date().toISOString(),
              lastFertilizedIso: new Date().toISOString(),
              estimatedMoisturePercent: 75,
              qrTagConfig: {
                qrCodeValue: qrId,
                tagSize: 'medium',
                plantName: preset.nameBn,
                varietyName: preset.variety,
                isAllSeason: preset.isAllSeason,
                customQuoteBengali: 'গাছ লাগান, পরিবেশ বাঁচান',
                ownerName: user.displayName,
                ownerAddressOrCode: `${user.district} • ${qrId}`,
              },
            };
            onAddNewPlant(createdPlant);
          }}
        />
      </View>
    </ScrollView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  scrollOuter: {
    width: '100%',
    alignItems: 'center',
  },
  container: {
    width: '100%',
    maxWidth: 1000,
    paddingHorizontal: DESIGN_TOKENS.screenHorizontalPadding,
    paddingVertical: DESIGN_TOKENS.spacing.md,
    gap: DESIGN_TOKENS.spacing.md,
    paddingBottom: 32,
  },
  card: {
    width: '100%',
    padding: DESIGN_TOKENS.cardPadding,
    borderRadius: DESIGN_TOKENS.mainCardRadius,
    borderWidth: 1,
    gap: 12,
  },
  heroProfileRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: 14,
  },
  avatarBox: {
    width: 68,
    height: 68,
    borderRadius: 16,
    borderWidth: 1.5,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    overflow: 'hidden',
    flexShrink: 0,
  },
  avatarImg: {
    width: '100%',
    height: '100%',
  },
  heroTextCol: {
    flex: 1,
    minWidth: 0,
    gap: 3,
  },
  greetingTopLabel: {
    fontSize: 24,
    fontWeight: '800',
    lineHeight: 30,
  },
  greetingText: {
    fontSize: 18,
    fontWeight: '700',
  },
  quoteText: {
    fontSize: 13,
    marginTop: 2,
  },
  weatherSummaryBar: {
    paddingHorizontal: 14,
    paddingVertical: 12,
    borderRadius: DESIGN_TOKENS.smallCardRadius,
    borderWidth: 1,
    gap: 4,
  },
  dateText: {
    fontSize: 13,
    fontWeight: '600',
  },
  weatherLiveText: {
    fontSize: 13,
    fontWeight: '600',
  },
  weatherExpandBox: {
    padding: 14,
    borderRadius: DESIGN_TOKENS.smallCardRadius,
    borderWidth: 1,
    gap: 8,
  },
  weatherGroupTitle: {
    fontSize: 13,
    fontWeight: '600',
  },
  weatherStrip: {
    gap: 8,
    paddingVertical: 4,
  },
  weatherPill: {
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 8,
    borderRadius: 10,
    borderWidth: 1,
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: 2,
  },
  wDay: {
    fontSize: 12,
    fontWeight: '500',
  },
  wTemp: {
    fontSize: 13,
    fontWeight: '600',
  },
  prescRow: {
    padding: 10,
    borderRadius: 10,
    borderWidth: 1,
    gap: 2,

  statsGrid: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    flexWrap: 'wrap',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',

  statCard: {
    width: '48.6%',
    padding: 14,
    borderRadius: DESIGN_TOKENS.smallCardRadius,
    borderWidth: 1,
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: 12,
    minWidth: 0,
  },
  statIconWrap: {
    width: 40,
    height: 40,
    borderRadius: 10,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',

  modalOverlay: {

/**
 * BAGANI — Modular Screen Routes & Navigation Registry
 * Supports all 5 primary bottom navigation tabs (Home, My Garden, Scan, Guide, Profile)
 * plus dedicated views for all 18 screens in DESIGN_SYSTEM.md.
 */

export type AppScreenRoute =
  | 'splash'
  | 'onboarding'
  | 'auth'
  | 'home'
  | 'my_garden'
  | 'scan'
  | 'guide'
  | 'profile'
  | 'plant_profile'
  | 'weather_dashboard'
  | 'notifications'
  // Legacy alias routes mapped seamlessly
  | 'qr_tracker'
  | 'planner'
  | 'scanner'
  | 'directory'
  | 'settings';
