// ##############################################################################
// FILE START: lib/screens/my_garden/my_garden_screen.tsx
// ##############################################################################

/**
 * BAGANI — My Garden Screen (Screens 5, 6 & 8 of DESIGN_SYSTEM.md)
 */

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Modal,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { AppLanguage } from '../../core/l10n/translations';
import { AddPlantModal } from '../../core/components/add_plant_modal';
import { BotanicalSearchModal } from '../../core/components/botanical_search_modal';
import {
  APP_TYPOGRAPHY,
  AppThemeColors,
  DESIGN_TOKENS,
} from '../../core/theme/app_theme';
import { toBengaliNumber } from '../../core/utils/date_utils';
import { PlantModel } from '../../models/plant_model';
import { UserModel } from '../../models/user_model';
import { LocalDbService } from '../../services/local_db_service';

interface MyGardenScreenProps {
  user: UserModel;
  plants: PlantModel[];
  theme: AppThemeColors;
  lang: AppLanguage;
  onSelectPlant: (plant: PlantModel) => void;
  onAddNewPlant: (plant: PlantModel) => void;
  onOpenQrTagDesigner: () => void;
}

export const MyGardenScreen: React.FC<MyGardenScreenProps> = ({
  user,
  plants,
  theme,
  lang,
  onSelectPlant,
  onAddNewPlant,
  onOpenQrTagDesigner,
}) => {
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [filterMode, setFilterMode] = useState<
    'all' | 'all_season' | 'attention'
  >('all');
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'grid' | 'list'>('grid');
  const [addModalOpen, setAddModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [botanicalSearchOpen, setBotanicalSearchOpen] = useState(false);

  const [newNameBn, setNewNameBn] = useState('');
  const [newVariety, setNewVariety] = useState('');
  const [newBagSize, setNewBagSize] = useState('20');
  const [newIsAllSeason, setNewIsAllSeason] = useState(true);

  const fontFam =
    lang === 'bn'
      ? APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyBengali
      : APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyEnglish;

  const filteredPlants = plants.filter((p) => {
    const matchesSearch =
      p.nameBengali.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      p.nameEnglish.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      p.variety.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      p.qrCodeId.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase());

    if (!matchesSearch) return false;
    if (filterMode === 'all_season') return p.isAllSeason;
    if (filterMode === 'attention') return p.healthState !== 'healthy';
    return true;
  });

  const handleCreatePlant = () => {
    const cleanName = newNameBn.trim() || 'ব্যানানা ম্যাঙ্গো (আম)';
    const cleanVar = newVariety.trim() || 'থাই বারোমাসি কলম';
    const qrId = 'BAGANI-QR-' + (1000 + plants.length + 1);

    const created: PlantModel = {
      id: 'plt_' + Date.now().toString(36),
      qrCodeId: qrId,
      nameBengali: cleanName,
      nameEnglish: cleanName,
      variety: cleanVar,
      category: 'fruit',
      isAllSeason: newIsAllSeason,
      plantedDateIso: new Date().toISOString(),
      containerSizeInches: Number(newBagSize) || 20,
      rooftopZone: `ছাদ বাগান সারি - টব #${plants.length + 1}`,
      healthState: 'healthy',
      lastHealthDiagnosisSummary: 'নতুন রেজিস্টার্ড চারা — সুস্থ অবস্থা',
      lastWateredIso: new Date().toISOString(),
      lastFertilizedIso: new Date().toISOString(),
      estimatedMoisturePercent: 75,
      qrTagConfig: {
        qrCodeValue: qrId,
        tagSize: 'medium',
        plantName: cleanName,
        varietyName: cleanVar,
        isAllSeason: newIsAllSeason,
        customQuoteBengali: 'গাছ লাগান, পরিবেশ বাঁচান',
        ownerName: user.displayName,
        ownerAddressOrCode: `${user.district} • ${qrId}`,
      },
    };
    onAddNewPlant(created);
    setNewNameBn('');
    setNewVariety('');
    setAddModalOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <ScrollView
      showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
      contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollOuter}
    >
      <View style={styles.container}>
        {/* Top Action Header */}
        <View
          style={[
            styles.mainCard,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={styles.headerRow}>
            <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.sectionHeading,
                  { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                🌳 {lang === 'bn' ? 'আমার ছাদবাগান' : 'My Rooftop Garden'}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.captionText,
                  { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? `মোট ${toBengaliNumber(plants.length)}টি ডিজিটাল গাছ রেজিস্টার্ড রয়েছে`
                  : `${plants.length} plants digitally registered with QR IDs`}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.viewToggleRow}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => setViewMode('grid')}
                style={[
                  styles.viewToggleBtn,
                  {
                    backgroundColor:
                      viewMode === 'grid'
                        ? theme.primary
                        : theme.surfaceElevated,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: viewMode === 'grid' ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                    fontSize: 12,
                    fontWeight: '600',
                  }}
                >
                  ⊞ Grid
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => setViewMode('list')}
                style={[
                  styles.viewToggleBtn,
                  {
                    backgroundColor:
                      viewMode === 'list'
                        ? theme.primary
                        : theme.surfaceElevated,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: viewMode === 'list' ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                    fontSize: 12,
                    fontWeight: '600',
                  }}
                >
                  ☰ List
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Manual Plant Search Input */}
          <TextInput
            value={searchQuery}
            onChangeText={setSearchQuery}
            placeholder={
              lang === 'bn'
                ? '🔍 গাছের নাম, জাত বা QR কোড দিয়ে খুঁজুন...'
                : '🔍 Search plant by name, variety or QR ID...'
            }
            placeholderTextColor={theme.textSecondary}
            style={[
              styles.inputField,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                color: theme.textPrimary,
                borderColor: theme.border,
                fontFamily: fontFam,
              },
            ]}
          />

          {/* Filter Chips */}
          <View style={styles.filterRow}>
            {(
              [
                { id: 'all', bn: 'সব গাছ', en: 'All Plants' },
                { id: 'all_season', bn: 'বারোমাসি জাত', en: 'All-Season' },
                { id: 'attention', bn: 'যত্ন প্রয়োজন', en: 'Needs Attention' },
              ] as const
            ).map((f) => {
              const active = filterMode === f.id;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={f.id}
                  onPress={() => setFilterMode(f.id)}
                  style={[
                    styles.filterChip,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: active
                        ? theme.selectedSurface
                        : theme.surfaceElevated,
                      borderColor: active ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: active ? theme.primary : theme.textSecondary,
                      fontSize: 12.5,
                      fontWeight: '600',
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    {lang === 'bn' ? f.bn : f.en}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
          </View>

          {/* Action Buttons: + Add Plant & Plant QR */}
          <View style={styles.actionRow}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => setAddModalOpen(true)}
              style={[
                styles.primaryBtn,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.primary,
                  flexDirection: 'row',
                  gap: 8,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <View
                style={{
                  width: 24,
                  height: 24,
                  borderRadius: 12,
                  backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.22)',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                }}
              >
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: '#FFFFFF',
                    fontSize: 16,
                    fontWeight: '800',
                    lineHeight: 18,
                  }}
                >
                  +
                </Text>
              </View>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.btnText,
                  { color: '#FFFFFF', fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'গাছ যুক্ত করুন' : 'Add Plant'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={onOpenQrTagDesigner}
              style={[
                styles.secondaryBtn,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surface,
                  borderColor: theme.primary,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.btnText,
                  { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                📷 {lang === 'bn' ? 'প্ল্যান্ট QR' : 'Plant QR'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => setBotanicalSearchOpen(true)}
              style={[
                styles.secondaryBtn,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.btnText,
                  { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                🌿 {lang === 'bn' ? 'উদ্ভিদ সার্চ' : 'Search Species'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Registered Plants Grid / List */}
        <View
          style={
            viewMode === 'grid' ? styles.plantsGridWrap : styles.plantsListWrap
          }
        >
          {filteredPlants.map((plant) => {
            const forecast = LocalDbService.getPlantForecast(plant);
            const isHealthy = plant.healthState === 'healthy';
            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={plant.id}
                activeOpacity={0.82}
                onPress={() => onSelectPlant(plant)}
                style={[
                  viewMode === 'grid'
                    ? styles.plantCardGrid
                    : styles.plantCardList,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.surface,
                    borderColor: theme.border,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <View style={styles.cardTopRow}>
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.qrBadge,
                      {
                        color: theme.primary,
                        backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    {plant.qrCodeId}
                  </Text>
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: isHealthy ? theme.primary : theme.warning,
                      fontSize: 12,
                      fontWeight: '600',
                    }}
                  >
                    {isHealthy
                      ? lang === 'bn'
                        ? '● সুস্থ'
                        : '● Healthy'
                      : lang === 'bn'
                      ? '● যত্ন প্রয়োজন'
                      : '● Care Needed'}
                  </Text>
                </View>

                <Text
                  numberOfLines={1}
                  style={[
                    styles.cardHeading,
                    { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                  ]}
                >
                  🌱 {lang === 'bn' ? plant.nameBengali : plant.nameEnglish}
                </Text>
                <Text
                  numberOfLines={1}
                  style={[
                    styles.captionText,
                    { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                  ]}
                >
                  {plant.variety} •{' '}
                  {lang === 'bn'
                    ? forecast.containerSummaryBn ||
                      `${plant.containerSizeInches}" জিও ব্যাগ`
                    : forecast.containerSummaryEn ||
                      `${plant.containerSizeInches}" Geo-Bag`}
                </Text>

                <View style={styles.metaRow}>
                  <Text
                    style={[styles.captionText, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}
                  >
                    ⏳{' '}
                    {lang === 'bn'
                      ? `বয়স: ${toBengaliNumber(forecast.ageInDays)} দিন`
                      : `Age: ${forecast.ageInDays}d`}
                  </Text>
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.captionText,
                      { color: theme.primary, fontWeight: '600' },
                    ]}
                  >
                    💧 {plant.estimatedMoisturePercent}%
                  </Text>
                </View>

                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.cardFooterTag,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                      borderColor: theme.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text
                    numberOfLines={1}
                    style={[
                      styles.cardFooterText,
                      { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                    ]}
                  >
                    🔮{' '}
                    {lang === 'bn'
                      ? 'সার ও পরিচর্যা গাইড দেখুন →'
                      : 'Care & Fertilizer Guide →'}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          })}
        </View>

        {/* Comprehensive Add & Register Plant Modal */}
        <AddPlantModal
          visible={addModalOpen}
          lang={lang}
          theme={theme}
          existingPlantCount={plants.length}
          ownerName={user.displayName}
          ownerDistrict={user.district}
          onClose={() => setAddModalOpen(false)}
          onSavePlant={(created) => {
            onAddNewPlant(created);
            setAddModalOpen(false);
          }}
        />

        {/* Structured Botanical Search & Variety Encyclopedia Modal */}
        <BotanicalSearchModal
          visible={botanicalSearchOpen}
          theme={theme}
          lang={lang}
          onClose={() => setBotanicalSearchOpen(false)}
          onSelectPlantToAdd={(preset) => {
            const qrId = 'BAGANI-QR-' + (1000 + plants.length + 1);
            const createdPlant: PlantModel = {
              id: 'plt_' + Date.now().toString(36),
              qrCodeId: qrId,
              nameBengali: preset.nameBn,
              nameEnglish: preset.nameEn,
              variety: preset.variety,
              category: preset.category,
              isAllSeason: preset.isAllSeason,
              plantedDateIso: new Date().toISOString(),
              containerSizeInches: preset.containerSize || 20,
              containerType: 'geo_bag',
              containerCapacityLabel: `${preset.containerSize || 20} Inch Geo-Bag`,
              growthStage: 'vegetative',
              canopySize: 'medium',
              soilCondition: 'loam_compost',
              sunlightExposure: 'full_sun',
              rooftopZone: `ছাদবাগান সারি - টব #${plants.length + 1}`,
              healthState: 'healthy',
              lastHealthDiagnosisSummary: 'অনলাইন ভেরিফাইড চারা — সতেজ ও সুস্থ অবস্থা',
              lastWateredIso: new Date().toISOString(),
              lastFertilizedIso: new Date().toISOString(),
              estimatedMoisturePercent: 75,
              qrTagConfig: {
                qrCodeValue: qrId,
                tagSize: 'medium',
                plantName: preset.nameBn,
                varietyName: preset.variety,
                isAllSeason: preset.isAllSeason,
                customQuoteBengali: 'গাছ লাগান, পরিবেশ বাঁচান',
                ownerName: user.displayName,
                ownerAddressOrCode: `${user.district} • ${qrId}`,
              },
            };
            onAddNewPlant(createdPlant);
          }}
        />
      </View>
    </ScrollView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  scrollOuter: {
    width: '100%',
    alignItems: 'center',
  },
  container: {
    width: '100%',
    maxWidth: 1000,
    paddingHorizontal: DESIGN_TOKENS.screenHorizontalPadding,
    paddingVertical: DESIGN_TOKENS.spacing.md,
    gap: DESIGN_TOKENS.spacing.md,
    paddingBottom: 32,
  },
  mainCard: {
    width: '100%',
    padding: DESIGN_TOKENS.cardPadding,
    borderRadius: DESIGN_TOKENS.mainCardRadius,
    borderWidth: 1,
    gap: 12,
  },
  headerRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
    flexWrap: 'wrap',
    gap: 8,
  },
  sectionHeading: {
    fontSize: 18,
    fontWeight: '700',
  },
  cardHeading: {
    fontSize: 16,
    fontWeight: '700',
  },
  captionText: {
    fontSize: 13,
  },
  viewToggleRow: {

// ##############################################################################
// FILE START: lib/screens/notifications/notifications_modal.tsx
// ##############################################################################

/**
 * BAGANI — Notifications Center Modal (Screen 14 of DESIGN_SYSTEM.md)
 * Allows viewing all arrived notifications & created tasks, selecting items,
 * choosing "Already Completed" vs "Do It Later", and clicking "Save" on the right-hand side.
 */

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Modal,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { AppLanguage } from '../../core/l10n/translations';
import {
  APP_TYPOGRAPHY,
  AppThemeColors,
  DESIGN_TOKENS,
} from '../../core/theme/app_theme';
import {
  CareTaskStatus,
  GranularCareTask,
} from '../../models/care_schedule_model';

interface NotificationsModalProps {
  visible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  theme: AppThemeColors;
  lang: AppLanguage;
  careTasks: GranularCareTask[];
  onToggleTask: (task: GranularCareTask) => void;
  onSaveTaskStatus?: (
    task: GranularCareTask,
    nextStatus: CareTaskStatus
  ) => void;
}

export const NotificationsModal: React.FC<NotificationsModalProps> = ({
  visible,
  onClose,
  theme,
  lang,
  careTasks,
  onToggleTask,
  onSaveTaskStatus,
}) => {
  const [filterTab, setFilterTab] = useState<
    'all' | 'pending' | 'do_later' | 'completed'
  >('all');
  const [selectedDrafts, setSelectedDrafts] = useState<
    Record<string, CareTaskStatus>
  >({});

  const fontFam =
    lang === 'bn'
      ? APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyBengali
      : APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyEnglish;

  const filteredList = careTasks.filter((t) => {
    const status: CareTaskStatus = t.isCompleted
      ? 'completed'
      : t.status || 'pending';
    if (filterTab === 'pending') return status === 'pending';
    if (filterTab === 'do_later') return status === 'do_later';
    if (filterTab === 'completed') return status === 'completed';
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <Modal visible={visible} animationType="fade" transparent>
      <View style={styles.backdrop}>
        <View
          style={[
            styles.sheet,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={[styles.headerRow, { borderBottomColor: theme.border }]}>
            <View style={styles.titleRow}>
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 18 }}>🔔</Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.sectionHeading,
                  { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? `নোটিফিকেশন ও পরিচর্যা তালিকা (${careTasks.length})`
                  : `Notifications & Care Tasks (${careTasks.length})`}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={onClose} style={styles.closeBtn}>
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textSecondary,
                  fontSize: 16,
                  fontWeight: '700',
                }}
              >
                ✕
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          {/* Filter Tabs inside Notification Center */}
          <View style={[styles.tabRow, { borderBottomColor: theme.border }]}>
            {(
              [
                {
                  id: 'all',
                  bn: `সব (${careTasks.length})`,
                  en: `All (${careTasks.length})`,
                },
                {
                  id: 'pending',
                  bn: `বাকি (${
                    careTasks.filter(
                      (x) => !x.isCompleted && x.status !== 'do_later'
                    ).length
                  })`,
                  en: `Pending (${
                    careTasks.filter(
                      (x) => !x.isCompleted && x.status !== 'do_later'
                    ).length
                  })`,
                },
                {
                  id: 'do_later',
                  bn: `পরে করব (${
                    careTasks.filter((x) => x.status === 'do_later').length
                  })`,
                  en: `Do Later (${
                    careTasks.filter((x) => x.status === 'do_later').length
                  })`,
                },
                {
                  id: 'completed',
                  bn: `সম্পন্ন (${
                    careTasks.filter(
                      (x) => x.isCompleted || x.status === 'completed'
                    ).length
                  })`,
                  en: `Completed (${
                    careTasks.filter(
                      (x) => x.isCompleted || x.status === 'completed'
                    ).length
                  })`,
                },
              ] as const
            ).map((tab) => {
              const active = filterTab === tab.id;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={tab.id}
                  onPress={() => setFilterTab(tab.id)}
                  style={[
                    styles.tabBtn,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: active
                        ? theme.primary
                        : theme.surfaceElevated,
                      borderColor: active ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: active ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textSecondary,
                      fontSize: 11.5,
                      fontWeight: '700',
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    {lang === 'bn' ? tab.bn : tab.en}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
          </View>

          <ScrollView
            showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
            contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
          >
            {filteredList.map((task) => {
              const isSelected = task.id in selectedDrafts;
              const draftChoice = selectedDrafts[task.id] || 'completed';
              const effectiveStatus: CareTaskStatus = task.isCompleted
                ? 'completed'
                : task.status || 'pending';

              return (
                <View
                  key={task.id}
                  style={[
                    styles.notifCard,
                    {
                      backgroundColor:
                        effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                          ? theme.selectedSurface
                          : theme.surfaceElevated,
                      borderColor: isSelected
                        ? theme.primary
                        : effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                        ? theme.primary
                        : theme.border,
                      borderWidth: isSelected ? 1.5 : 1,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <View style={styles.notifTopRow}>
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      onPress={() => {
                        setSelectedDrafts((prev) => {
                          const next = { ...prev };
                          if (task.id in next) {
                            delete next[task.id];
                          } else {
                            next[task.id] =
                              effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                                ? 'do_later'
                                : 'completed';
                          }
                          return next;
                        });
                      }}
                      style={{
                        flex: 1,
                        flexDirection: 'row',
                        alignItems: 'flex-start',
                        gap: 10,
                      }}
                    >
                      <Text style={{ fontSize: 16 }}>
                        {effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                          ? '✅'
                          : effectiveStatus === 'do_later'
                          ? '⏰'
                          : '🔔'}
                      </Text>
                      <View style={{ flex: 1, gap: 4 }}>
                        <Text
                          style={{
                            color: theme.textPrimary,
                            fontWeight: '700',
                            fontSize: 14,
                            fontFamily: fontFam,
                            textDecorationLine:
                              effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                                ? 'line-through'
                                : 'none',
                          }}
                        >
                          {lang === 'bn'
                            ? task.plantNameBengali
                            : task.plantNameEnglish}{' '}
                          • {task.waterAmountLiters}L (
                          {task.recommendedTimeSlot})
                        </Text>
                        <Text
                          style={{
                            color: theme.textSecondary,
                            fontSize: 12.5,
                            lineHeight: 19,
                            fontFamily: fontFam,
                          }}
                        >
                          {lang === 'bn'
                            ? task.fertilizerOrSprayInstructionBengali
                            : task.fertilizerOrSprayInstructionEnglish}
                        </Text>
                      </View>
                    </TouchableOpacity>

                    {/* Right-side Save button or Select/Status badge */}
                    {isSelected ? (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        onPress={() => {
                          if (onSaveTaskStatus) {
                            onSaveTaskStatus(task, draftChoice);
                          } else {
                            onToggleTask(task);
                          }
                          setSelectedDrafts((prev) => {
                            const next = { ...prev };
                            delete next[task.id];
                            return next;
                          });
                        }}
                        style={[
                          styles.saveBtn,
                          { backgroundColor: theme.primary },
                        ]}
                      >
                        <Text
                          style={{
                            color: '#FFFFFF',
                            fontWeight: '700',
                            fontSize: 12.5,
                            fontFamily: fontFam,
                          }}
                        >
                          💾 {lang === 'bn' ? 'সেভ' : 'Save'}
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    ) : (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        onPress={() => {
                          setSelectedDrafts((prev) => ({
                            ...prev,
                            [task.id]:
                              effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                                ? 'do_later'
                                : 'completed',
                          }));
                        }}
                        style={[
                          styles.statusBadge,
                          {
                            backgroundColor:
                              effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                                ? theme.primary
                                : effectiveStatus === 'do_later'
                                ? 'rgba(217, 119, 6, 0.14)'
                                : theme.surface,
                            borderColor:
                              effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                                ? theme.primary
                                : effectiveStatus === 'do_later'
                                ? theme.warning
                                : theme.border,
                          },
                        ]}
                      >
                        <Text
                          style={{
                            color:
                              effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                                ? '#FFFFFF'
                                : effectiveStatus === 'do_later'
                                ? theme.warning
                                : theme.primary,
                            fontSize: 11,
                            fontWeight: '700',
                            fontFamily: fontFam,
                          }}
                        >
                          {effectiveStatus === 'completed'
                            ? lang === 'bn'
                              ? '✓ সম্পন্ন'
                              : '✓ Completed'
                            : effectiveStatus === 'do_later'
                            ? lang === 'bn'
                              ? '⏰ পরে করব'
                              : '⏰ Do Later'
                            : lang === 'bn'
                            ? 'নির্বাচন'
                            : 'Select'}
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    )}
                  </View>

                  {isSelected && (
                    <View
                      style={[
                        styles.choiceBar,
                        { borderTopColor: theme.border },
                      ]}
                    >
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        onPress={() =>
                          setSelectedDrafts((prev) => ({
                            ...prev,
                            [task.id]: 'completed',
                          }))
                        }
                        style={[
                          styles.choicePill,
                          {
                            backgroundColor:
                              draftChoice === 'completed'
                                ? theme.primary
                                : theme.surface,
                            borderColor:
                              draftChoice === 'completed'
                                ? theme.primary
                                : theme.border,
                          },
                        ]}
                      >
                        <Text
                          style={{
                            color:
                              draftChoice === 'completed'
                                ? '#FFFFFF'
                                : theme.textPrimary,
                            fontSize: 12,
                            fontWeight: '700',
                            fontFamily: fontFam,
                          }}
                        >
                          ✓{' '}
                          {lang === 'bn'
                            ? 'সম্পন্ন করেছি'
                            : 'Already Completed'}
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>

                      <TouchableOpacity
                        onPress={() =>
                          setSelectedDrafts((prev) => ({
                            ...prev,
                            [task.id]: 'do_later',
                          }))
                        }
                        style={[
                          styles.choicePill,
                          {
                            backgroundColor:
                              draftChoice === 'do_later'
                                ? theme.warning
                                : theme.surface,
                            borderColor:
                              draftChoice === 'do_later'
                                ? theme.warning
                                : theme.border,
                          },
                        ]}
                      >
                        <Text
                          style={{
                            color:
                              draftChoice === 'do_later'
                                ? '#FFFFFF'
                                : theme.textPrimary,
                            fontSize: 12,
                            fontWeight: '700',
                            fontFamily: fontFam,
                          }}
                        >
                          ⏰ {lang === 'bn' ? 'পরে করব' : 'Do It Later'}
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    </View>
                  )}
                </View>
              );
            })}
          </ScrollView>
        </View>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  backdrop: {
    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(16, 23, 19, 0.6)',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    alignItems: 'center',
    padding: DESIGN_TOKENS.screenHorizontalPadding,
  },
  sheet: {
    width: '100%',
    maxWidth: 520,
    maxHeight: '85%',
    borderRadius: DESIGN_TOKENS.mainCardRadius,
    borderWidth: 1,
    overflow: 'hidden',
  },
  headerRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
    padding: DESIGN_TOKENS.cardPadding,
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
  },
  titleRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: 8,
    flex: 1,
  },
  sectionHeading: {
    fontSize: 16,
    fontWeight: '700',
  },
  closeBtn: {
    padding: 6,
  },
  tabRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    flexWrap: 'wrap',
    gap: 6,
    paddingHorizontal: DESIGN_TOKENS.cardPadding,
    paddingVertical: 10,
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
  },
  tabBtn: {
    paddingHorizontal: 10,
    paddingVertical: 6,
    borderRadius: 8,
    borderWidth: 1,
  },
  listContent: {
    padding: DESIGN_TOKENS.cardPadding,
    gap: DESIGN_TOKENS.spacing.sm,
  },
  notifCard: {
    padding: DESIGN_TOKENS.spacing.sm,
    borderRadius: DESIGN_TOKENS.smallCardRadius,
    borderWidth: 1,
    gap: 8,
  },
  notifTopRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: 8,
  },
  saveBtn: {
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 8,
    borderRadius: 8,
    flexShrink: 0,
  },
  statusBadge: {
    paddingHorizontal: 10,
    paddingVertical: 6,
    borderRadius: 8,
    borderWidth: 1,
    flexShrink: 0,
  },
  choiceBar: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    flexWrap: 'wrap',
    gap: 8,
    paddingTop: 8,
    borderTopWidth: 1,
  },
  choicePill: {
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 6,
    borderRadius: 8,
    borderWidth: 1,
  },
});


// ##############################################################################

// ##############################################################################


// ##############################################################################
// FILE START: lib/screens/onboarding/onboarding_modal.tsx
// ##############################################################################

/**
 * BAGANI — Minimalist Splash & 3-Step Onboarding Walkthrough (Screens 1 & 2 of DESIGN_SYSTEM.md)
 */

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Image,
  Modal,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { APP_CONFIG } from '../../core/constants/app_constants';
import { AppLanguage } from '../../core/l10n/translations';
import {
  APP_TYPOGRAPHY,
  AppThemeColors,
  DESIGN_TOKENS,
} from '../../core/theme/app_theme';

interface OnboardingModalProps {
  visible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  theme: AppThemeColors;
  lang: AppLanguage;
}

const SLIDES = [
  {
    icon: '📷',
    titleBn: 'ডিজিটাল প্ল্যান্ট রেজিস্ট্রেশন ও QR ট্যাগ',
    titleEn: 'Digital Plant Registration & QR Tags',
    descBn:
      'আপনার ছাদবাগানের প্রতিটি গাছকে ইউনিক QR কোড ট্যাগ দিয়ে চিহ্নিত করুন এবং এক স্ক্যানেই গাছের সম্পূর্ণ প্রোফাইল দেখুন।',
    descEn:
      'Register every rooftop pot with a printable QR tag and scan anytime to view its digital profile and care history.',
  },
  {
    icon: '🔬',
    titleBn: 'এআই রোগ নির্ণয় ও ভবিষ্যৎ পূর্বাভাস',
    titleEn: 'AI Disease Diagnosis & Growth Forecast',
    descBn:
      'পাতার ছবি ও লক্ষণ বিশ্লেষণ করে রোগ নির্ণয় করুন এবং গাছের বর্তমান বয়স ও ফল ধরার সম্ভাব্য সময় (Harvesting Countdown) ট্র্যাক করুন।',
    descEn:
      'Detect leaf diseases with organic treatment steps and track real-time plant status and harvesting countdowns.',
  },
  {
    icon: '🌦️',
    titleBn: 'আবহাওয়া ভিত্তিক সুনির্দিষ্ট পরিচর্যা',
    titleEn: 'Weather-Indexed Smart Care',
    descBn:
      'আপনার জেলার লাইভ আবহাওয়া ও বৃষ্টির পূর্বাভাস অনুযায়ী প্রতিদিন কোন গাছে কত লিটার পানি ও সার প্রয়োজন তার স্বয়ংক্রিয় নির্দেশনা পান।',
    descEn:
      'Receive daily watering and organic fertilizer recommendations tailored to your local temperature and rain forecast.',
  },
];

export const OnboardingModal: React.FC<OnboardingModalProps> = ({
  visible,
  onClose,
  theme,
  lang,
}) => {
  const [step, setStep] = useState<'splash' | number>('splash');
  const fontFam =
    lang === 'bn'
      ? APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyBengali
      : APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyEnglish;

  if (!visible) return null;

  return (
    <Modal visible={visible} animationType="fade" transparent>
      <View
        style={[
          styles.fullScreen,
          { backgroundColor: theme.background },
        ]}
      >
        {step === 'splash' ? (
          <View
            style={[
              styles.card,
              { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
            ]}
          >
            <Image
              source={require('../../../assets/images/logo.png')}
              style={styles.logo}
              resizeMode="contain"
            />
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.mainHeading,
                { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {APP_CONFIG.appName}
            </Text>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.sectionSub,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {APP_CONFIG.subtitle}
            </Text>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.caption,
                { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {APP_CONFIG.tagline} • {APP_CONFIG.developerName}
            </Text>

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => setStep(0)}
              style={[styles.primaryBtn, { backgroundColor: theme.primary }]}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.btnText, { fontFamily: fontFam }]}>
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'অনবোর্ডিং ট্যুর শুরু করুন →'
                  : 'Explore Onboarding →'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity onPress={onClose} style={styles.skipBtn}>
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textSecondary,
                  fontWeight: '600',
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'ড্যাশবোর্ডে ফিরে যান' : 'Return to App'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        ) : (
          <View
            style={[
              styles.card,
              { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
            ]}
          >
            <View
              style={[
                styles.iconCircle,
                { backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 28 }}>{SLIDES[step].icon}</Text>
            </View>

            <Text
              style={[
                styles.slideTitle,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn' ? SLIDES[step].titleBn : SLIDES[step].titleEn}
            </Text>

            <Text
              style={[
                styles.slideDesc,
                { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn' ? SLIDES[step].descBn : SLIDES[step].descEn}
            </Text>

            <View style={styles.dotsRow}>
              {SLIDES.map((_, idx) => (
                <View
                  key={idx}
                  style={[
                    styles.dot,
                    {
                      backgroundColor:
                        idx === step ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                      width: idx === step ? 24 : 8,
                    },
                  ]}
                />
              ))}
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => {
                if (step < SLIDES.length - 1) {
                  setStep(step + 1);
                } else {
                  setStep('splash');
                  onClose();
                }
              }}
              style={[styles.primaryBtn, { backgroundColor: theme.primary }]}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.btnText, { fontFamily: fontFam }]}>
                {step < SLIDES.length - 1
                  ? lang === 'bn'
                    ? 'পরবর্তী ধাপ →'
                    : 'Next →'
                  : lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'শুরু করুন →'
                  : 'Get Started →'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        )}
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  fullScreen: {
    flex: 1,
    justifyContent: 'center',
    alignItems: 'center',
    padding: DESIGN_TOKENS.screenHorizontalPadding,
  },
  card: {
    width: '100%',
    maxWidth: 460,
    padding: DESIGN_TOKENS.spacing.xl,
    borderRadius: DESIGN_TOKENS.mainCardRadius,
    borderWidth: 1,
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: DESIGN_TOKENS.spacing.md,
  },
  logo: {
    width: 140,
    height: 110,
  },
  mainHeading: {
    fontSize: 28,
    fontWeight: '800',
  },
  sectionSub: {
    fontSize: 16,
    fontWeight: '600',
    textAlign: 'center',
  },
  caption: {
    fontSize: 13,
    textAlign: 'center',
  },
  iconCircle: {
    width: 64,
    height: 64,
    borderRadius: 32,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },
  slideTitle: {
    fontSize: 20,
    fontWeight: '700',
    textAlign: 'center',
  },
  slideDesc: {
    fontSize: 14,
    textAlign: 'center',
    lineHeight: 22,
  },
  dotsRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    gap: 6,
    marginVertical: 4,
  },
  dot: {
    height: 8,
    borderRadius: 4,
  },
  primaryBtn: {
    width: '100%',
    minHeight: DESIGN_TOKENS.buttonHeight,
    borderRadius: DESIGN_TOKENS.buttonRadius,
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    gap: 8,

export const PlannerScreen: React.FC<PlannerScreenProps> = ({
  theme,
  lang,
}) => {
  const [totalRoofSqFtInput, setTotalRoofSqFtInput] = useState('600');
  const [sunlight, setSunlight] = useState<SunlightExposure>('full_sun');
  const [hasHighDust, setHasHighDust] = useState(true);
  const [isDroughtProne, setIsDroughtProne] = useState(false);
  const [selectedCategories, setSelectedCategories] = useState<PlantCategory[]>([
    'fruit',
    'vegetable',
    'herb',
  ]);
  const [completedStages, setCompletedStages] = useState<
    Record<number, boolean>
  >({
    1: true,
    2: true,
  });

  const toggleCategory = (cat: PlantCategory) => {
    setSelectedCategories((prev) =>
      prev.includes(cat) ? prev.filter((c) => c !== cat) : [...prev, cat]
    );
  };

  const planSummary = useMemo(() => {
    const rawSqFt = Math.max(50, Number(totalRoofSqFtInput) || 400);
    const usableSqFt = Math.round(rawSqFt * 0.65);
    const maxSafeLoadKg = usableSqFt * 35;

    const smallCount = Math.max(2, Math.floor(usableSqFt * 0.04));
    const mediumCount = Math.max(2, Math.floor(usableSqFt * 0.03));
    const largeCount =
      sunlight === 'full_sun' ? Math.max(1, Math.floor(usableSqFt * 0.015)) : 1;

    const geoBags: GeoBagRecommendation[] = [
      {
        bagSizeInches: '12x12 inch (১০ গ্যালন)',
        suitablePlantsBn: 'মরিচ, বেগুন, টমেটো, পুদিনা ও ধনেপাতা',
        suitablePlantsEn: 'Chili, Brinjal, Tomato, Mint & Coriander',
        recommendedCount: smallCount,
        estimatedSoilWeightKg: smallCount * 14,
      },
      {
        bagSizeInches: '16x16 inch (২০ গ্যালন)',
        suitablePlantsBn: 'বারোমাসি লেবু, ডালিম, পেয়ারা ও শিম',
        suitablePlantsEn: 'All-Season Lemon, Pomegranate, Guava & Beans',
        recommendedCount: mediumCount,
        estimatedSoilWeightKg: mediumCount * 28,
      },
      {
        bagSizeInches: '24x24 inch (৪৫ গ্যালন - হাফ ড্রাম বিকল্প)',
        suitablePlantsBn: 'কাটিমন বারোমাসি আম, মাল্টা ও ড্রাগন ফল',
        suitablePlantsEn: 'Katimon Mango, Malta & Dragon Fruit',
        recommendedCount: largeCount,
        estimatedSoilWeightKg: largeCount * 55,
      },
    ];

    const totalEstimatedWeightKg = geoBags.reduce(
      (acc, item) => acc + item.estimatedSoilWeightKg,
      0
    );

    const totalSoilKg = Math.round(totalEstimatedWeightKg * 0.85);
    const loamKg = Math.round(totalSoilKg * 0.4);
    const compostKg = Math.round(totalSoilKg * 0.3);
    const cocopeatKg = Math.round(totalSoilKg * 0.2);
    const bioAmendmentKg = Math.max(1, Math.round(totalSoilKg * 0.1));

    const recommendedVarieties = [
      sunlight === 'full_sun'
        ? lang === 'bn'
          ? '🥭 কাটিমন বারোমাসি আম (রোদপ্রিয় ও উচ্চ ফলনশীল)'
          : '🥭 Katimon All-Season Mango (Full Sun & High Yield)'
        : lang === 'bn'
        ? '🌿 পুদিনা, থানকুনি ও অ্যালোভেরা (আংশিক ছায়া সহনশীল)'
        : '🌿 Mint, Thankuni & Aloe Vera (Shade Tolerant)',
      hasHighDust
        ? lang === 'bn'
          ? '🍋 সিডলেস বারোমাসি লেবু ও থাই পেয়ারা (ধুলোবালি সহনশীল পাতা)'
          : '🍋 Seedless Lemon & Thai Guava (Dust-Resilient Foliage)'
        : lang === 'bn'
        ? '🍅 বারি হাইব্রিড টমেটো ও ক্যাপসিকাম'
        : '🍅 BARI Hybrid Tomato & Capsicum',
      isDroughtProne
        ? lang === 'bn'
          ? '🐉 ড্রাগন ফল ও ডালিম (খরা ও কম পানিতে টিকে থাকে)'
          : '🐉 Dragon Fruit & Pomegranate (Drought Tolerant)'
        : lang === 'bn'
        ? '🌶️ নাগা মরিচ ও চেরি টমেটো'
        : '🌶️ Naga Chili & Cherry Tomato',
    ];

    return {
      rawSqFt,
      usableSqFt,
      maxSafeLoadKg,
      totalEstimatedWeightKg,
      geoBags,
      loamKg,
      compostKg,
      cocopeatKg,
      bioAmendmentKg,
      recommendedVarieties,
    };
  }, [totalRoofSqFtInput, sunlight, hasHighDust, isDroughtProne, lang]);

  return (
    <ScrollView
      showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
      contentContainerStyle={styles.container}
    >
      {/* Wizard Card: Input Roof Dimensions & Conditions */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.card,
          { backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground, borderColor: theme.border },
        ]}
      >
        <Text style={[styles.sectionTitle, { color: theme.textPrimary }]}>
          🏡{' '}
          {lang === 'bn'
            ? 'স্মার্ট ছাদবাগান প্ল্যানার উইজার্ড (Smart Rooftop Wizard)'
            : 'Smart Rooftop Garden Planner Wizard'}
        </Text>
        <Text style={[styles.subText, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
          {lang === 'bn'
            ? 'আপনার ছাদের আয়তন, রোদ ও পরিবেশ অনুযায়ী নিরাপদ ওজন, জিও ব্যাগ ও জৈব মাটির নিখুঁত হিসাব।'
            : 'Calculate safe structural load, Geo-Bag layout, and exact organic soil mix for your roof.'}
        </Text>

        {/* Roof Area Input */}
        <Text style={[styles.fieldLabel, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
          {lang === 'bn'
            ? 'ছাদের মোট আয়তন (বর্গফুট / Sq. Ft.):'
            : 'Total Roof Area (Sq. Ft.):'}
        </Text>
        <TextInput
          value={totalRoofSqFtInput}
          onChangeText={setTotalRoofSqFtInput}
          keyboardType="numeric"
          style={[
            styles.input,
            {
              backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
              color: theme.textPrimary,
              borderColor: theme.border,
            },
          ]}
          placeholder="600"
          placeholderTextColor={theme.textSecondary}
        />

        {/* Sunlight Exposure */}
        <Text style={[styles.fieldLabel, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
          {lang === 'bn'
            ? 'দৈনিক রোদের পরিমাণ (Sunlight):'
            : 'Daily Sunlight Exposure:'}
        </Text>
        <View style={styles.pillRow}>
          {(
            [
              {
                id: 'full_sun',
                bn: '☀️ পূর্ণ রোদ (৬+ ঘণ্টা)',
                en: '☀️ Full Sun (6+ hrs)',
              },
              {
                id: 'partial_shade',
                bn: '⛅ আংশিক ছায়া (৩-৫ ঘণ্টা)',
                en: '⛅ Partial Shade (3-5 hrs)',
              },
              {
                id: 'low_light',
                bn: '🌥️ কম আলো (ইনডোর/বারান্দা)',
                en: '🌥️ Low Light / Balcony',
              },
            ] as { id: SunlightExposure; bn: string; en: string }[]
          ).map((item) => {
            const active = sunlight === item.id;
            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={item.id}
                onPress={() => setSunlight(item.id)}
                style={[
                  styles.pill,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: active
                      ? theme.primary
                      : theme.surfaceElevated,
                    borderColor: active ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: active ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                    fontSize: 12,
                    fontWeight: '700',
                  }}
                >
                  {lang === 'bn' ? item.bn : item.en}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          })}
        </View>

        {/* Dust & Drought Toggles */}
        <Text style={[styles.fieldLabel, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
          {lang === 'bn'
            ? 'পরিবেশগত অবস্থা (Environmental Conditions):'
            : 'Environmental Conditions:'}
        </Text>
        <View style={styles.pillRow}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => setHasHighDust(!hasHighDust)}
            style={[
              styles.pill,
              {
                backgroundColor: hasHighDust
                  ? theme.primary
                  : theme.surfaceElevated,
                borderColor: hasHighDust ? theme.primary : theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={{
                color: hasHighDust ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: '700',
              }}
            >
              💨 {lang === 'bn' ? 'অতিরিক্ত ধুলোবালি আছে' : 'High Urban Dust'}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => setIsDroughtProne(!isDroughtProne)}
            style={[
              styles.pill,
              {
                backgroundColor: isDroughtProne
                  ? theme.primary
                  : theme.surfaceElevated,
                borderColor: isDroughtProne ? theme.primary : theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={{
                color: isDroughtProne ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: '700',
              }}
            >
              🔥 {lang === 'bn' ? 'খরা বা পানির স্বল্পতা' : 'Drought / Low Water'}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        {/* Plant Category Preferences */}
        <Text style={[styles.fieldLabel, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
          {lang === 'bn'
            ? 'পছন্দের গাছের ধরন:'
            : 'Preferred Plant Categories:'}
        </Text>
        <View style={styles.pillRow}>
          {(
            [
              { id: 'fruit', bn: '🥭 ফলজ', en: '🥭 Fruit' },
              { id: 'vegetable', bn: '🍅 সবজি', en: '🍅 Vegetable' },
              { id: 'flower', bn: '🌺 ফুল', en: '🌺 Flower' },
              { id: 'herb', bn: '🌿 মসলা ও ভেষজ', en: '🌿 Herb & Medicinal' },
            ] as { id: PlantCategory; bn: string; en: string }[]
          ).map((cat) => {
            const active = selectedCategories.includes(cat.id);
            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={cat.id}
                onPress={() => toggleCategory(cat.id)}
                style={[
                  styles.pill,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: active
                      ? theme.secondary
                      : theme.surfaceElevated,
                    borderColor: active ? theme.secondary : theme.border,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: active ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                    fontSize: 12,
                    fontWeight: '700',
                  }}
                >
                  {lang === 'bn' ? cat.bn : cat.en}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          })}
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Output Card: Structural Load, Geo-Bag Plan & Soil Mix Formula */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.card,
          { backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground, borderColor: theme.primary },
        ]}
      >
        <Text style={[styles.sectionTitle, { color: theme.primary }]}>
          📐{' '}
          {lang === 'bn'
            ? 'আপনার ছাদের কাস্টম বাগান ব্লুপ্রিন্ট'
            : 'Your Custom Rooftop Blueprint'}
        </Text>

        <View style={styles.metricsRow}>
          <View
            style={[
              styles.metricBox,
              { backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated },
            ]}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.metricValue, { color: theme.primary }]}>
              {planSummary.usableSqFt} sq.ft
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.metricLabel, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
              {lang === 'bn' ? 'ব্যবহারযোগ্য জায়গা (৬৫%)' : 'Usable Garden Area'}
            </Text>
          </View>

          <View
            style={[
              styles.metricBox,
              { backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated },
            ]}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.metricValue, { color: theme.success }]}>
              {planSummary.totalEstimatedWeightKg} kg
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.metricLabel, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? `নিরাপদ সীমা: ${planSummary.maxSafeLoadKg} kg`
                : `Safe Limit: ${planSummary.maxSafeLoadKg} kg`}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Geo-Bag Breakdown */}
        <Text style={[styles.subHeading, { color: theme.textPrimary }]}>
          🛍️{' '}
          {lang === 'bn'
            ? 'প্রস্তাবিত জিও ব্যাগ বিন্যাস (হালকা ও ছাদবান্ধব):'
            : 'Recommended Geo-Bag Allocation:'}
        </Text>
        {planSummary.geoBags.map((bag, idx) => (
          <View
            key={idx}
            style={[
              styles.bagRow,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  fontWeight: '800',
                  fontSize: 13,
                }}
              >
                {bag.bagSizeInches} × {bag.recommendedCount}{' '}
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'টি' : 'bags'}
              </Text>
              <Text style={{ color: theme.textSecondary, fontSize: 12 }}>
                {lang === 'bn' ? bag.suitablePlantsBn : bag.suitablePlantsEn}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <Text
              style={{ color: theme.primary, fontWeight: '800', fontSize: 12 }}
            >
              ~{bag.estimatedSoilWeightKg} kg
            </Text>
          </View>
        ))}

        {/* Custom Soil Mix Formula */}
        <View
          style={[
            styles.soilBox,
            {
              backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
              borderColor: theme.border,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <Text style={[styles.subHeading, { color: theme.textPrimary }]}>
            🧪{' '}
            {lang === 'bn'
              ? 'আদর্শ জৈব মাটি প্রস্তুতির ফর্মুলা (৪০:৩০:২০:১০):'
              : 'Ideal Organic Soil Mix Formula (40:30:20:10):'}
          </Text>
          <Text style={[styles.soilText, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
            • {lang === 'bn' ? 'দোআঁশ মাটি (৪০%):' : 'Loamy Soil (40%):'}{' '}
            <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800', color: theme.textPrimary }}>
              {planSummary.loamKg} kg
            </Text>
          </Text>
          <Text style={[styles.soilText, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
            •{' '}
            {lang === 'bn'
              ? 'ভার্মি কম্পোস্ট / পচা গোবর (৩০%):'
              : 'Vermicompost (30%):'}{' '}
            <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800', color: theme.textPrimary }}>
              {planSummary.compostKg} kg
            </Text>
          </Text>
          <Text style={[styles.soilText, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
            •{' '}
            {lang === 'bn'
              ? 'কোকোপিট - ছাদের ওজন কমাতে (২০%):'
              : 'Cocopeat - Lightweight (20%):'}{' '}
            <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800', color: theme.textPrimary }}>
              {planSummary.cocopeatKg} kg
            </Text>
          </Text>
          <Text style={[styles.soilText, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
            •{' '}
            {lang === 'bn'
              ? 'নিম খৈল, হাড়ের গুঁড়ো ও ট্রাইকোডার্মা (১০%):'
              : 'Neem Cake, Bone Meal & Trichoderma (10%):'}{' '}
            <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800', color: theme.textPrimary }}>
              {planSummary.bioAmendmentKg} kg
            </Text>
          </Text>
        </View>

        {/* Recommended Resilient Varieties */}
        <Text style={[styles.subHeading, { color: theme.textPrimary }]}>
          🌟{' '}
          {lang === 'bn'
            ? 'আপনার ছাদের আবহাওয়ায় সেরা গাছের জাত:'
            : 'Best Plant Varieties for Your Conditions:'}
        </Text>
        {planSummary.recommendedVarieties.map((v, i) => (
          <Text
            key={i}
            style={{ color: theme.textSecondary, fontSize: 13, lineHeight: 20 }}
          >
            {v}
          </Text>
        ))}
      </View>

      {/* 6-Stage End-to-End Practical Rooftop Gardening Lifecycle Guide */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.card,
          { backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground, borderColor: theme.border },
        ]}
      >
        <Text style={[styles.sectionTitle, { color: theme.textPrimary }]}>
          📋{' '}
          {lang === 'bn'
            ? 'শুরু থেকে ফলন: ৬ ধাপের ছাদবাগান গাইডলাইন'
            : '6-Stage End-to-End Rooftop Lifecycle Guide'}
        </Text>

        {ROOFTOP_LIFECYCLE_GUIDES.map((stage) => {
          const isDone = !!completedStages[stage.stageNumber];
          return (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={stage.stageNumber}
              onPress={() =>
                setCompletedStages((prev) => ({
                  ...prev,
                  [stage.stageNumber]: !prev[stage.stageNumber],
                }))
              }
              style={[
                styles.stageItem,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderColor: isDone ? theme.success : theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <View style={styles.stageHeaderRow}>
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: isDone ? theme.success : theme.primary,
                    fontWeight: '800',
                    fontSize: 14,
                    flex: 1,
                  }}
                >
                  {isDone ? '✅' : `০${stage.stageNumber}.`}{' '}
                  {lang === 'bn' ? stage.titleBengali : stage.titleEnglish}
                </Text>
                <Text
                  style={{
                    fontSize: 11,
                    color: isDone ? theme.success : theme.textSecondary,
                    fontWeight: '700',
                  }}
                >
                  {isDone
                    ? lang === 'bn'
                      ? 'সম্পন্ন'
                      : 'Completed'
                    : lang === 'bn'
                    ? 'ট্যাপ করে সম্পন্ন করুন'
                    : 'Tap to mark done'}
                </Text>
              </View>

              {(lang === 'bn'
                ? stage.checklistItemsBengali
                : stage.checklistItemsEnglish
              ).map((point, pIdx) => (
                <Text
                  key={pIdx}
                  style={{
                    color: theme.textSecondary,
                    fontSize: 12,
                    lineHeight: 19,
                    marginLeft: 6,
                  }}
                >
                  • {point}
                </Text>
              ))}
            </TouchableOpacity>
          );
        })}
      </View>
    </ScrollView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    width: '100%',
    maxWidth: 1000,
    alignSelf: 'center',
    padding: 20,
    gap: 16,
    paddingBottom: 36,
  },
  card: {
    padding: 16,
    borderRadius: 16,
    borderWidth: 1,
    gap: 12,
  },
  sectionTitle: {
    fontSize: 16,
    fontWeight: '800',
  },
  subText: {
    fontSize: 13,
    lineHeight: 19,
  },
  fieldLabel: {
    fontSize: 12,
    fontWeight: '700',
    marginTop: 4,
  },
  input: {
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderRadius: 10,
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 10,
    fontSize: 14,
    fontWeight: '700',
  },
  pillRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    flexWrap: 'wrap',
    gap: 8,
  },
  pill: {
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 9,
    borderRadius: 20,
    borderWidth: 1,
  },
  metricsRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    gap: 10,
  },
  metricBox: {
    flex: 1,
    padding: 12,
    borderRadius: 12,
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: 2,
  },
  metricValue: {
    fontSize: 18,
    fontWeight: '800',
  },
  metricLabel: {
    fontSize: 11,
    fontWeight: '600',
  },
  subHeading: {
    fontSize: 14,
    fontWeight: '800',
    marginTop: 4,
  },
  bagRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
    padding: 12,
    borderRadius: 10,
    borderWidth: 1,
  },

    padding: 12,

    fontSize: 12,

  },

    padding: 12,
    borderRadius: 12,
    borderWidth: 1,
    gap: 6,
  },
  stageHeaderRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
  },
});

// ##############################################################################
// FILE START: lib/screens/plant_profile/plant_profile_screen.tsx
