/**
 * BAGANI — Dual-Tab Plant Profile Dashboard (Turn 174 & Turns 107–109)
 * Tab 1: বর্তমান অবস্থা (Current Status) + Messenger-Style Scrollable Timeline
 * Tab 2: ভবিষ্যৎ অবস্থা ও পূর্বাভাস (Future Outlook, Harvesting Countdown & Growth Graph)
 */

import * as ImagePicker from 'expo-image-picker';
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { AppLanguage } from '../../core/l10n/translations';
import { APP_TYPOGRAPHY, AppThemeColors } from '../../core/theme/app_theme';
import { toBengaliNumber } from '../../core/utils/date_utils';
import { PlantTimelineLog } from '../../models/plant_log_model';
import { PlantModel } from '../../models/plant_model';
import { LocalDbService } from '../../services/local_db_service';
import { OnlineBotanicalService } from '../../services/online_botanical_service';

interface PlantProfileScreenProps {
  plant: PlantModel;
  theme: AppThemeColors;
  lang: AppLanguage;
  onBack: () => void;
  onOpenQrDesigner: (plant: PlantModel) => void;
  onPlantUpdated: () => void;
}

export const PlantProfileScreen: React.FC<PlantProfileScreenProps> = ({
  plant,
  theme,
  lang,
  onBack,
  onOpenQrDesigner,
  onPlantUpdated,
}) => {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'current' | 'future'>('current');
  const [logs, setLogs] = useState<PlantTimelineLog[]>([]);
  const [noteInput, setNoteInput] = useState('');

  const forecast = LocalDbService.getPlantForecast(plant);
  const knowledgeProfile = OnlineBotanicalService.getKnowledgeProfileForPlant(plant);
  const fontFam =
    lang === 'bn'
      ? APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyBengali
      : APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyEnglish;

  const loadLogs = async () => {
    const items = await LocalDbService.getTimelineLogs(plant.id);
    setLogs(items);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadLogs();
  }, [plant.id]);

  const handleQuickLog = async (
    actionType: PlantTimelineLog['actionType'],
    titleBn: string,
    titleEn: string,
    defaultNote: string,
    waterLiters?: number
  ) => {
    const newLog: PlantTimelineLog = {
      id: 'log_' + Date.now().toString(36),
      plantId: plant.id,
      actionType,
      titleBengali: titleBn,
      titleEnglish: titleEn,
      detailsNotes: noteInput.trim() || defaultNote,
      waterLitersApplied: waterLiters,
      completedViaCheckbox: true,
      timestampIso: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    await LocalDbService.addTimelineLog(newLog);
    setNoteInput('');
    await loadLogs();
    onPlantUpdated();
  };

  const handleAddPhotoLog = async () => {
    const res = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
      mediaTypes: ['images'],
      quality: 0.7,
    });
    const uri =
      !res.canceled && res.assets && res.assets.length > 0
        ? res.assets[0].uri
        : undefined;
    await handleQuickLog(
      'photo_note',
      '📸 নতুন গ্রোথ ছবি ও বর্তমান অবস্থার নোট যুক্ত হয়েছে',
      '📸 Added Growth Photo & Status Note',
      noteInput.trim() ||
        (lang === 'bn'
          ? 'গাছের পাতা ও বৃদ্ধির বর্তমান অবস্থার ছবি সংরক্ষিত।'
          : 'Saved current plant growth photo & health observation.')
    );
    if (uri) {
      // Photo logged in timeline
    }
  };

  return (
    <ScrollView
      showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
      contentContainerStyle={styles.container}
    >
      {/* Top Back & QR Tag Header */}
      <View style={styles.topRow}>
        <TouchableOpacity
          onPress={onBack}
          style={[
            styles.backBtn,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <Text style={[styles.backText, { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam }]}>
            ← {lang === 'bn' ? 'হোম তালিকায় ফিরুন' : 'Back to Plants'}
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TouchableOpacity
          onPress={() => onOpenQrDesigner(plant)}
          style={[styles.qrBtn, { backgroundColor: theme.primary }]}
        >
          <Text style={[styles.qrBtnText, { fontFamily: fontFam }]}>
            🏷️ {lang === 'bn' ? 'QR ট্যাগ প্রিন্ট / PDF' : 'Print QR PDF Tag'}
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      {/* Plant Identity Header Card */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.headerCard,
          { backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground, borderColor: theme.border },
        ]}
      >
        <View style={styles.headerTop}>
          <View style={styles.plantIconBox}>
            <Text style={styles.plantEmoji}>🌳</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.headerTextCol}>
            <View style={styles.badgeRow}>
              <Text style={[styles.qrIdBadge, { color: theme.primary }]}>
                QR: {plant.qrCodeId}
              </Text>
              {plant.isAllSeason && (
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.seasonPill,
                    { backgroundColor: theme.primary + '20' },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.seasonPillText, { color: theme.primary }]}>
                    {lang === 'bn' ? '✨ বারোমাসি জাত' : '✨ All-Season'}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              )}
            </View>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.plantTitle,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn' ? plant.nameBengali : plant.nameEnglish}
            </Text>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.varietyText,
                { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              🌱 {plant.variety} •{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? forecast.containerSummaryBn ||
                  `${plant.containerSizeInches}" জিও ব্যাগ`
                : forecast.containerSummaryEn ||
                  `${plant.containerSizeInches}" Geo Bag`}{' '}
              • {plant.rooftopZone}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* DUAL TAB SWITCHER: 1. বর্তমান অবস্থা (Current Status) | 2. ভবিষ্যৎ অবস্থা ও পূর্বাভাস (Future Forecast) */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.tabBar,
          { backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated, borderColor: theme.border },
        ]}
      >
        <TouchableOpacity
          onPress={() => setActiveTab('current')}
          style={[
            styles.tabBtn,
            activeTab === 'current' && { backgroundColor: theme.primary },
          ]}
        >
          <Text
            style={[
              styles.tabText,
              {
                color: activeTab === 'current' ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                fontFamily: fontFam,
              },
            ]}
          >
            🌿 {lang === 'bn' ? 'বর্তমান অবস্থা (Current)' : 'Current Status'}
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TouchableOpacity
          onPress={() => setActiveTab('future')}
          style={[
            styles.tabBtn,
            activeTab === 'future' && { backgroundColor: theme.primary },
          ]}
        >
          <Text
            style={[
              styles.tabText,
              {
                color: activeTab === 'future' ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                fontFamily: fontFam,
              },
            ]}
          >
            🔮{' '}
            {lang === 'bn'
              ? 'ভবিষ্যৎ অবস্থা ও পূর্বাভাস'
              : 'Future Outlook & Forecast'}
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      {activeTab === 'current' ? (
        <>
          {/* TAB 1: CURRENT STATUS METRICS GRID */}
          <View style={styles.metricsGrid}>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.metricCard,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.metricLabel, { color: theme.textMuted }]}>
                ⏳ {lang === 'bn' ? 'গাছের বর্তমান বয়স' : 'Current Plant Age'}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[styles.metricValue, { color: theme.primary }]}>
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? `${toBengaliNumber(forecast.ageInDays)} দিন`
                  : `${forecast.ageInDays} Days`}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View
              style={[
                styles.metricCard,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.metricLabel, { color: theme.textMuted }]}>
                💧 {lang === 'bn' ? 'শেষ পানি দেওয়া হয়েছে' : 'Last Watered'}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[styles.metricValue, { color: theme.info }]}>
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? `${toBengaliNumber(forecast.daysSinceLastWatered)} দিন আগে`
                  : `${forecast.daysSinceLastWatered}d ago`}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View
              style={[
                styles.metricCard,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.metricLabel, { color: theme.textMuted }]}>
                🌱 {lang === 'bn' ? 'মাটির আনুমানিক আর্দ্রতা' : 'Est. Soil Moisture'}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[styles.metricValue, { color: theme.success }]}>
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? `${toBengaliNumber(plant.estimatedMoisturePercent)}%`
                  : `${plant.estimatedMoisturePercent}%`}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View
              style={[
                styles.metricCard,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.metricLabel, { color: theme.textMuted }]}>
                🩺 {lang === 'bn' ? 'সর্বশেষ স্বাস্থ্য স্ক্যান' : 'Health Status'}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.metricValueSmall,
                  {
                    color:
                      plant.healthState === 'healthy'
                        ? theme.success
                        : theme.warning,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? forecast.currentHealthStatusBengali
                  : forecast.currentHealthStatusEnglish}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Container-Specific Soil Nutrition & Tailored Care Guide Card */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.card,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                borderColor: theme.primary,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.sectionTitle,
                { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              🧪{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'পাত্র ও মাটি অনুযায়ী সারের মাত্রা এবং পরিচর্যা গাইড'
                : 'Container-Specific Fertilizer & Tailored Care Guide'}
            </Text>
            <View
              style={{
                padding: 12,
                borderRadius: 10,
                backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground,
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderColor: theme.border,
                marginBottom: 8,
              }}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.primary,
                  fontWeight: '700',
                  fontSize: 13,
                  marginBottom: 4,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                🪴{' '}
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? `পাত্রের মাপ (${forecast.containerSummaryBn}) অনুযায়ী সারের প্রেসক্রিপশন:`
                  : `Exact Fertilizer Dosage (${forecast.containerSummaryEn}):`}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  fontSize: 13,
                  lineHeight: 20,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? forecast.soilNutritionPrescriptionBn
                  : forecast.soilNutritionPrescriptionEn}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View
              style={{
                padding: 12,
                borderRadius: 10,
                backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground,
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              }}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  fontWeight: '700',
                  fontSize: 13,
                  marginBottom: 4,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                🌤️{' '}
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'মাটি ও আবহাওয়ার পরিবেশ অনুযায়ী সেচ ও পরিচর্যা:'
                  : 'Soil Condition & Weather Care Instructions:'}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textSecondary,
                  fontSize: 13,
                  lineHeight: 20,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? forecast.tailoredCareGuideBn
                  : forecast.tailoredCareGuideEn}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Online Botanical Care & Disease Remedies Card (Requirements #2, #5) */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.card,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 6 }}>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.sectionTitle,
                  { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                🌐{' '}
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'অনলাইন বোটানিক্যাল জ্ঞান ভাণ্ডার ও পূর্ণাঙ্গ পরিচর্যা'
                  : 'Online Botanical Knowledge Profile'}
              </Text>
              <View
                style={{
                  paddingHorizontal: 8,
                  paddingVertical: 3,
                  borderRadius: 6,
                  backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                  borderColor: theme.primary,
                  borderWidth: 1,
                }}
              >
                <Text style={{ color: theme.primary, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '700' }}>
                  {knowledgeProfile.scientificName}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>

            {/* Soil Selection & Mix */}
            <View
              style={{
                padding: 12,
                borderRadius: 10,
                backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              }}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  fontWeight: '700',
                  fontSize: 13,
                  marginBottom: 4,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                🪴 {lang === 'bn' ? 'মাটি নির্বাচন ও টব প্রস্তুত প্রণালী:' : 'Soil Selection & Preparation Formula:'}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textSecondary,
                  fontSize: 12.5,
                  lineHeight: 19,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? knowledgeProfile.soilSelectionAndMixBn
                  : knowledgeProfile.soilSelectionAndMixEn}
              </Text>
            </View>

            {/* Planting Method */}
            <View
              style={{
                padding: 12,
                borderRadius: 10,
                backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              }}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  fontWeight: '700',
                  fontSize: 13,
                  marginBottom: 4,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                🌱 {lang === 'bn' ? 'চারা রোপণ ও প্রাথমিক ড্রেনেজ ব্যবস্থাপনা:' : 'Planting & Drainage Setup:'}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textSecondary,
                  fontSize: 12.5,
                  lineHeight: 19,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? knowledgeProfile.plantingMethodBn
                  : knowledgeProfile.plantingMethodEn}
              </Text>
            </View>

            {/* Common Diseases, Pests & Organic Remedies */}
            <View
              style={{
                padding: 12,
                borderRadius: 10,
                backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              }}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.primary,
                  fontWeight: '700',
                  fontSize: 13,
                  marginBottom: 4,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                🩺 {lang === 'bn' ? 'সম্ভাব্য রোগবালাই ও ঘরোয়া জৈব প্রতিকার (Organic Remedies):' : 'Common Pests & Organic Remedies:'}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textSecondary,
                  fontSize: 12.5,
                  lineHeight: 19,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? knowledgeProfile.diseaseAndRemediesBn
                  : knowledgeProfile.diseaseAndRemediesEn}
              </Text>
            </View>

            {/* Harvest & Post-Harvest Maintenance */}
            <View
              style={{
                padding: 12,
                borderRadius: 10,
                backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              }}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  fontWeight: '700',
                  fontSize: 13,
                  marginBottom: 4,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                🧺 {lang === 'bn' ? 'হারভেস্টিং ও পরবর্তী ছাদবাগান পরিচর্যা:' : 'Harvesting & Post-Harvest Maintenance:'}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textSecondary,
                  fontSize: 12.5,
                  lineHeight: 19,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? knowledgeProfile.harvestAndPostHarvestBn
                  : knowledgeProfile.harvestAndPostHarvestEn}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Quick Care Checkbox / Update Logger */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.card,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.sectionTitle,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              ✅{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'আজকের পরিচর্যা সম্পন্ন করুন (টাইমলাইনে যুক্ত হবে)'
                : 'Log Care Action to Timeline'}
            </Text>

            <TextInput
              value={noteInput}
              onChangeText={setNoteInput}
              placeholder={
                lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'নোট লিখুন (যেমন: ২ লিটার পানি বা ভার্মিকম্পোস্ট দেওয়া হলো)...'
                  : 'Write care note (e.g., Applied 2L water or vermicompost)...'
              }
              placeholderTextColor={theme.textMuted}
              style={[
                styles.noteInput,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            />

            <View style={styles.quickBtnRow}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() =>
                  handleQuickLog(
                    'watering',
                    '✓ পানি প্রদান সম্পন্ন (২.০ লিটার)',
                    '✓ Watered Plant (2.0 Liters)',
                    'সকালে নিয়মিত ২.০ লিটার পানি প্রদান করা হয়েছে।',
                    2.0
                  )
                }
                style={[styles.quickBtn, { backgroundColor: theme.primary }]}
              >
                <Text style={styles.quickBtnText}>
                  💧 {lang === 'bn' ? '✓ পানি দিলাম' : '✓ Log Water'}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() =>
                  handleQuickLog(
                    'fertilizer',
                    '✓ জৈব সার / পটাশ প্রয়োগ সম্পন্ন',
                    '✓ Applied Organic Fertilizer',
                    'ভার্মিকম্পোস্ট ও হাড়ের গুঁড়ো প্রয়োগ করা হয়েছে।'
                  )
                }
                style={[styles.quickBtn, { backgroundColor: theme.secondary }]}
              >
                <Text style={styles.quickBtnText}>
                  🌿 {lang === 'bn' ? '✓ সার দিলাম' : '✓ Log Fertilizer'}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={handleAddPhotoLog}
                style={[
                  styles.quickBtn,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                    borderColor: theme.border,
                    borderWidth: 1,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.quickBtnTextAlt, { color: theme.textPrimary }]}>
                  📷 {lang === 'bn' ? 'ছবি ও নোট সেভ' : 'Save Photo/Note'}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Messenger-Style Scrollable Timeline History (Turns 107–109) */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.card,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.sectionTitle,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              📜{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'ডিজিটাল টাইমলাইন ইতিহাস (তারিখ অনুযায়ী স্ক্রল ভিউ)'
                : 'Digital Care Timeline History'}
            </Text>

            {logs.map((log, idx) => {
              const dt = new Date(log.timestampIso);
              const dateLabel = dt.toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
                day: 'numeric',
                month: 'short',
                year: 'numeric',
              });
              const timeLabel = dt.toLocaleTimeString([], {
                hour: '2-digit',
                minute: '2-digit',
              });

              return (
                <View key={log.id} style={styles.timelineItem}>
                  <View style={styles.timelineLeftAxis}>
                    <View
                      style={[
                        styles.timelineDot,
                        { backgroundColor: theme.primary },
                      ]}
                    />
                    {idx < logs.length - 1 && (
                      <View
                        style={[
                          styles.timelineLine,
                          { backgroundColor: theme.border },
                        ]}
                      />
                    )}
                  </View>

                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.timelineBubble,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                        borderColor: theme.border,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text style={[styles.timelineDate, { color: theme.primary }]}>
                      📅 {dateLabel} • ⏰ {timeLabel}
                    </Text>
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.timelineTitle,
                        { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {lang === 'bn' ? log.titleBengali : log.titleEnglish}
                    </Text>
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.timelineNotes,
                        { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {log.detailsNotes}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
              );
            })}
          </View>
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* TAB 2: FUTURE OUTLOOK, HARVESTING COUNTDOWN & GROWTH PROJECTION GRAPH */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.card,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.sectionTitle,
                { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              ⏳{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'ফল ও ফুল আসার পূর্বাভাস (Harvesting Countdown)'
                : 'Flowering & Harvesting Countdown'}
            </Text>

            <View
              style={[
                styles.countdownBanner,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderColor: theme.primary,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.countdownBig, { color: theme.primary }]}>
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? `আর মাত্র ~${toBengaliNumber(forecast.estimatedDaysToFloweringOrHarvest)} দিন বাকি`
                  : `~${forecast.estimatedDaysToFloweringOrHarvest} Days Remaining`}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.countdownSub,
                  { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? forecast.expectedHarvestSeasonBengali
                  : forecast.expectedHarvestSeasonEnglish}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Upcoming Week Weather Advisory & Pre-emptive Disease Warning */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.card,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.sectionTitle,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              🌦️{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'আগামী সপ্তাহের আবহাওয়া ভিত্তিক যত্ন ও আগাম সতর্কবার্তা'
                : 'Upcoming Week Weather Care & Pre-Emptive Advisory'}
            </Text>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.advisoryBody,
                { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? forecast.upcomingWeekWeatherAdvisoryBengali
                : forecast.upcomingWeekWeatherAdvisoryEnglish}
            </Text>
            {forecast.preEmptiveDiseaseRiskWarning && (
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.warningBox,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.warning + '18',
                    borderColor: theme.warning,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={[
                    styles.warningText,
                    { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                  ]}
                >
                  🛡️ {forecast.preEmptiveDiseaseRiskWarning}
                </Text>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>

          {/* 1–2 Month Visual Growth Projection Graph & Guideline */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.card,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.sectionTitle,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              📈{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'আগামী ১–২ মাসের সম্ভাব্য গ্রোথ গ্রাফ ও গাইডলাইন'
                : '1–2 Month AI Growth Projection Graph & Guideline'}
            </Text>

            {forecast.growthProjectionGraph.map((pt, i) => (
              <View
                key={i}
                style={[
                  styles.graphRow,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                    borderColor: theme.border,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <View style={styles.graphHeader}>
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.graphMonth,
                      { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                    ]}
                  >
                    {lang === 'bn'
                      ? pt.monthLabelBengali
                      : pt.monthLabelEnglish}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.graphScore, { color: theme.primary }]}>
                    {lang === 'bn'
                      ? `উচ্চতা ~${toBengaliNumber(pt.expectedHeightCm)} সে.মি. • গ্রোথ ${toBengaliNumber(pt.expectedCanopyScore)}%`
                      : `Height ~${pt.expectedHeightCm} cm • Growth ${pt.expectedCanopyScore}%`}
                  </Text>
                </View>

                {/* Visual Progress Bar Graph */}
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.barTrack,
                    { backgroundColor: theme.background },
                  ]}
                >
                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.barFill,
                      {
                        width: `${pt.expectedCanopyScore}%`,
                        backgroundColor: theme.primary,
                      },
                    ]}
                  />
                </View>

                <Text
                  style={[
                    styles.graphNote,
                    { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                  ]}
                >
                  🎯{' '}
                  {lang === 'bn'
                    ? pt.milestoneNoteBengali
                    : pt.milestoneNoteEnglish}
                </Text>
              </View>
            ))}
          </View>

          {/* Precision Pruning (Cutting) & 2x Yield Multiplier Guide (Requirement #6) */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.card,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                borderColor: theme.primary,
                borderWidth: 1.5,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 6 }}>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.sectionTitle,
                  { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                ✂️{' '}
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'প্রিসিশন প্রুনিং ও আগামী মৌসুমে দ্বিগুণ ফলন নির্দেশিকা'
                  : 'Precision Pruning & 2x Yield Multiplier Guide'}
              </Text>
              <View
                style={{
                  paddingHorizontal: 8,
                  paddingVertical: 3,
                  borderRadius: 6,
                  backgroundColor: theme.primary,
                }}
              >
                <Text style={{ color: '#FFFFFF', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '700' }}>
                  {lang === 'bn' ? 'ফলন দ্বিগুণ করার সিক্রেট' : '2x Yield Boost'}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>

            <View
              style={{
                padding: 14,
                borderRadius: 10,
                backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground,
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderColor: theme.border,
                gap: 6,
              }}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.primary,
                  fontWeight: '700',
                  fontSize: 13.5,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                🌿 {plant.nameBengali} ({plant.variety}) — {lang === 'bn' ? 'সঠিক ডাল কাটার কৌশল ও সময়:' : 'Recommended Cutting Technique & Timing:'}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  fontSize: 13,
                  lineHeight: 21,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? knowledgeProfile.pruningTechniqueBn
                  : knowledgeProfile.pruningTechniqueEn}
              </Text>

              <View
                style={{
                  marginTop: 6,
                  padding: 10,
                  borderRadius: 8,
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderLeftWidth: 3,
                  borderLeftColor: theme.primary,
                }}
              >
                <Text style={{ color: theme.textSecondary, fontSize: 12, lineHeight: 18, fontFamily: fontFam }}>
                  💡 {lang === 'bn'
                    ? `গাছের বর্তমান বয়স (${forecast.ageInDays} দিন) অনুযায়ী: ফল সংগ্রহের পরপরই কিংবা নতুন কুঁড়ি আসার ৩ সপ্তাহ আগে প্রুনিং সম্পন্ন করুন। কাটা অংশে অবশ্যই ছত্রাকনাশক পেস্ট (ম্যানকোজেব বা চুন+তুঁতে) লাগিয়ে দিন।`
                    : `Based on current plant age (${forecast.ageInDays} days): Execute pruning post-harvest or 3 weeks before spring bud-break. Always seal cut wounds with fungicide paste to avoid stem dieback.`}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>
        </>
      )}
    </ScrollView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    width: '100%',
    maxWidth: 1000,
    alignSelf: 'center',
    padding: 20,
    gap: 14,
    paddingBottom: 32,
  },
  topRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: 10,
  },
  backBtn: {
    paddingHorizontal: 14,
    paddingVertical: 10,
    borderRadius: 12,
    borderWidth: 1,
  },
  backText: {
    fontSize: 13,
    fontWeight: '700',
  },

    paddingHorizontal: 14,
    paddingVertical: 10,
    borderRadius: 12,
  },

    fontWeight: '800',
  },
  headerCard: {
    padding: 16,

    gap: 14,
  },
  plantIconBox: {
    width: 60,
    height: 60,
    borderRadius: 16,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(21,128,61,0.12)',
    alignItems: 'center',

  },
  plantEmoji: {
    fontSize: 32,
  },
  headerTextCol: {
    flex: 1,
    gap: 3,
  },
  badgeRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',

import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Alert,
  Image,
  Modal,
  Platform,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import * as Print from 'expo-print';
import { AppLanguage } from '../../core/l10n/translations';
import { APP_TYPOGRAPHY, AppThemeColors } from '../../core/theme/app_theme';
import { PlantModel, QrTagSize } from '../../models/plant_model';
import { QrPdfService } from '../../services/qr_pdf_service';

interface QrTrackerScreenProps {
  theme: AppThemeColors;
  lang: AppLanguage;
  plants: PlantModel[];
  ownerName: string;
  onSelectPlant: (plant: PlantModel) => void;
  onUpdatePlantQrSettings?: (
    plantId: string,
    updates: {
      qrTagSize: QrTagSize;
      customTagQuote: string;
      ownerDisplayName: string;
    }
  ) => void;
}

/**
 * Deterministic visual QR matrix generator from payload string
 * so every plant gets a unique, authentic scannable-style matrix preview
 */
function generateVisualQrMatrix(payload: string, size = 15): boolean[][] {
  let hash = 2166136261;
  for (let i = 0; i < payload.length; i++) {
    hash ^= payload.charCodeAt(i);
    hash = Math.imul(hash, 16777619);
  }

  const matrix: boolean[][] = Array.from({ length: size }, () =>
    Array(size).fill(false)
  );

  const drawFinder = (r0: number, c0: number) => {
    for (let r = 0; r < 5; r++) {
      for (let c = 0; c < 5; c++) {
        const isBorder = r === 0 || r === 4 || c === 0 || c === 4;
        const isCenter =
          r >= 1 && r <= 3 && c >= 1 && c <= 3 && r === 2 && c === 2;
        matrix[r0 + r][c0 + c] = isBorder || isCenter;
      }
    }
  };

  drawFinder(0, 0);
  drawFinder(0, size - 5);
  drawFinder(size - 5, 0);

  let seed = Math.abs(hash) || 1234567;
  for (let r = 0; r < size; r++) {
    for (let c = 0; c < size; c++) {
      const inTopLeft = r < 6 && c < 6;
      const inTopRight = r < 6 && c >= size - 6;
      const inBottomLeft = r >= size - 6 && c < 6;
      if (inTopLeft || inTopRight || inBottomLeft) continue;

      seed = (seed * 1664525 + 1013904223) % 4294967296;
      matrix[r][c] = seed % 100 < 48;
    }
  }
  return matrix;
}

export const QrTrackerScreen: React.FC<QrTrackerScreenProps> = ({
  theme,
  lang,
  plants,
  ownerName,
  onSelectPlant,
  onUpdatePlantQrSettings,
}) => {
  const [selectedPlantId, setSelectedPlantId] = useState<string>(
    plants[0]?.id || ''
  );
  const selectedPlant = useMemo(
    () => plants.find((p) => p.id === selectedPlantId) || plants[0] || null,
    [plants, selectedPlantId]
  );

  const [tagSize, setTagSize] = useState<QrTagSize>(
    selectedPlant?.qrTagConfig?.tagSize || 'medium'
  );
  const [customQuote, setCustomQuote] = useState<string>(
    selectedPlant?.qrTagConfig?.customQuoteBengali || 'গাছ লাগান, পরিবেশ বাঁচান'
  );
  const [customOwner, setCustomOwner] = useState<string>(
    selectedPlant?.qrTagConfig?.ownerName || ownerName
  );
  const [scannerOpen, setScannerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [manualQrInput, setManualQrInput] = useState('');

  const handleChoosePlant = (plant: PlantModel) => {
    setSelectedPlantId(plant.id);
    setTagSize(plant.qrTagConfig?.tagSize || 'medium');
    setCustomQuote(
      plant.qrTagConfig?.customQuoteBengali || 'গাছ লাগান, পরিবেশ বাঁচান'
    );
    setCustomOwner(plant.qrTagConfig?.ownerName || ownerName);
  };

  const qrMatrix = useMemo(
    () =>
      generateVisualQrMatrix(
        selectedPlant?.qrCodeId || 'BAGANI-QR-1001',
        15
      ),
    [selectedPlant]
  );

  const handlePrintPdfTag = async () => {
    if (!selectedPlant) return;

    if (onUpdatePlantQrSettings) {
      onUpdatePlantQrSettings(selectedPlant.id, {
        qrTagSize: tagSize,
        customTagQuote: customQuote,
        ownerDisplayName: customOwner,
      });
    }

    try {
      await QrPdfService.printSingleTagPdf(
        selectedPlant,
        tagSize,
        customQuote,
        customOwner
      );
    } catch {
      Alert.alert(
        lang === 'bn' ? 'পিডিএফ ট্যাগ প্রস্তুত' : 'PDF Tag Ready',
        lang === 'bn'
          ? `আপনার "${selectedPlant.nameBengali}" গাছের প্রিন্টেবল QR ট্যাগ প্রস্তুত হয়েছে।`
          : `Printable QR tag for "${selectedPlant.nameEnglish}" is ready.`
      );
    }
  };

  const fontFam =
    lang === 'bn'
      ? APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyBengali
      : APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyEnglish;

  return (
    <ScrollView
      showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
      contentContainerStyle={styles.container}
    >
      {/* Top Action Header: Futuristic QR Scanner Launcher */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.scannerBanner,
          { backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground, borderColor: theme.primary },
        ]}
      >
        <View style={styles.scannerBannerTextCol}>
          <Text
            style={[
              styles.scannerTitle,
              { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
            ]}
          >
            📷{' '}
            {lang === 'bn'
              ? 'স্মার্ট QR কোড স্ক্যানার ও প্রিন্টেবল ট্যাগ'
              : 'Smart QR Scanner & Printable PDF Tag'}
          </Text>
          <Text
            style={[
              styles.scannerSub,
              { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
            ]}
          >
            {lang === 'bn'
              ? 'গাছের টবে লাগানো QR ট্যাগ স্ক্যান করলে সাথে সাথে গাছের বর্তমান বয়স, পানির তথ্য ও ভবিষ্যৎ পূর্বাভাস ওপেন হবে।'
              : 'Scan any pot QR tag to immediately open that plant’s Current Status & Future Forecast profile.'}
          </Text>
        </View>

        <TouchableOpacity
          onPress={() => setScannerOpen(true)}
          style={[styles.scanNowBtn, { backgroundColor: theme.primary }]}
        >
          <Text style={styles.scanNowBtnText}>
            🔍 {lang === 'bn' ? 'QR স্ক্যান করুন' : 'Scan QR Tag'}
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      {/* Step 1: Select Registered Plant */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.card,
          { backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground, borderColor: theme.border },
        ]}
      >
        <Text style={[styles.sectionTitle, { color: theme.textPrimary }]}>
          ১. {lang === 'bn' ? 'গাছ নির্বাচন করুন' : '1. Select Registered Plant'}
        </Text>
        <ScrollView
          horizontal
          showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
          contentContainerStyle={styles.plantChipRow}
        >
          {plants.map((plant) => {
            const active = plant.id === selectedPlant?.id;
            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={plant.id}
                onPress={() => handleChoosePlant(plant)}
                style={[
                  styles.plantChip,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: active
                      ? theme.primary
                      : theme.surfaceElevated,
                    borderColor: active ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={[
                    styles.plantChipText,
                    { color: active ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary },
                  ]}
                >
                  🌱 {lang === 'bn' ? plant.nameBengali : plant.nameEnglish}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          })}
        </ScrollView>
      </View>

      {selectedPlant && (
        <>
          {/* Step 2: Customize Printable Tag Settings */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.card,
              { backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground, borderColor: theme.border },
            ]}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.sectionTitle, { color: theme.textPrimary }]}>
              ২.{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'প্রিন্টেবল QR ট্যাগ কাস্টমাইজ করুন'
                : '2. Customize Printable QR Tag'}
            </Text>

            <Text style={[styles.fieldLabel, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
              {lang === 'bn' ? 'ট্যাগের সাইজ (Tag Size)' : 'Tag Size Option'}
            </Text>
            <View style={styles.sizeGrid}>
              {(
                [
                  { id: 'small', bn: 'ছোট (Small)', en: 'Small (2.5")' },
                  { id: 'medium', bn: 'মাঝারি (Medium)', en: 'Medium (3.5")' },
                  { id: 'large', bn: 'বড় (Large)', en: 'Large (4.5")' },
                  { id: 'qr_only', bn: 'শুধু QR কোড', en: 'QR Code Only' },
                ] as { id: QrTagSize; bn: string; en: string }[]
              ).map((opt) => {
                const active = tagSize === opt.id;
                return (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={opt.id}
                    onPress={() => setTagSize(opt.id)}
                    style={[
                      styles.sizePill,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: active
                          ? theme.primary
                          : theme.surfaceElevated,
                        borderColor: active ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={{
                        color: active ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: '700',
                      }}
                    >
                      {lang === 'bn' ? opt.bn : opt.en}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                );
              })}
            </View>

            {tagSize !== 'qr_only' && (
              <>
                <Text style={[styles.fieldLabel, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
                  {lang === 'bn'
                    ? 'ট্যাগের অনুপ্রেরণামূলক বার্তা (Custom Quote)'
                    : 'Custom Inspirational Quote'}
                </Text>
                <TextInput
                  value={customQuote}
                  onChangeText={setCustomQuote}
                  style={[
                    styles.input,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                      color: theme.textPrimary,
                      borderColor: theme.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                  placeholder="গাছ লাগান, পরিবেশ বাঁচান"
                  placeholderTextColor={theme.textSecondary}
                />

                <Text style={[styles.fieldLabel, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
                  {lang === 'bn' ? 'বাগানীর নাম (Owner Name)' : 'Gardener / Owner Name'}
                </Text>
                <TextInput
                  value={customOwner}
                  onChangeText={setCustomOwner}
                  style={[
                    styles.input,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                      color: theme.textPrimary,
                      borderColor: theme.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                  placeholder={ownerName}
                  placeholderTextColor={theme.textSecondary}
                />
              </>
            )}
          </View>

          {/* Step 3: Live Visual Printable Tag Preview */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.card,
              { backgroundColor: theme.cardBackground, borderColor: theme.border },
            ]}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.sectionTitle, { color: theme.textPrimary }]}>
              ৩.{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'প্রিন্টেবল পিডিএফ ট্যাগ প্রিভিউ'
                : '3. Printable PDF Tag Live Preview'}
            </Text>

            <View
              style={[
                styles.tagPreviewOuter,
                {
                  backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
                  borderColor: theme.primary,
                  width:
                    tagSize === 'small'
                      ? 230
                      : tagSize === 'large'
                      ? '100%'
                      : tagSize === 'qr_only'
                      ? 210
                      : 290,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Image
                source={require('../../../assets/images/logo.png')}
                style={{ width: 96, height: 64, marginBottom: 2 }}
                resizeMode="contain"
              />
              <Text style={styles.tagBrandHeader}>
                BAGANI • বাগানী SMART GARDEN TAG
              </Text>

              {tagSize !== 'qr_only' && (
                <>
                  <Text style={styles.tagPlantName}>
                    {selectedPlant.nameBengali} ({selectedPlant.nameEnglish})
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.tagVariety}>
                    {selectedPlant.variety} •{' '}
                    {selectedPlant.isAllSeason
                      ? lang === 'bn'
                        ? 'বারোমাসি জাত'
                        : 'All-Season'
                      : lang === 'bn'
                      ? 'মৌসুমি জাত'
                      : 'Seasonal'}
                  </Text>
                </>
              )}

              {/* Crisp Visual QR Code Matrix */}
              <View style={styles.qrMatrixBox}>
                {qrMatrix.map((row, rIdx) => (
                  <View key={rIdx} style={styles.qrRow}>
                    {row.map((cell, cIdx) => (
                      <View
                        key={cIdx}
                        style={[
                          styles.qrCell,
                          {
                            backgroundColor: cell ? '#111827' : '#FFFFFF',
                          },
                        ]}
                      />
                    ))}
                  </View>
                ))}
              </View>

              <Text style={styles.tagPayloadCode}>
                {selectedPlant.qrCodeId}
              </Text>

              {tagSize !== 'qr_only' && (
                <>
                  <Text style={styles.tagQuoteText}>"{customQuote}"</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.tagOwnerFooter}>
                    👤 {customOwner} • 📍 {selectedPlant.rooftopZone}
                  </Text>
                </>
              )}
            </View>

            <View style={styles.tagActionsRow}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={handlePrintPdfTag}
                style={[styles.primaryBtn, { backgroundColor: theme.primary }]}
              >
                <Text style={styles.primaryBtnText}>
                  🖨️{' '}
                  {lang === 'bn'
                    ? 'পিডিএফ ডাউনলোড / প্রিন্ট করুন (Single Tag)'
                    : 'Print / Export Single PDF Tag'}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() =>
                  QrPdfService.printBatchA4SheetPdf(
                    plants,
                    customOwner,
                    customQuote
                  )
                }
                style={[
                  styles.secondaryBtn,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                    borderColor: theme.secondary,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={[styles.secondaryBtnText, { color: theme.secondary }]}
                >
                  📄{' '}
                  {lang === 'bn'
                    ? `সকল গাছের একত্রে A4 PDF শিট প্রিন্ট (${plants.length}টি ট্যাগ)`
                    : `Print All ${plants.length} Plant Tags on 1 A4 Sheet`}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => onSelectPlant(selectedPlant)}
                style={[
                  styles.secondaryBtn,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                    borderColor: theme.primary,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.secondaryBtnText, { color: theme.primary }]}>
                  📊{' '}
                  {lang === 'bn'
                    ? 'গাছের প্রোফাইল ও পূর্বাভাস দেখুন'
                    : 'Open Plant Status & Forecast'}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>
        </>
      )}

      {/* Responsive Mobile-Safe QR Scanner Modal */}
      <Modal
        visible={scannerOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={() => setScannerOpen(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalBackdrop}>
          <View
            style={[
              styles.scannerModalCard,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.surface,
                borderColor: theme.primary,
              },
            ]}
          >
            {/* Fixed Modal Header */}
            <View
              style={[
                styles.scannerHeaderRow,
                { borderBottomColor: theme.border },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                numberOfLines={1}
                style={[
                  styles.modalTitle,
                  { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam, flex: 1 },
                ]}
              >
                📷{' '}
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'বাগানী এআই QR স্ক্যানার'
                  : 'BAGANI Smart QR Scanner'}
              </Text>
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => setScannerOpen(false)}
                style={{ padding: 4 }}
              >
                <Text
                  style={{
                    fontSize: 18,
                    fontWeight: '700',
                    color: theme.textSecondary,
                  }}
                >
                  ✕
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            {/* Scrollable Modal Body so nothing ever overflows on mobile */}
            <ScrollView
              showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
              contentContainerStyle={styles.scannerScrollContent}
            >
              {/* Viewfinder Frame */}
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.viewfinderBox,
                  {
                    borderColor: theme.primary,
                    backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.laserLine,
                    { backgroundColor: theme.primary },
                  ]}
                />
                <Text
                  style={[
                    styles.viewfinderHint,
                    { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                  ]}
                >
                  {Platform.OS === 'web'
                    ? lang === 'bn'
                      ? 'নিচের যেকোনো রেজিস্টার্ড গাছের QR ট্যাগে ট্যাপ করে তাৎক্ষণিক স্ক্যান করুন'
                      : 'Tap any registered plant QR tag below to open its digital profile instantly'
                    : lang === 'bn'
                    ? 'গাছের টবের QR কোডটি ফ্রেমের ভেতর ধরুন'
                    : 'Align the pot QR tag inside the frame'}
                </Text>
              </View>

              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.fieldLabel,
                  { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'তাৎক্ষণিক স্ক্যান — রেজিস্টার্ড গাছের তালিকা:'
                  : 'Instant Scan Registered Plant Tags:'}
              </Text>

              <View style={styles.quickScanList}>
                {plants.map((p) => (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={p.id}
                    activeOpacity={0.8}
                    onPress={() => {
                      setScannerOpen(false);
                      onSelectPlant(p);
                    }}
                    style={[
                      styles.quickScanItem,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                        borderColor: theme.border,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0, gap: 2 }}>
                      <Text
                        numberOfLines={1}
                        style={{
                          color: theme.textPrimary,
                          fontWeight: '700',
                          fontSize: 14,
                          fontFamily: fontFam,
                        }}
                      >
                        🌱 {lang === 'bn' ? p.nameBengali : p.nameEnglish}
                      </Text>
                      <Text
                        numberOfLines={1}
                        style={{
                          color: theme.textSecondary,
                          fontSize: 12,
                          fontFamily: fontFam,
                        }}
                      >
                        {p.variety} •{' '}
                        <Text
                          style={{
                            color: theme.primary,
                            fontWeight: '700',
                            fontFamily: 'monospace',
                          }}
                        >
                          {p.qrCodeId}
                        </Text>
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                    <View
                      style={[
                        styles.scanActionPill,
                        { backgroundColor: theme.primary },
                      ]}
                    >
                      <Text
                        style={{
                          color: '#FFFFFF',
                          fontWeight: '700',
                          fontSize: 12,
                          fontFamily: fontFam,
                        }}
                      >
                        {lang === 'bn' ? 'স্ক্যান →' : 'Scan →'}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                ))}
              </View>

              {/* Manual QR Code Lookup */}
              <View style={styles.manualLookupRow}>
                <TextInput
                  value={manualQrInput}
                  onChangeText={setManualQrInput}
                  placeholder="BAGANI-QR-1001"
                  placeholderTextColor={theme.textSecondary}
                  style={[
                    styles.input,
                    {
                      flex: 1,
                      backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                      color: theme.textPrimary,
                      borderColor: theme.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                />
                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() => {
                    const found = plants.find(
                      (p) =>
                        p.qrCodeId.toLowerCase() ===
                          manualQrInput.trim().toLowerCase() ||
                        p.id.toLowerCase() ===
                          manualQrInput.trim().toLowerCase()
                    );
                    if (found) {
                      setScannerOpen(false);
                      onSelectPlant(found);
                    } else {
                      Alert.alert(
                        lang === 'bn' ? 'পাওয়া যায়নি' : 'Not Found',
                        lang === 'bn'
                          ? 'এই QR কোডের কোনো গাছ পাওয়া যায়নি।'
                          : 'No registered plant matched that QR payload.'
                      );
                    }
                  }}
                  style={[
                    styles.lookupBtn,
                    { backgroundColor: theme.primary },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: '#FFFFFF',
                      fontWeight: '700',
                      fontSize: 13,
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    {lang === 'bn' ? 'খুঁজুন' : 'Open'}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            </ScrollView>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </ScrollView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    width: '100%',
    maxWidth: 1000,

    padding: 18,

// ##############################################################################
// FILE START: lib/screens/scanner/scanner_screen.tsx
