/**
 * BAGANI — AI Plant Disease Diagnosis & QR Scan Hub (Section 7 of DESIGN_SYSTEM.md)
 * Includes:
 * - Camera capture & Gallery upload
 * - Image preview
 * - Diagnosis progress indicator
 * - Disease name & Confidence level
 * - Symptoms, Suggested treatment & Prevention recommendations
 * - Explicit non-guaranteed AI botanical disclaimer
 * - Quick toggle to QR Code Scanner & Printable PDF Tag Designer
 */

import * as ImagePicker from 'expo-image-picker';
import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Alert,
  Image,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { AppLanguage } from '../../core/l10n/translations';
import {
  APP_TYPOGRAPHY,
  AppThemeColors,
  DESIGN_TOKENS,
} from '../../core/theme/app_theme';
import { PlantHealthState, PlantModel } from '../../models/plant_model';
import {
  AI_SCAN_KNOWLEDGE_BASE,
  ScanMode,
} from '../../services/ai_scanner_service';

interface ScannerScreenProps {
  theme: AppThemeColors;
  lang: AppLanguage;
  plants: PlantModel[];
  onSaveScanToPlant?: (
    plantId: string,
    diagnosisBn: string,
    diagnosisEn: string,
    remedyBn: string,
    remedyEn: string,
    healthState: PlantHealthState
  ) => void;
  onOpenQrScannerTab?: () => void;
}

export const ScannerScreen: React.FC<ScannerScreenProps> = ({
  theme,
  lang,
  plants,
  onSaveScanToPlant,
  onOpenQrScannerTab,
}) => {
  const [scanMode, setScanMode] = useState<ScanMode>('diagnose');
  const [selectedSampleId, setSelectedSampleId] = useState<string>(
    'sample_mealybug'
  );
  const [targetPlantId, setTargetPlantId] = useState<string>(
    plants[0]?.id || ''
  );
  const [capturedImageUri, setCapturedImageUri] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [analysisProgress, setAnalysisProgress] = useState<number>(100);

  // Interactive Symptom Q&A State
  const [qWhiteCotton, setQWhiteCotton] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [qLeafCurl, setQLeafCurl] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [qYellowBrownSpot, setQYellowBrownSpot] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const fontFam =
    lang === 'bn'
      ? APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyBengali
      : APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyEnglish;

  const activeResult = useMemo(() => {
    const byId = AI_SCAN_KNOWLEDGE_BASE.find((s) => s.id === selectedSampleId);
    if (byId && byId.mode === scanMode) return byId;
    return AI_SCAN_KNOWLEDGE_BASE.find((s) => s.mode === scanMode)!;
  }, [scanMode, selectedSampleId]);

  const handleSymptomSelect = (
    whiteCotton: boolean,
    leafCurl: boolean,
    spot: boolean
  ) => {
    setQWhiteCotton(whiteCotton);
    setQLeafCurl(leafCurl);
    setQYellowBrownSpot(spot);
    setAnalysisProgress(100);

    if (whiteCotton) {
      setSelectedSampleId('sample_mealybug');
    } else if (leafCurl) {
      setSelectedSampleId('sample_leaf_curl');
    } else if (spot) {
      setSelectedSampleId('sample_fungal_spot');
    }
  };

  const handleCameraCapture = async () => {
    const res = await ImagePicker.launchCameraAsync({
      mediaTypes: ['images'],
      quality: 0.75,
    });
    if (!res.canceled && res.assets && res.assets.length > 0) {
      setCapturedImageUri(res.assets[0].uri);
      setAnalysisProgress(100);
    }
  };

  const handleGalleryUpload = async () => {
    const res = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
      mediaTypes: ['images'],
      quality: 0.75,
    });
    if (!res.canceled && res.assets && res.assets.length > 0) {
      setCapturedImageUri(res.assets[0].uri);
      setAnalysisProgress(100);
    }
  };

  return (
    <ScrollView
      style={{ flex: 1, width: '100%' }}
      showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
      contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollOuter}
    >
      <View style={styles.container}>
        {/* Top Mode Switcher: AI Disease Diagnosis | Species Identifier | QR Code Scanner */}
        <View
          style={[
            styles.mainCard,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={styles.headerRow}>
            <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 200 }}>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.sectionHeading,
                  { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                🔬{' '}
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'এআই প্ল্যান্ট ডায়াগনোসিস ও স্ক্যানার'
                  : 'AI Plant Diagnosis & Scanner'}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.captionText,
                  { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'পাতার ছবি ও লক্ষণ বিশ্লেষণ করে রোগ নির্ণয় এবং ঘরোয়া প্রতিকার'
                  : 'Image-based plant disease detection, symptoms & organic remedies'}
              </Text>
            </View>

            {onOpenQrScannerTab && (
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={onOpenQrScannerTab}
                style={[
                  styles.qrSwitchPill,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                    borderColor: theme.primary,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: theme.primary,
                    fontSize: 13,
                    fontWeight: '700',
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  }}
                >
                  📷 {lang === 'bn' ? 'QR ট্যাগ স্ক্যান' : 'QR Tag Scan'}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            )}
          </View>

          <View style={styles.modeRow}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => {
                setScanMode('diagnose');
                setSelectedSampleId('sample_mealybug');
              }}
              style={[
                styles.modeBtn,
                {
                  backgroundColor:
                    scanMode === 'diagnose'
                      ? theme.primary
                      : theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderColor:
                    scanMode === 'diagnose' ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color:
                    scanMode === 'diagnose' ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                  fontWeight: '700',
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                🩺 {lang === 'bn' ? 'রোগ ও পোকা নির্ণয়' : 'Disease Diagnosis'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => {
                setScanMode('identify');
                setSelectedSampleId('sample_katimon_id');
              }}
              style={[
                styles.modeBtn,
                {
                  backgroundColor:
                    scanMode === 'identify'
                      ? theme.primary
                      : theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderColor:
                    scanMode === 'identify' ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color:
                    scanMode === 'identify' ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                  fontWeight: '700',
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                🌿 {lang === 'bn' ? 'জাত শনাক্তকরণ' : 'Species Identifier'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          {/* Camera Capture & Gallery Upload Buttons + Image Preview */}
          <View style={styles.captureRow}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={handleCameraCapture}
              style={[
                styles.captureBtn,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.buttonText,
                  { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                📸 {lang === 'bn' ? 'ক্যামেরায় ছবি তুলুন' : 'Camera Capture'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={handleGalleryUpload}
              style={[
                styles.captureBtn,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.buttonText,
                  { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                🖼️ {lang === 'bn' ? 'গ্যালারি আপলোড' : 'Gallery Upload'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          {capturedImageUri && (
            <View
              style={[
                styles.previewBox,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Image
                source={{ uri: capturedImageUri }}
                style={styles.previewImg}
                resizeMode="cover"
              />
            </View>
          )}

          {/* Diagnosis Progress Bar */}
          <View style={styles.progressSection}>
            <View style={styles.progressHeader}>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.captionText,
                  { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'ডায়াগনোসিস বিশ্লেষণ অগ্রগতি'
                  : 'Diagnosis Analysis Progress'}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.captionText,
                  { color: theme.primary, fontWeight: '700' },
                ]}
              >
                {analysisProgress}%
              </Text>
            </View>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.progressTrack,
                { backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated },
              ]}
            >
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.progressFill,
                  {
                    width: `${analysisProgress}%`,
                    backgroundColor: theme.primary,
                  },
                ]}
              />
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Interactive Symptom Selector */}
        {scanMode === 'diagnose' ? (
          <View
            style={[
              styles.mainCard,
              { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.cardHeading,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              🔍{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'পাতার লক্ষণ নির্বাচন করুন (Symptom Selection)'
                : 'Select Observed Leaf Symptoms'}
            </Text>

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => handleSymptomSelect(true, false, false)}
              style={[
                styles.symptomItem,
                {
                  backgroundColor: qWhiteCotton
                    ? theme.selectedSurface
                    : theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderColor: qWhiteCotton ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 16 }}>
                {qWhiteCotton ? '✅' : '⬜'}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.bodyText,
                  { color: theme.textPrimary, flex: 1, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'পাতার নিচে বা ডগায় সাদা তুলোর মতো পোকা ও পিঁপড়া দেখা যাচ্ছে'
                  : 'White cottony insects and ants under leaves or tender shoots'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => handleSymptomSelect(false, true, false)}
              style={[
                styles.symptomItem,
                {
                  backgroundColor: qLeafCurl
                    ? theme.selectedSurface
                    : theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderColor: qLeafCurl ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 16 }}>{qLeafCurl ? '✅' : '⬜'}</Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.bodyText,
                  { color: theme.textPrimary, flex: 1, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'কচি পাতা নৌকার মতো কুঁকড়ে ও শক্ত হয়ে যাচ্ছে'
                  : 'Young leaves curling upward/downward and stiffening'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => handleSymptomSelect(false, false, true)}
              style={[
                styles.symptomItem,
                {
                  backgroundColor: qYellowBrownSpot
                    ? theme.selectedSurface
                    : theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderColor: qYellowBrownSpot ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 16 }}>
                {qYellowBrownSpot ? '✅' : '⬜'}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.bodyText,
                  { color: theme.textPrimary, flex: 1, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'পাতায় হলুদ বলয়সহ কালচে-বাদামী ছত্রাকের দাগ দেখা যাচ্ছে'
                  : 'Dark brown/black fungal spots with yellow halos on leaves'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        ) : (
          <View
            style={[
              styles.mainCard,
              { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.cardHeading,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              🌿{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'প্রজাতি নমুনা নির্বাচন করুন'
                : 'Select Species Sample'}
            </Text>
            <View style={styles.modeRow}>
              {AI_SCAN_KNOWLEDGE_BASE.filter((s) => s.mode === 'identify').map(
                (sp) => {
                  const active = selectedSampleId === sp.id;
                  return (
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      key={sp.id}
                      onPress={() => setSelectedSampleId(sp.id)}
                      style={[
                        styles.modeBtn,
                        {
                          backgroundColor: active
                            ? theme.selectedSurface
                            : theme.surfaceElevated,
                          borderColor: active ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                        },
                      ]}
                    >
                      <Text
                        style={{
                          color: active ? theme.primary : theme.textPrimary,
                          fontWeight: '700',
                          fontSize: 13,
                          fontFamily: fontFam,
                        }}
                      >
                        {lang === 'bn' ? sp.titleBn.split('(')[0] : sp.titleEn}
                      </Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  );
                }
              )}
            </View>
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Diagnosis Results Card (Screen 10 of DESIGN_SYSTEM.md) */}
        <View
          style={[
            styles.mainCard,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={styles.resultHeaderRow}>
            <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 180 }}>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.captionText,
                  { color: theme.primary, fontWeight: '700' },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'ডায়াগনোসিস ফলাফল' : 'DIAGNOSIS RESULT'}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.cardHeading,
                  { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? activeResult.titleBn : activeResult.titleEn}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={[styles.captionText, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}
              >
                {activeResult.scientificName}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View
              style={[
                styles.confidenceBadge,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                  borderColor: theme.primary,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.primary,
                  fontWeight: '700',
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
              >
                ✅ {activeResult.confidence}% Confidence
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Symptoms */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.infoBlock,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.cardHeading,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontSize: 14, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              🔎{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'সনাক্তকৃত লক্ষণসমূহ (Symptoms)'
                : 'Observed Symptoms'}
            </Text>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.bodyText,
                { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? activeResult.diagnosisBn
                : activeResult.diagnosisEn}
            </Text>
          </View>

          {/* Suggested Treatment */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.infoBlock,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.cardHeading,
                { color: theme.primary, fontSize: 14, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              💊{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'পরামর্শকৃত জৈব চিকিৎসা (Suggested Treatment)'
                : 'Suggested Organic Treatment'}
            </Text>
            {(lang === 'bn'
              ? activeResult.remediesBn
              : activeResult.remediesEn
            ).map((rem, idx) => (
              <View key={idx} style={styles.bulletRow}>
                <Text style={{ color: theme.primary, fontWeight: '700' }}>
                  ✓
                </Text>
                <Text
                  style={[
                    styles.bodyText,
                    { color: theme.textPrimary, flex: 1, fontFamily: fontFam },
                  ]}
                >
                  {rem}
                </Text>
              </View>
            ))}
          </View>

          {/* Prevention Recommendations */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.infoBlock,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.cardHeading,
                { color: theme.textPrimary, fontSize: 14, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              🛡️{' '}
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'ভবিষ্যৎ প্রতিরোধমূলক পরামর্শ (Prevention)'
                : 'Prevention Recommendations'}
            </Text>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.bodyText,
                { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'গাছের গোড়ায় পানি জমতে দেবেন না, পর্যাপ্ত রোদ ও বাতাস চলাচল নিশ্চিত করুন এবং প্রতি ১০-১৫ দিন অন্তর জৈব নিম তেল স্প্রে করুন।'
                : 'Avoid waterlogging at the root zone, ensure proper canopy airflow and sunlight, and apply preventive organic neem spray every 10–14 days.'}
            </Text>
          </View>

          {/* Non-Guaranteed AI Botanical Disclaimer (Mandatory per Section 7) */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.disclaimerBox,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                borderColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text style={{ fontSize: 14 }}>ℹ️</Text>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.captionText,
                { color: theme.textSecondary, flex: 1, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              {lang === 'bn'
                ? 'সতর্কবার্তা: এআই পূর্বাভাস ও ফলাফল শতভাগ নিশ্চিত ডায়াগনোসিস নয়; এটি প্রাথমিক বাগান পরিচর্যার সহায়ক পরামর্শ। জটিল সমস্যায় নিকটস্থ কৃষি অফিসারের পরামর্শ নিন।'
                : 'Note: AI predictions are advisory estimates based on visual patterns and are not guaranteed diagnoses. Consult a certified agriculturist for severe infections.'}
            </Text>
          </View>

          {/* Save Result to Plant History */}
          {plants.length > 0 && (
            <View style={styles.saveSection}>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.captionText,
                  {
                    color: theme.textSecondary,
                    fontWeight: '600',
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'এই ফলাফলটি কোন গাছের ইতিহাসে সংরক্ষণ করবেন?'
                  : 'Select plant to save this diagnosis report:'}
              </Text>
              <ScrollView
                horizontal
                showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
                contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 8, paddingVertical: 6 }}
              >
                {plants.map((p) => {
                  const active = p.id === targetPlantId;
                  return (
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      key={p.id}
                      onPress={() => setTargetPlantId(p.id)}
                      style={[
                        styles.plantChip,
                        {
                          backgroundColor: active
                            ? theme.selectedSurface
                            : theme.surfaceElevated,
                          borderColor: active ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                        },
                      ]}
                    >
                      <Text
                        style={{
                          color: active ? theme.primary : theme.textPrimary,
                          fontSize: 13,
                          fontWeight: '600',
                          fontFamily: fontFam,
                        }}
                      >
                        🌱{' '}
                        {lang === 'bn' ? p.nameBengali : p.nameEnglish}
                      </Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  );
                })}
              </ScrollView>

              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => {
                  if (onSaveScanToPlant && targetPlantId) {
                    onSaveScanToPlant(
                      targetPlantId,
                      activeResult.titleBn,
                      activeResult.titleEn,
                      activeResult.remediesBn[0],
                      activeResult.remediesEn[0],
                      activeResult.healthState
                    );
                    Alert.alert(
                      lang === 'bn'
                        ? 'টাইমলাইনে সংরক্ষিত'
                        : 'Saved to Plant History',
                      lang === 'bn'
                        ? 'ডায়াগনোসিস ফলাফলটি গাছের পরিচর্যা ইতিহাসে সংরক্ষিত হয়েছে।'
                        : 'Diagnosis result has been logged to the plant history timeline.'
                    );
                  }
                }}
                style={[styles.saveBtn, { backgroundColor: theme.primary }]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={[
                    styles.buttonText,
                    { color: '#FFFFFF', fontFamily: fontFam },
                  ]}
                >
                  💾{' '}
                  {lang === 'bn'
                    ? 'গাছের ইতিহাসে সংরক্ষণ করুন'
                    : 'Save to Plant History'}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          )}
        </View>
      </View>
    </ScrollView>
  );
};

/**
 * BAGANI — Professional User Profile & App Settings Screen (Refined Architecture)
 * Fully compliant with User Corrections:
 * - Language switcher & Text Size Control Bar with [-] sign, slider track, and [+] sign
 * - Global bounded scaling (88% to 118%) so layout integrity is permanently preserved
 * - Redesigned professional app-style grouped settings (replacing robotic block buttons)
 * - Identical user profile card mirroring the homepage user header
 */

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Alert,
  Image,
  Platform,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { APP_CONFIG } from '../../core/constants/app_constants';
import { AppLanguage } from '../../core/l10n/translations';
import {
  APP_TYPOGRAPHY,
  AppThemeColors,
  DESIGN_TOKENS,
  FontScalePreference,
  ThemeModePreference,
} from '../../core/theme/app_theme';
import { UserModel } from '../../models/user_model';
import {
  AdConfiguration,
  BannerPlacementPosition,
} from '../../services/ad_service';
import {
  CloudSyncMetadata,
  CloudSyncService,
} from '../../services/cloud_sync_service';
import { LegalModal } from '../auth/legal_modal';

interface SettingsScreenProps {
  theme: AppThemeColors;
  themePref: ThemeModePreference;
  onChangeThemePref: (pref: ThemeModePreference) => void;
  lang: AppLanguage;
  onChangeLang: (lang: AppLanguage) => void;
  fontScale: FontScalePreference;
  onChangeFontScale: (scale: FontScalePreference) => void;
  fontScalePercent?: number;
  onChangeFontScalePercent?: (percent: number) => void;
  adConfig?: AdConfiguration;
  onChangeAdPosition?: (pos: BannerPlacementPosition) => void;
  onToggleAdsEnabled?: () => void;
  user: UserModel;
  onEditProfile: () => void;
  onSignOut: () => void;
  onDeleteAccount?: () => void;
  onOpenOnboarding?: () => void;
  onOpenNotifications?: () => void;
}

export const SettingsScreen: React.FC<SettingsScreenProps> = ({
  theme,
  themePref,
  onChangeThemePref,
  lang,
  onChangeLang,
  fontScalePercent = 100,
  onChangeFontScalePercent,
  user,
  onEditProfile,
  onSignOut,
  onDeleteAccount,
  onOpenOnboarding,
  onOpenNotifications,
}) => {
  const [legalVisible, setLegalVisible] = useState(false);
  const [syncMeta, setSyncMeta] = useState<CloudSyncMetadata | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    CloudSyncService.getSyncMetadata(user.email).then(setSyncMeta);
  }, [user.email]);

  const handleManualSync = async () => {
    const updated = await CloudSyncService.syncNow(user.email);
    setSyncMeta(updated);
    Alert.alert(
      lang === 'bn' ? 'ক্লাউড ব্যাকআপ সম্পন্ন' : 'Cloud Sync Complete',
      lang === 'bn'
        ? `${updated.totalPlantsSynced}টি গাছ এবং ${updated.totalTimelineLogsSynced}টি লগ ক্লাউডে সংরক্ষিত হয়েছে।`
        : `${updated.totalPlantsSynced} plants and ${updated.totalTimelineLogsSynced} logs synced to ${updated.accountEmail}.`
    );
  };

  const fontFam =
    lang === 'bn'
      ? APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyBengali
      : APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyEnglish;

  const isDarkActive = theme.mode === 'dark' || themePref === 'dark';

  return (
    <ScrollView
      style={{ flex: 1, width: '100%' }}
      showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
      contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollOuter}
    >
      <View style={styles.container}>
        {/* 1. Verified User Profile Header Card (Exact match with Homepage) */}
        <View
          style={[
            styles.card,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={styles.profileTopRow}>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.avatarBox,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                  borderColor: theme.primary,
                },
              ]}
            >
              {user.photoUrl ? (
                <Image
                  source={{ uri: user.photoUrl }}
                  style={styles.avatarImage}
                />
              ) : (
                <Image
                  source={require('../../../assets/images/logo.png')}
                  style={{ width: 54, height: 44 }}
                  resizeMode="contain"
                />
              )}
            </View>

            <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 180, gap: 3 }}>
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                <Text
                  style={[
                    styles.profileName,
                    { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                  ]}
                >
                  {user.displayName}
                </Text>
                <View style={[styles.verifiedChip, { backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface, borderColor: theme.primary }]}>
                  <Text style={{ color: theme.primary, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '700' }}>
                    ✓ {lang === 'bn' ? 'ভেরিফাইড বাগানি' : 'Verified'}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </View>

              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textSecondary,
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                📧 {user.email}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.primary,
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: '600',
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                📍 {user.district}
                {user.address ? ` • ${user.address}` : ''}
                {user.organizationOrProfession
                  ? ` • ${user.organizationOrProfession}`
                  : ` • ${APP_CONFIG.developerName}`}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={onEditProfile}
              activeOpacity={0.82}
              style={[
                styles.editProfileBtn,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.primary,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: '#FFFFFF',
                  fontWeight: '700',
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                ✏️ {lang === 'bn' ? 'এডিট' : 'Edit'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          {/* Gardener Taste Focus Tags */}
          <View style={styles.tasteBadgeRow}>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.tastePill,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  fontSize: 12,
                  fontWeight: '600',
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                🌱{' '}
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? `পছন্দ: ${
                      user.tasteProfile?.cultivationInterest === 'fruits'
                        ? 'ফল গাছ'
                        : user.tasteProfile?.cultivationInterest === 'vegetables'
                        ? 'সবজি বাগান'
                        : 'ফল ও সবজি উভয়ই'
                    }`
                  : `Focus: ${
                      user.tasteProfile?.cultivationInterest === 'fruits'
                        ? 'Fruits'
                        : user.tasteProfile?.cultivationInterest === 'vegetables'
                        ? 'Vegetables'
                        : 'Fruits & Vegetables'
                    }`}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View
              style={[
                styles.tastePill,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  fontSize: 12,
                  fontWeight: '600',
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                🍒{' '}
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? `স্বাদ: ${
                      user.tasteProfile?.tastePreference === 'sweet'
                        ? 'মিষ্টি ফল'
                        : user.tasteProfile?.tastePreference === 'sour_tangy'
                        ? 'টক-মিষ্টি'
                        : 'সব ধরনের'
                    }`
                  : `Taste: ${
                      user.tasteProfile?.tastePreference === 'sweet'
                        ? 'Sweet'
                        : user.tasteProfile?.tastePreference === 'sour_tangy'
                        ? 'Tangy'
                        : 'All'
                    }`}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* 2. Language Selection */}
        <View
          style={[
            styles.card,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={styles.sectionHeaderRow}>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.cardTitle,
                { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
              ]}
            >
              🌐 {lang === 'bn' ? 'ভাষা নির্বাচন' : 'Language'}
            </Text>

            {/* Clean Segmented Language Dock */}
            <View
              style={[
                styles.segmentedDock,
                { backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated, borderColor: theme.border },
              ]}
            >
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => onChangeLang('bn')}
                activeOpacity={0.82}
                style={[
                  styles.segmentItem,
                  lang === 'bn'
                    ? { backgroundColor: theme.primary }
                    : { backgroundColor: 'transparent' },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: lang === 'bn' ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textSecondary,
                    fontWeight: '700',
                    fontSize: 12.5,
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  }}
                >
                  🇧🇩 বাংলা
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => onChangeLang('en')}
                activeOpacity={0.82}
                style={[
                  styles.segmentItem,
                  lang === 'en'
                    ? { backgroundColor: theme.primary }
                    : { backgroundColor: 'transparent' },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: lang === 'en' ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textSecondary,
                    fontWeight: '700',
                    fontSize: 12.5,
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  }}
                >
                  🇬🇧 English
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* 3. Appearance & Theme Selection (Clean 1-Tap Toggle) */}
        <View
          style={[
            styles.card,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={styles.sectionHeaderRow}>
            <View>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.cardTitle,
                  { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                🎨 {lang === 'bn' ? 'থিম মোড' : 'Theme Mode'}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textSecondary,
                  fontSize: 12.5,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'লাইট ও ডার্ক মোডের মধ্যে সুইচ করুন' : 'Toggle Light or Dark appearance'}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View
              style={[
                styles.segmentedDock,
                { backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated, borderColor: theme.border },
              ]}
            >
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => onChangeThemePref('light')}
                activeOpacity={0.82}
                style={[
                  styles.segmentItem,
                  !isDarkActive
                    ? { backgroundColor: theme.primary }
                    : { backgroundColor: 'transparent' },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: !isDarkActive ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textSecondary,
                    fontWeight: '700',
                    fontSize: 12.5,
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  }}
                >
                  ☀️ {lang === 'bn' ? 'লাইট' : 'Light'}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => onChangeThemePref('dark')}
                activeOpacity={0.82}
                style={[
                  styles.segmentItem,
                  isDarkActive
                    ? { backgroundColor: theme.primary }
                    : { backgroundColor: 'transparent' },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: isDarkActive ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textSecondary,
                    fontWeight: '700',
                    fontSize: 12.5,
                    fontFamily: fontFam,
                  }}
                >
                  🌙 {lang === 'bn' ? 'ডার্ক' : 'Dark'}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* 4. Professional Grouped Settings & App Actions (Native App Aesthetic) */}
        <View
          style={[
            styles.card,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <Text
            style={[
              styles.cardTitle,
              { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
            ]}
          >
            ⚙️ {lang === 'bn' ? 'অ্যাপ সেটিংস ও সুবিধাসমূহ' : 'App Settings & Tools'}
          </Text>

          {syncMeta && (
            <View
              style={[
                styles.syncBox,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                  borderColor: theme.primary,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.primary,
                  fontSize: 12.5,
                  fontWeight: '700',
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                ☁️{' '}
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? `ক্লাউড ব্যাকআপ সক্রিয়: ${syncMeta.totalPlantsSynced}টি গাছ • ${syncMeta.totalTimelineLogsSynced}টি লগ সংরক্ষিত`
                  : `Cloud Backup Synced: ${syncMeta.totalPlantsSynced} Plants • ${syncMeta.totalTimelineLogsSynced} Timeline Logs`}
              </Text>
            </View>
          )}

          <View style={[styles.groupedListCard, { backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated, borderColor: theme.border }]}>
            {/* Row 1: Cloud Sync */}
            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={handleManualSync}
              activeOpacity={0.7}
              style={[styles.settingsRow, { borderBottomColor: theme.border }]}
            >
              <View style={[styles.iconChip, { backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface }]}>
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 16 }}>☁️</Text>
              </View>
              <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                <Text style={[styles.rowTitle, { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam }]}>
                  {lang === 'bn' ? 'গুগল ক্লাউড ব্যাকআপ' : 'Google Cloud Backup'}
                </Text>
                <Text style={[styles.rowSub, { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam }]}>
                  {lang === 'bn' ? 'সমস্ত গাছ ও টাইমলাইন ডেটা ক্লাউডে সুরক্ষিত করুন' : 'Safeguard all plant logs and photos'}
                </Text>
              </View>
              <Text style={{ color: theme.primary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: '700' }}>›</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            {/* Row 2: Privacy & Terms */}
            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => setLegalVisible(true)}
              activeOpacity={0.7}
              style={styles.settingsRow}
            >
              <View style={[styles.iconChip, { backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface }]}>
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 16 }}>📄</Text>
              </View>
              <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                <Text style={[styles.rowTitle, { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam }]}>
                  {lang === 'bn' ? 'প্রাইভেসি পলিসি ও ব্যবহারের শর্তাবলী' : 'Privacy Policy & Terms'}
                </Text>
                <Text style={[styles.rowSub, { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam }]}>
                  {lang === 'bn' ? 'ডেটা নিরাপত্তা ও ব্যবহারের নীতিমালা' : 'Data protection & legal compliance'}
                </Text>
              </View>
              <Text style={{ color: theme.primary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: '700' }}>›</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* 5. Account Security Actions (Clean Professional Rows) */}
        <View
          style={[
            styles.card,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <Text
            style={[
              styles.cardTitle,
              { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
            ]}
          >
            🔒 {lang === 'bn' ? 'অ্যাকাউন্ট ও নিরাপত্তা' : 'Account & Security'}
          </Text>

          <View style={[styles.groupedListCard, { backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated, borderColor: theme.border }]}>
            {/* Sign Out */}
            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={onSignOut}
              activeOpacity={0.7}
              style={styles.settingsRow}
            >
              <View style={[styles.iconChip, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(217, 119, 6, 0.12)' }]}>
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 16 }}>🚪</Text>
              </View>
              <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                <Text style={[styles.rowTitle, { color: theme.warning, fontFamily: fontFam }]}>
                  {lang === 'bn' ? 'সাইন আউট (Sign Out)' : 'Sign Out'}
                </Text>
                <Text style={[styles.rowSub, { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam }]}>
                  {lang === 'bn' ? 'অ্যাকাউন্ট থেকে নিরাপদে লগআউট করুন' : 'Safely log out of this device'}
                </Text>
              </View>
              <Text style={{ color: theme.warning, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: '700' }}>›</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>

        <LegalModal
          visible={legalVisible}
          onClose={() => setLegalVisible(false)}
          theme={theme}
          lang={lang}
        />
      </View>
    </ScrollView>
  );
};

/**
 * BAGANI — Weather Dashboard & Automated Plant Care Recommendations (Screens 11 & 12 of DESIGN_SYSTEM.md)
 * Displays:
 * - Current temperature, Humidity, Rain probability, Solar intensity
 * - 15-Day Weather Forecast (7 Days Past + Today + 7 Days Upcoming)
 * - Weather-related care alerts & actionable instructions
 * - Plant-specific watering & organic fertilizer recommendations with completion checkboxes
 */

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { AppLanguage } from '../../core/l10n/translations';
import {
  APP_TYPOGRAPHY,
  AppThemeColors,
  DESIGN_TOKENS,
} from '../../core/theme/app_theme';
import { toBengaliNumber } from '../../core/utils/date_utils';
import {
  DailyWeatherSnapshot,
  GranularCareTask,
} from '../../models/care_schedule_model';
import { PlantModel } from '../../models/plant_model';
import { LocalDbService } from '../../services/local_db_service';
import {
  DistrictCropRecommendation,
  OnlineBotanicalService,
} from '../../services/online_botanical_service';
import { WeatherService } from '../../services/weather_service';

interface WeatherCareScreenProps {
  theme: AppThemeColors;
  lang: AppLanguage;
  userDistrict: string;
  plants: PlantModel[];
  careTasks: GranularCareTask[];
  onToggleTaskCheckbox: (task: GranularCareTask) => void;
  onSelectPlant?: (plant: PlantModel) => void;
}

export const WeatherCareScreen: React.FC<WeatherCareScreenProps> = ({
  theme,
  lang,
  userDistrict,
  plants,
  careTasks,
  onToggleTaskCheckbox,
  onSelectPlant,
}) => {
  const [weatherWindow, setWeatherWindow] = useState<{
    past7Days: DailyWeatherSnapshot[];
    today: DailyWeatherSnapshot;
    next7Days: DailyWeatherSnapshot[];
    isLiveOnline?: boolean;
  }>(() => LocalDbService.get15DayWeatherWindow());

  const [filterMode, setFilterMode] = useState<'all' | 'pending' | 'done'>(
    'all'
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true;
    WeatherService.fetchLive15DayWeather(userDistrict).then((liveData) => {
      if (mounted) {
        setWeatherWindow(liveData);
      }
    });
    return () => {
      mounted = false;
    };
  }, [userDistrict]);

  const fontFam =
    lang === 'bn'
      ? APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyBengali
      : APP_TYPOGRAPHY.fontFamilyEnglish;

  const filteredTasks = careTasks.filter((t) => {
    if (filterMode === 'pending') return !t.isCompleted;
    if (filterMode === 'done') return t.isCompleted;
    return true;
  });

  const rainChance = weatherWindow.today.rainProbabilityPercent;
  const temp = weatherWindow.today.tempCelsius;
  const humidity = weatherWindow.today.humidityPercent;

  const cropRecommendations = useMemo(() => {
    return OnlineBotanicalService.getLocationAndWeatherCropRecommendations(
      userDistrict,
      temp,
      humidity,
      rainChance
    );
  }, [userDistrict, temp, humidity, rainChance]);

  return (
    <ScrollView
      style={{ flex: 1, width: '100%' }}
      showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
      contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollOuter}
    >
      <View style={styles.container}>
        {/* 1. Primary Weather Dashboard Card (Screen 11) */}
        <View
          style={[
            styles.card,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={styles.headerRow}>
            <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 200 }}>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.sectionTitle,
                  { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                🌦️{' '}
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? `লাইভ আবহাওয়া ড্যাশবোর্ড (${userDistrict})`
                  : `Live Weather Dashboard (${userDistrict})`}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.subText,
                  { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'আপনার এলাকার লাইভ তাপমাত্রা, আর্দ্রতা, বৃষ্টির সম্ভাবনা ও স্বয়ংক্রিয় গাছ পরিচর্যা নির্দেশনা'
                  : 'Real-time local temperature, humidity, rain probability & automated plant care'}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View
              style={[
                styles.liveBadge,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                  borderColor: theme.primary,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.primary,
                  fontSize: 12,
                  fontWeight: '700',
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                {weatherWindow.isLiveOnline
                  ? lang === 'bn'
                    ? '🟢 লাইভ আবহাওয়া'
                    : '🟢 Live Sync'
                  : lang === 'bn'
                  ? '⚡ অফলাইন প্রিসেট'
                  : '⚡ Offline Mode'}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* 3 Primary Weather Metrics: Temperature, Humidity, Rain Probability */}
          <View style={styles.metricsGrid}>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.metricBox,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={styles.metricEmoji}>🌡️</Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.metricVal,
                  { color: theme.primary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? toBengaliNumber(temp) : temp}°C
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.metricLabel,
                  { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'বর্তমান তাপমাত্রা' : 'Temperature'}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View
              style={[
                styles.metricBox,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={styles.metricEmoji}>💧</Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.metricVal,
                  { color: theme.info, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? toBengaliNumber(humidity) : humidity}%
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.metricLabel,
                  { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'বাতাসের আর্দ্রতা' : 'Humidity'}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View
              style={[
                styles.metricBox,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                  borderColor: theme.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={styles.metricEmoji}>☔</Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.metricVal,
                  { color: theme.secondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? toBengaliNumber(rainChance) : rainChance}%
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.metricLabel,
                  { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn' ? 'বৃষ্টির সম্ভাবনা' : 'Rain Probability'}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Weather-Related Care Alert Banner */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.alertBox,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                borderColor: theme.primary,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text style={{ fontSize: 18 }}>⚠️</Text>
            <View style={{ flex: 1, gap: 3 }}>
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.primary,
                  fontWeight: '700',
                  fontSize: 14,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'আবহাওয়া ভিত্তিক বিশেষ সতর্কতা (Weather Care Alert)'
                  : 'Weather-Related Care Alert'}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.textPrimary,
                  fontSize: 13,
                  lineHeight: 20,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                {rainChance >= 50
                  ? lang === 'bn'
                    ? 'আজ বৃষ্টির সম্ভাবনা বেশি। টবের নিচে জমে থাকা অতিরিক্ত পানি নিষ্কাশনের পথ পরিষ্কার রাখুন এবং আজ তরল সার বা কীটনাশক স্প্রে করা থেকে বিরত থাকুন।'
                    : 'High rain probability today. Keep pot drainage holes clear and avoid foliar sprays or liquid fertilizers today.'
                  : temp >= 32
                  ? lang === 'bn'
                    ? 'তাপমাত্রা বেশি থাকায় দুপুরের কড়া রোদে গাছে পানি দেবেন না। সকাল ৬–৮টা অথবা বিকেল ৫টার পর টবের মাটিতে পরিমিত পানি দিন।'
                    : 'High daytime heat. Never water roots under midday sun; water between 6–8 AM or after 5 PM.'
                  : lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'আজকের আবহাওয়া ছাদবাগানের পরিচর্যা, আগাছা পরিষ্কার এবং জৈব সার প্রয়োগের জন্য অত্যন্ত অনুকূল।'
                  : 'Ideal weather conditions today for pruning, soil aeration, and applying organic compost.'}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* 2. 15-Day Weather Forecast Strip (Past 7 Days + Today + Next 7 Days) */}
        <View
          style={[
            styles.card,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <Text
            style={[
              styles.cardHeading,
              { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
            ]}
          >
            📅{' '}
            {lang === 'bn'
              ? '১৫ দিনের আবহাওয়া পরিক্রমা (বিগত ৭ দিন • আজ • আগামী ৭ দিন)'
              : '15-Day Weather Forecast (Past 7 Days • Today • Next 7 Days)'}
          </Text>

          <ScrollView
            horizontal
            showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
            contentContainerStyle={styles.forecastStrip}
          >
            {[
              ...weatherWindow.past7Days,
              weatherWindow.today,
              ...weatherWindow.next7Days,
            ].map((day, idx) => {
              const isToday = idx === 7;
              return (
                <View
                  key={idx}
                  style={[
                    styles.dayChip,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: isToday
                        ? theme.primary
                        : theme.surfaceElevated,
                      borderColor: isToday ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: isToday ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textSecondary,
                      fontSize: 11,
                      fontWeight: '700',
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    {lang === 'bn' ? day.dayLabelBengali : day.dayLabelEnglish}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={{ fontSize: 18 }}>
                    {day.condition === 'rainy'
                      ? '🌧️'
                      : day.condition === 'hot_dry'
                      ? '☀️'
                      : '⛅'}
                  </Text>
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: isToday ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                      fontSize: 14,
                      fontWeight: '800',
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    {lang === 'bn'
                      ? toBengaliNumber(day.tempCelsius)
                      : day.tempCelsius}
                    °C
                  </Text>
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: isToday ? '#E7F4EB' : theme.info,
                      fontSize: 11,
                      fontWeight: '600',
                    }}
                  >
                    💧{day.rainProbabilityPercent}%
                  </Text>
                </View>
              );
            })}
          </ScrollView>
        </View>

        {/* 3. Automated Plant Care Recommendations (Screen 12 of DESIGN_SYSTEM.md) */}
        <View
          style={[
            styles.card,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={styles.headerRow}>
            <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 200 }}>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.cardHeading,
                  { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                📋{' '}
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'স্বয়ংক্রিয় গাছ পরিচর্যা ও পানির রুটিন'
                  : 'Automated Plant-Specific Care Recommendations'}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.subText,
                  { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? 'চেকবক্সে টিক (✓) দিলে তা স্বয়ংক্রিয়ভাবে গাছের টাইমলাইনে সেভ হবে'
                  : 'Check off completed care tasks to log them directly into the plant timeline'}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.filterPillsRow}>
              {(
                [
                  { id: 'all', bn: 'সব', en: 'All' },
                  { id: 'pending', bn: 'বাকি', en: 'Pending' },
                  { id: 'done', bn: 'সম্পন্ন', en: 'Done' },
                ] as const
              ).map((f) => {
                const active = filterMode === f.id;
                return (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={f.id}
                    onPress={() => setFilterMode(f.id)}
                    style={[
                      styles.filterPill,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: active
                          ? theme.primary
                          : theme.surfaceElevated,
                        borderColor: active ? theme.primary : theme.border,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={{
                        color: active ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.textPrimary,
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: '700',
                        fontFamily: fontFam,
                      }}
                    >
                      {lang === 'bn' ? f.bn : f.en}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                );
              })}
            </View>
          </View>

          {filteredTasks.map((task) => {
            const matchedPlant = plants.find((p) => p.id === task.plantId);
            return (
              <View
                key={task.id}
                style={[
                  styles.taskCard,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: task.isCompleted
                      ? theme.selectedSurface
                      : theme.surfaceElevated,
                    borderColor: task.isCompleted
                      ? theme.primary
                      : theme.border,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() => onToggleTaskCheckbox(task)}
                  style={styles.taskMainRow}
                  activeOpacity={0.8}
                >
                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.checkbox,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: task.isCompleted
                          ? theme.primary
                          : 'transparent',
                        borderColor: task.isCompleted
                          ? theme.primary
                          : theme.textSecondary,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    {task.isCompleted && (
                      <Text
                        style={{
                          color: '#FFFFFF',
                          fontSize: 12,
                          fontWeight: '800',
                        }}
                      >
                        ✓
                      </Text>
                    )}
                  </View>

                  <View style={{ flex: 1, gap: 4 }}>
                    <View style={styles.taskTitleRow}>
                      <Text
                        style={{
                          color: theme.textPrimary,
                          fontWeight: '700',
                          fontSize: 15,
                          fontFamily: fontFam,
                          textDecorationLine: task.isCompleted
                            ? 'line-through'
                            : 'none',
                        }}
                      >
                        {lang === 'bn'
                          ? task.plantNameBengali
                          : task.plantNameEnglish}
                      </Text>
                      <View
                        style={[
                          styles.waterPill,
                          {
                            backgroundColor: theme.surface,
                            borderColor: theme.border,
                          },
                        ]}
                      >
                        <Text
                          style={{
                            color: theme.primary,
                            fontSize: 12,
                            fontWeight: '700',
                            fontFamily: fontFam,
                          }}
                        >
                          💧 {task.waterAmountLiters}L •{' '}
                          {task.recommendedTimeSlot === 'morning'
                            ? lang === 'bn'
                              ? 'সকাল ৬–৮টা'
                              : 'Morning 6–8 AM'
                            : lang === 'bn'
                            ? 'বিকেল ৫–৬টা'
                            : 'Afternoon 5–6 PM'}
                        </Text>
                      </View>
                    </View>

                    <Text
                      style={{
                        color: theme.textSecondary,
                        fontSize: 13,
                        lineHeight: 20,
                        fontFamily: fontFam,
                      }}
                    >
                      🌱{' '}
                      {lang === 'bn'
                        ? task.fertilizerOrSprayInstructionBengali
                        : task.fertilizerOrSprayInstructionEnglish}
                    </Text>

                    {task.shadeOrMoistureAdvisoryBengali && (
                      <Text
                        style={{
                          color: theme.warning,
                          fontSize: 12,
                          fontWeight: '600',
                          fontFamily: fontFam,
                        }}
                      >
                        ☂️{' '}
                        {lang === 'bn'
                          ? task.shadeOrMoistureAdvisoryBengali
                          : task.shadeOrMoistureAdvisoryEnglish}
                      </Text>
                    )}
                  </View>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                {matchedPlant && onSelectPlant && (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    onPress={() => onSelectPlant(matchedPlant)}
                    style={[
                      styles.openPlantBtn,
                      { borderTopColor: theme.border },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={{
                        color: theme.primary,
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: '700',
                        fontFamily: fontFam,
                      }}
                    >
                      {lang === 'bn'
                        ? '🔮 গাছটির প্রোফাইল ও টাইমলাইন দেখুন →'
                        : '🔮 View Plant Profile & Timeline →'}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                )}
              </View>
            );
          })}
        </View>

        {/* 4. Real-Time Location & Weather-Based Crop Recommendations (Requirement #4) */}
        <View
          style={[
            styles.card,
            { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={styles.headerRow}>
            <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 200 }}>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.cardHeading,
                  { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                🌾{' '}
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? `${userDistrict}-এর আবহাওয়া ও ঋতু ভিত্তিক উপযোগী ফল ও সবজি`
                  : `Location-Adapted Crops for ${userDistrict}`}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.subText,
                  { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                ]}
              >
                {lang === 'bn'
                  ? `আপনার জেলার বর্তমান তাপমাত্রা (${temp}°C) ও আর্দ্রতা (${humidity}%) অনুযায়ী ছাদবাগানের সেরা ফসল ও বিশেষ পরিচর্যা গাইড`
                  : `Horticultural recommendations matched to local thermal units (${temp}°C) & humidity (${humidity}%)`}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View
              style={[
                styles.liveBadge,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                  borderColor: theme.primary,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: theme.primary,
                  fontWeight: '700',
                  fontSize: 12,
                  fontFamily: fontFam,
                }}
              >
                🎯 {lang === 'bn' ? 'এলাকা অনুযায়ী যাচাইকৃত' : 'Geo-Optimized'}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          <View style={{ gap: 12, marginTop: 4 }}>
            {cropRecommendations.map((crop) => (
              <View
                key={crop.id}
                style={[
                  styles.cropRecCard,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.surfaceElevated,
                    borderColor: theme.border,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <View style={styles.cropRecHeader}>
                  <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.cropTitle,
                        { color: theme.textPrimary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {crop.category === 'fruit' ? '🍎 ' : '🥬 '}
                      {lang === 'bn' ? crop.cropNameBn : crop.cropNameEn}
                    </Text>
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.cropVariety,
                        { color: theme.textSecondary, fontFamily: fontFam },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {lang === 'bn' ? 'সেরা জাত: ' : 'Best Variety: '}
                      <Text style={{ color: theme.primary, fontWeight: '700' }}>
                        {lang === 'bn' ? crop.bestVarietyBn : crop.bestVarietyEn}
                      </Text>
                    </Text>
                  </View>

                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.suitBadge,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                        borderColor: theme.primary,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={{
                        color: theme.primary,
                        fontSize: 11.5,
                        fontWeight: '700',
                        fontFamily: fontFam,
                      }}
                    >
                      ★ {lang === 'bn' ? crop.suitabilityBadgeBn : crop.suitabilityBadgeEn}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>

                {/* Why Suitable */}
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.cropDetailBox,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: theme.surface,
                      borderColor: theme.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: theme.primary,
                      fontWeight: '700',
                      fontSize: 12,
                      marginBottom: 2,
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    🌤️ {lang === 'bn' ? 'কেন আপনার জেলার জন্য উপযুক্ত:' : 'Why Suitable for this District:'}
                  </Text>
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: theme.textSecondary,
                      fontSize: 12.5,
                      lineHeight: 18,
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    {lang === 'bn'
                      ? crop.whySuitableForDistrictBn
                      : crop.whySuitableForDistrictEn}
                  </Text>
                </View>

                {/* Soil & Planting Guide */}
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.cropDetailBox,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: theme.surface,
                      borderColor: theme.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: theme.textPrimary,
                      fontWeight: '700',
                      fontSize: 12,
                      marginBottom: 2,
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    🪴 {lang === 'bn' ? 'টব ও মাটি প্রস্তুতি:' : 'Container & Soil Guide:'}
                  </Text>
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: theme.textSecondary,
                      fontSize: 12.5,
                      lineHeight: 18,
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    {lang === 'bn'
                      ? crop.plantingAndSoilGuideBn
                      : crop.plantingAndSoilGuideEn}
                  </Text>
                </View>

                {/* Pruning & Yield Tip */}
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.cropDetailBox,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: theme.selectedSurface,
                      borderColor: theme.primary,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: theme.primary,
                      fontWeight: '700',
                      fontSize: 12,
                      marginBottom: 2,
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    ✂️ {lang === 'bn' ? 'প্রুনিং ও ফলন বাড়ানোর সিক্রেট:' : 'Pruning & Yield Secret:'}
                  </Text>
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      color: theme.textPrimary,
                      fontSize: 12.5,
                      lineHeight: 18,
                      fontFamily: fontFam,
                    }}
                  >
                    {lang === 'bn'
                      ? crop.careAndPruningGuideBn
                      : crop.careAndPruningGuideEn}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </View>
            ))}
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>
    </ScrollView>
  );
};

/**
 * BAGANI — Monetization & Banner Ad Service (Phase 2)
 * Developer: ProFix - Web & Soft
 * Features:
 * - Configurable Top & Bottom Banner Ad placement options
 * - Google Play Ads Policy & Apple App Tracking Transparency (ATT) consent state
 * - SafeArea separation to prevent accidental clicks
 */

import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { Platform } from 'react-native';

export type BannerPlacementPosition = 'bottom' | 'top' | 'both';

export interface AdConfiguration {
  adsEnabled: boolean;
  placementPosition: BannerPlacementPosition;
  personalizedAdsConsent: boolean;
  attAuthorizedOnIos: boolean;
  androidBannerUnitId: string;
  iosBannerUnitId: string;
}

const AD_CONFIG_STORAGE_KEY = '@bagani_ad_config_v1';

export const DEFAULT_AD_CONFIG: AdConfiguration = {
  adsEnabled: true,
  placementPosition: 'bottom',
  personalizedAdsConsent: true,
  attAuthorizedOnIos: true,
  // Official Google AdMob standard test/placeholder IDs ready for production swap by ProFix - Web & Soft
  androidBannerUnitId: 'ca-app-pub-3940256099942544/6300978111',
  iosBannerUnitId: 'ca-app-pub-3940256099942544/2934735716',
};

export interface gardeningSponsorMessage {
  id: string;
  headlineBn: string;
  headlineEn: string;
  subtextBn: string;
  subtextEn: string;
  badgeText: string;
}

export const ROTATING_BANNER_ADS: gardeningSponsorMessage[] = [
  {
    id: 'ad_profix_1',
    headlineBn: 'ProFix - Web & Soft • স্মার্ট গার্ডেনিং সলিউশন',
    headlineEn: 'ProFix - Web & Soft • Smart Gardening Solutions',
    subtextBn: 'উন্নত মানের জিও ব্যাগ, ভার্মিকম্পোস্ট ও কলমের চারা সংগ্রহ করুন',
    subtextEn: 'Get premium Geo Bags, Vermicompost & Grafted Saplings',
    badgeText: 'AD',
  },
  {
    id: 'ad_profix_2',
    headlineBn: 'ছাদ বাগানের ওয়াটারপ্রুফিং ও জৈব সার গাইড',
    headlineEn: 'Rooftop Waterproofing & Organic Fertilizer Guide',
    subtextBn: 'BAGANI অ্যাপের সাথে আপনার ছাদ হোক ১০০% সুরক্ষিত ও ফলবতী',
    subtextEn: 'Keep your rooftop 100% safe and fruitful with BAGANI',
    badgeText: 'AD',
  },
];

export class AdService {
  static async getAdConfig(): Promise<AdConfiguration> {
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(AD_CONFIG_STORAGE_KEY);
      if (!raw) return DEFAULT_AD_CONFIG;
      return { ...DEFAULT_AD_CONFIG, ...(JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<AdConfiguration>) };
    } catch {
      return DEFAULT_AD_CONFIG;
    }
  }

  static async updateAdConfig(
    updates: Partial<AdConfiguration>
  ): Promise<AdConfiguration> {
    const current = await this.getAdConfig();
    const next: AdConfiguration = { ...current, ...updates };
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(AD_CONFIG_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(next));
    return next;
  }

  static getActiveUnitId(config: AdConfiguration): string {
    return Platform.OS === 'ios'
      ? config.iosBannerUnitId
      : config.androidBannerUnitId;
  }
}



// ##############################################################################
// FILE END: lib/services/ad_service.ts
// ##############################################################################
